        {tags={"Carrier",},text={"*Fixed an issue where ammo pickups mutated by Hosts would confuse Clients-side Carriers as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/480877-carrier-looting-bug-ammo-clicking/ forum post%]"}},

        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue causing mind-controlled Heavy Gunners to use their ground slam when allies were nearby."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed players receiving a ‘power in use’ message when casting Excalibur’s Exalted Blade while switching to a melee weapon."}},

        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed the Cephalon in Cephalon Capture turning invisible when players perform quick melee attacks or melee combos."}},
},--fixes end

        {tags={"Excavation","Survival",},text={"*The Excavation spawn logic has been replaced with the logic Survival has been using since February; rather than a slow stream of enemies they will now come in slightly more coordinated waves with gaps between them for pacing."}},

        {tags={"Excavation","Reach",},text={"*Improved the spawn placement strategy so that enemies don%'t have to travel as far to reach the action. Similarly improved the arrangement of enemies spawned before excavation starts so that they are only placed between the players and the first excavator (rather than towards extraction)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Resolved numerous collision issues with the excavators that sometimes shielded attacking melee units from gunfire."}},

        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed a crash that would occur when two players join the same team in Conclave, followed by the Client leaving and rejoining the match."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end

        {tags={},text={"*Made several small optimizations for long-running missions with lots of loot."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed Exalted Blade’s energy waves not receiving a boost from damage Mods when used by Clients."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed Exalted Blade’s energy wave not benefiting from channeling buffs."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed Clients using Exalted Blade not gaining benefits from Syndicate Mods."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Glaive",},text={"*Fixed the Proto Glaive skin causing Exalted Blade to not appear properly in Excalibur’s hand."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.9.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"June 19th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/477906-hotfix-1693/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Excalibur","Parry",},text={"*Reduced Stamina drain on Auto-Parry when using Exalted Blade."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed the some cases of Exalted Blade not appearing when activated when used with certain melee weapons."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed some cases of Exalted Blade use not allowing players to melee attack when activated or deactivated."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Life Strike",},text={"*Fixed Exalted Blade’s energy waves not getting a damage buff from Channeling or applying unique Mod effects (such as Life Strike)."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Glaive",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Proto Glaive skin with Exalted Blade preventing waves from appearing."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Exalted Blade damage not being correct on Clients."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed cloth effects not behaving properly in Archwing Missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some unusual %'bouncing%' behavior on cloth when applying customization in the Arsenal."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{

["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed cloth physics not properly reacting to falling distances."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed Excalibur’s Exalted Blade copying melee Stance Mods, which would break functionality of the Ability."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing Excalibur’s Exalted Blade to not properly release energy waves."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.9.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"June 19th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/477700-hotfix-1692/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Defense","Interception","Ceres","Grineer Shipyard",},text={"*Optimized the rendering in Grineer Shipyards (Ceres) Defense and Interception minimaps."}},
        {tags={"Braton Vandal","Vandal",},text={"*Updated the firing sound on the Braton Vandal shots."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Disabled many-core and dual-core physics optimizations until we can isolate why they cause crashes on certain machines."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some door audio effects playing too loudly."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some instances of duplicate player names showing up in the list of chat users."}},
        {tags={"Silva & Aegis",},text={"*Fixed an issue where the Aegis (from Silva and Aegis) would not collapse when holstered."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Gas City",},text={"*Fixed some instances of the Datamass spawning in walls on the Corpus Gas City tileset."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed Excalibur’s Exalted Blade’s melee attacks always counting as though it were channeled."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed a wall in Conclave’s Underwater Lab tileset that did not have proper collision."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation","Team Annihilation",},text={"*Fixed players being able to start Annihilation and Team Annihilation matches alone."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{

["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Reduced memory footprint slightly for many-core systems."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Optimizes physics task framework for dual-core systems using multi-threaded rendering."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed several crashes caused by rare timing-issues exposed by recent optimizations."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.9.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"June 17th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/476545-hotfix-1691/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed Excalibur%'s Exalted Blade not inheriting any melee mods. This fix now allows the damage of Exalted Blade attacks to scale based on the mods of your Melee weapon."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed game hangs related to Exaclibur%'s Slash Dash."}},
        {tags={"Dera","Clan",},text={"*Purple Text now only applies to global chat Moderators and won%'t appear within Clans/Alliances."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.9"},
["date"]={"June 17th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/476490-update-1690/"},
["title"]={"Excalibur Revisited"},
["image"]={"Update 16.9 Excalibur Revisited.jpg"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Noggles + Character Customization%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"Complement Excalibur%'s updated powers with these new items, available in the Market now:"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Mirage","Prisma",},text={"PRISMA EXCALIBUR BUNDLE"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Mirage","Prisma",},text={"Let Excalibur stand apart with skin and helmets forged from the purest Prisma Crystal."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Mirage","Prisma",},text={"PRISMA EXCALIBUR SKIN"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Mirage","Void","Prisma",},text={"This Excalibur Warframe has been coated with liquid Prisma, enriched by pure Void energy."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Mirage","Prisma",},text={"PRISMA, PRISMA AVALON and PRISMA PENDRAGON EXCALIBUR HELMETS"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Mirage","Void","Prisma",},text={"Three new, unique helmets for Excalibur, coated with liquid Prisma and enriched by pure Void energy."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"EXCALIBUR PROTO-ARMOR SKIN"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"Discovering the precise nature of the Excalibur Proto-Armor Skin has proved elusive. Initial attempts at dating point to ancient origins, perhaps even predating Orokin Empire. Get it from now until June 24 at 2 p.m. EDT -- when it%'s returned to the Vault."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Mirage","Prisma",},text={"PRISMA EXCALIBUR and PROTO-EXCALIBUR NOGGLE STATUES"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Mirage","Liset","Prisma",},text={"Decorate your Liset with new Prisma and Proto-Excalibur Noggle Statues!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"%'%'%'Excalibur Rework%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"No matter your play style, Excalibur is always a formidable Warframe choice. With newly re-worked Abilities, he is now more versatile than ever."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"The following changes have been made to Excalibur’s Abilities. Please note that we will continue to monitor these changes after launch to make any necessary tweaks to balance."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*First Ability: Slash Dash: Slash Dash will now target enemies within a conal{{sic}} distance from Excalibur, targeting all enemies within the range of cast. Enemies hit by Slash Dash will count towards the player’s melee combo counter."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Second Ability: Radial Blind: Cast on the move and opens AI to finishers."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Third Ability: Super Jump has been removed from Excalibur’s Abilities."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Third Ability: Radial Javelin is now Excalibur’s third Ability and costs 75 Energy to activate."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fourth Ability: New Ability: Exalted Blade: Excalibur summons a sword of pure light and immense power."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Prime",},text={"*Stat changes: Excalibur Armor: Excalibur’s base has been increased to 225 and to 250 for Excalibur Prime."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Excalibur’s Abilities have all been given a visual overhaul in line with his new Exalted Blade ability."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Excalibur has been rebalanced for PvP in accordance with his recent overhaul."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Defense",},text={"*Players joining an Defense Mission mid-progress will now only be able to join before the start of the 4th enemy wave- after then the mission is considered %'objective complete%'."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Standardized naming conventions for Warframe Animation Sets."}},
        {tags={"Tigris",},text={"*Improved the visual tint on the Tigris."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Volt’s Overload will now cause destructive visual effects on nearby active light fixtures when used."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players will now see a quantity listed next to the Arcane Enhancements in their inventory."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Interception","Fusion Core",},text={"*Added Rare 5 Fusion Core Bundles and Uncommon Fusion Core Bundles to Hard/ Easy Archwing Interception reward tables, respectively."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Noggle capacity in the Liset has been increased!"}},
        {tags={"Dera",},text={"*Users with Chat Moderation privileges have their chat messages display in Purple Text."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"*Fixed players not being able to leave a Mission if a Client revives partway through the G3 capture process."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being unable to sell Arcanes."}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Sonic Fracture",},text={"*Fixed Banshee’s Sonic Fracture Mod not affecting all enemies when used by Clients as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/465697-sonic-fracture-inconsistency/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being unable to select what Mission they would like to start when there are multiple Missions on a node."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the player HuD being cut off in certain resolutions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the weapon ammo counter appearing when unarmed."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed the timer in the Dojo Obstacle Course never counting up from 0."}},
        {tags={"Carrier",},text={"*Fixed the Para Carrier skin showing code in its description."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Syndicate Medallions becoming invisible during Syndicate Missions"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing Host Migrations in Conclave to not end a round properly for all players."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed extra corpses appearing when players die while invisible."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Prime",},text={"*Fixed a crash that would occur when joining a Mission through your Friends List as Excalibur Prime."}},
        {tags={"Atomos",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing Atomos particle effects to hover in mid-air after being fired as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/463128-floating-atomos-beams/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue that would cause some stats in the Arsenal to display one percent lower than they actually are."}},
        {tags={"Vor","Quest",},text={"*Fixed an issue preventing players from crafting blueprints in the Tutorial Quest %'Vor%'s Prize%'."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.8.4"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"June 15th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/475491-hotfix-1684/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed players being able to send Clan invites without the proper permissions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some clipping issues with new cloth physics."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some rigidity that would occur in cloth physics."}},
        {tags={"Deception","Ceres",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing the Datamass falling through levels at the start of a Missions on a certain Ceres spawn tile as seen here: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/topic/470421-the-datamass-for-the-deception-on-albedoceres-bugs/?page=2&tab=comments#comment-5285075 forum post%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed texture issues that would occur on some Grineer catwalks."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Gorgon",},text={"*Fixed duplicate entries for the Gorgon that would appear in player profile after killing certain enemies under the effects of Mind Control."}},
        {tags={"Vauban",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing an inappropriate amount of bounces from some projectile weapons."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a broken image showing in the Art Gallery."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.8.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"June 12th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/474057-hotfix-1683/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Reverted the change to Zephyr%'s Turbulence introduced in Update 16.8.0."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Players can now invite players to their clans from the chat menu."}},
        {tags={"Hind","Exterminate",},text={"*Exterminate minimap markers have been tweaked to make it less likely that you will leave a stray enemy behind and have to backtrack."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Adjustments have been made to the Kyroptera Syandana that should allow for better flip-flop physics."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Optimized the load time and memory footprint of the relays."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Elytron",},text={"*Fixed another issue causing players to take damage from Elytron’s Warhead ability."}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"*Fixed a visual issue that could occur with Rhino’s Iron Skin FX."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Fixed some audio cues inappropriately looping in Survival Missions."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Fixed a typo in ‘The New Strange’ Quest dialogue."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed some instances of players seeing multiple bubbles when Limbo casts Cataclysm."}},
        {tags={"Anku","Dex",},text={"*Fixed the Anku not appearing in the Codex."}},
        {tags={"Dex","Tempo Royale",},text={"*Fixed two Tempo Royale Mod entries appearing in the Codex."}},
        {tags={"Oberon",},text={"*Fixed Oberon’s agile animation being incorrectly labeled in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Anku",},text={"*Fixed Sugatras not properly attaching to the Anku."}},
        {tags={"Lephantis","Reach",},text={"*Fixed players being able to reach the ceiling of Lephantis’ lair after the boss encounter has been completed."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being able to ignore System or Global messages."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Grakata","Prisma Grakata","Prisma",},text={"*Fixed the Prisma Grakata not being tradable."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Grakata","Prisma Grakata","Prisma","Dex",},text={"*Fixed the Prisma Grakata not showing in Codex."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed Host Migrations not working properly when a player chooses to leave Conclave matches."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various localization issues."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.8.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"June 11th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/473134-hotfix-1681-1682/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue causing a broken HUD for players with their language set to Japanese."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.8.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"June 10th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/473134-hotfix-1681/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Enabled the following skin usage:"}},
        {tags={"Vaykor Marelok",},text={"**Day of the Dead now works on Vaykor Marelok."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Secura Dual Cestra",},text={"**Shock Camo now works on the Secura Dual Cestras."}},
        {tags={"Ballistica","Rakta Ballistica","Grineer Forest",},text={"**Grineer Forest Camo now works on the Rakta Ballistica"}},
        {tags={"Telos Akbolto",},text={"**Ormolu Skin now works on the Telos Akbolto!"}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Charge weapons not firing properly if you had any Fire Rate mods equipped."}},
        {tags={"Elytron","Thumper",},text={"*Rolled back a fix for Elytron%'s Thumper causing self damage - this is what broke weapons dealing self-damage intentionally where applicable."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed being unable to purchase Syndicate Restore Blueprints in Relays."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various crashes."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.8"},
["date"]={"June 10th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/473028-update-1680/"},
["image"]={"Update 16.8 Tenno Reinforcements.png"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'TENNO REINFORCEMENTS%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"KYROPTERA SYANDANA, ORMOLU SKIN BUNDLE and New Augment Mods available now!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Tenno, give your Warframe honorable style with the new Kyroptera Syandana! Complement the look with the Ormolu Weapon Skin Bundle, including a Skin for the Kyroptera. Add power to your Warframe with new Ability Augment Mods!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'KYROPTERA SYANDANA%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"Add the Kyroptera version of the traditional Tenno Syandana to your Warframe. Forged by Tenno artisans, its design is inspired by winged night creatures."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'ORMOLU SKIN BUNDLE%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Daikyu","Akbolto","Bolto","Tipedo",},text={"Ormolu Skins for your Daikyu, Akbolto, Bolto and Tipedo Weapons as well as the Ormolu Kyroptera Syandana will add an honorable brass and gold finish."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Augment Mods%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Reflect","Staggering Shield",},text={"Mesa: %[%[Staggering Shield%]%]: Reflected bullets have a 50PERZENTO chance to stumble enemies."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Oberon","Hallowed Reckoning",},text={"Oberon: %[%[Hallowed Reckoning%]%]: Enemies killed by Reckoning create small areas of Hallowed Ground for 10 seconds. Enemies standing in the Hallowed Ground take 150 damage per second, while allies gain 250 armor."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Additions%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Clan Warlords now have permissions to Kick and Suspend for certain timeframes in Clan & Alliance chat channels. Warlords also have the ability to grant this Permission to other Clan members."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added a ‘Charge Rate’ stat to the Arsenal for charge based weapons (bows, etc). You now have a number to associate your weapon Charge rate with - it will show how it is affected by the Fire Rate mods that you have equipped!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*The first map will now be selected randomly in all Conclave game types."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation","Team Annihilation",},text={"*Players will no longer spawn in waves in Team Annihilation."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Host Migration can now occur during Team Select screen."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Excavation","Dex",},text={"*The Excavation Scanner has been removed from the Codex."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Players can now control the camera when using the Dojo Pigment preview."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Adjusted the Ancient Healers healing pulse to be similar to a Small Team Health Restore with decreased frequency and a slightly shorter radius to match the Aura."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Mirage’s Hall of Mirror clones will no longer show cosmetic attachments for clients, or if your particle quality is set to low. This change is in an attempt to resolve reports of Performance/crash reports related to Mirage."}},
        {tags={"Simulacrum",},text={"*Simulacrum missions no longer count towards Mission Failed or Mission Quit statistics."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Users who are playing with UI/HUD off will no longer see outlines of their Squad allies through walls. This should help with Machinima/Content Creators needing cleaner scenes!"}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Interception",},text={"*Fixed an error that would occur in Interception when Rounds would not progress after killing the remaining enemies."}},
        {tags={"Lech Kril",},text={"*Fixed Lieutenant Lech Kril being unkillable due to players not being able to properly hit him with ranged weapons."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed Syndicate Spy missions not giving Vault rewards."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed Shotgun Mods not being included in the Conclave EOM rewards."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed all types of Ancient Healers not having the same Aura and healing pulse."}},
        {tags={"Elytron","Thumper",},text={"*Fixed incorrect color on the Elytron’s Thumper ability."}},
        {tags={"Castanas",},text={"*Fixed Castana explosions not using the proper energy color as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/456501-sancti-castanas-using-default-explosion-color-only/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed Frost’s Snowglobe giving two visual effects for Clients."}},
        {tags={"Hikou","Prime",},text={"*Fixed thrown weapons not showing the proper energy trails as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/469235-hikou-prime-energy-color-doesnt-corresponds-w-chosen-color/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={"Corpus Gas City",},text={"*Fixed spawning issue on Corpus Gas City tileset that would cause players to spawn inside of walls."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Lato",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Regulator pistols appearing when previewing other Warframes."}},
        {tags={"Oberon",},text={"*Fixed two copies of Oberon’s Reckoning FX showing on cast."}},
        {tags={"Lotus",},text={"*Fixed decorative Lotus symbols on challenge icons spinning sideways."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the minimap in Tutorial Missions not properly displaying the outline of the level."}},
        {tags={"Vor",},text={"*Fixed Captain Vor not properly using his abilities or attacks in the Tutorial Missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed several broken energy color effects as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/470549-several-energy-colour-based-effects-still-broken-since-165/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={"Tonkor",},text={"*Fixed Tonkor grenades bouncing off Boss weak points."}},
        {tags={"Lephantis",},text={"*Fixed radial damage not properly working against Lephantis."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Lato","Archwing",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Regulator pistols not being holstered when viewed in the Archwing Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed Zephyr’s Turbulence affecting enemies outside of its range."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed bullet deflection effects not playing on Mesa’s Shatter Shield."}},
        {tags={"Daikyu","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed the Daikyu having no charge limit in Conclave."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed visual issues on Orokin-Corrupted Corpus firing animations."}},
        {tags={"Lanka",},text={"*Fixed the Lanka’s fire rate not properly updating with fire rate Mods."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Zanuka becoming stuck on crates in various tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Simaris",},text={"*Fixed some visual issues on the Capture Target previewed in Simaris’ Library."}},
        {tags={"Elytron",},text={"*Fixed an issue allowing players to take damage from Elytron’s Warhead."}},
        {tags={"Attica",},text={"*Fixed the Attica not firing as a silent weapon - the weapon is now functioning as a Silent weapon by design."}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"*Fixed some alt-helmets not sitting properly on Prime Warframes."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the Gear menu not opening when players have their UI disabled."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Sentinel","Nightmare Mode",},text={"*Fixed Sentinels and Kubrow having shields in no-shields Nightmare Missions."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed the Loadout selection menu buttons appearing on screen when accessing the pause menu from the Conclave Mode selection menu."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed players fast switching Arsenal loadouts causing Archwing to appear in the PvE loadout."}},
        {tags={"Frost","Darvo",},text={"*Fixed a freeze that would occur when trying to view the Darvo Deal menu."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed Clan type and total number of members not properly displaying in the Clan menu under Communication."}},
        {tags={"Liset","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed an issue that would occur when viewing End of Mission results in the Liset after completing a Conclave match."}},
        {tags={"Conclave","Blind Shot","Lucky Shot",},text={"*Fixed the Lucky Shot/Blind Shot Conclave Mod adding accuracy instead of decreasing it."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed NPCs getting stuck in cover and ignoring the player."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed being able to spawn two of the same type of Specters if you have two different types of Specters equipped. The intention of Specters is to have only one instance for each type of Specter summoned."}},
        {tags={"Tonkor",},text={"*Fixed Tonkor’s iron sights not closing when quick meleeing."}},
        {tags={"Earth",},text={"*Fixed Grineer on Earth tilesets falling under the level; making them unkillable."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Mirage’s Hall of Mirrors causing a crash if you had no customizations on your Warframe or Melee weapon."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Syndicate Medallions being invisible as per : %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/470822-invisible-syndicate-collectibles/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various localization issues."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end

{
["update number"]={"16.7"},
["date"]={"June 4th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/469825-update-167/"},
["title"]={"Tenno Reinforcements: Anku"},
["image"]={"Syf9TB4.jpg"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Tenno Reinforcements%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Anku",},text={"%[%[Anku%]%] scythe available now!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Paris","Anku",},text={"Reap havoc with the Anku! This full-sized Tenno scythe%'s design is inspired by the Paris Bow and is just as deadly."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Anku","Clan","Research",},text={"Add the Anku to your Arsenal by visiting the Market or Tenno Clan Research today!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Augment Mods:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Cataclysmic Continuum",},text={"Limbo - Cataclysm: %[%[Cataclysmic Continuum%]%]: Duration is restored by 1s whenever an enemy is killed inside."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Saryn","Hind","Contagion Cloud",},text={"Saryn - Contagion: %[%[Contagion Cloud%]%]: Melee kills will leave behind a cloud of gas that deals 150 damage per second for 12 seconds within 2 meters."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Additions%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Spy","Earth",},text={"*A new Spy Mission is available on Earth, Cambria! This Mission contains Spy Vaults unique to the Earth tileset, including unique security measures for day and night cycles!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Some weapon attachments will now support energy color changes. This feature will slowly be expanded to other weapons, so keep an eye out for special effects on those dangles!"}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Added several missing weapon entries to the Codex."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Cephalons in Cephalon Capture have received a visual update!"}},
        {tags={"Mag","Burdened Magazine","Fast Hands","Magazine Warp","Slip Magazine","Stabilizer","Steady Hands","Tactical Pump","Terminal Velocity",},text={"*The following mods have been disabled in PvP: Magazine Warp, Slip Magazine, Burdened Magazine, Stabilizer, Steady Hands, Fast Hands, Quick Draw, Tactical Pump and Terminal Velocity. They have been replaced with PvP specific versions that have been added to the Mission Rewards of all PvP game types."}},
        {tags={"Anku",},text={"*The Anku is available for use in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Strun Wraith","Wraith",},text={"*Reduced the damage of Strun Wraith in PvP."}},
},--misc end
["additions"]={
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Spy","Earth",},text={"*Earth: Cambria is now a Spy Mission."}},
        {tags={"Excavation","Earth","Lua",},text={"*Earth: Lua is now an Excavation Mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Many Grineer and Corpus environmental props have received PBR updates!"}},
        {tags={"Strun",},text={"*The Strun has received a PBR update!"}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Chaos Sphere",},text={"*Nyx’s Chaos Sphere Augment FX has been made more visible."}},
        {tags={"Hyena Pack",},text={"*Added a new sound effect to the Hyena Pack’s charge ability."}},
        {tags={"Spy","Lotus",},text={"*Reduced the amount of Lotus transmissions that occur during Spy Missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various updates to incorrect or inaccurate item / Mod descriptions in game. More to come!"}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*In an effort to reduce in-game spam, accounts with a Mastery Rank of 0 can only start conversations with friends, clanmates, or recently encountered players."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Slightly altered the vocal sounds of Infested Chargers."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players can no longer open the pause menu when playing a hacking minigame."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Rare Resource Blueprints can now be sold for 30,000 Credits."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed Zephyr’s Turbulence not properly deflecting some ranged attacks against hit-scan enemies."}},
        {tags={"Interception","Hive",},text={"*Fixed an error causing a Hive related message to appear on Interception Missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Design Council features not properly appearing in Korean."}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Sonic Fracture",},text={"*Fixed Banshee’s Sonic Fracture FX lingering if the target became immediately ragdolled."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed Warframe armor not properly showing on Chroma when Effigy is cast followed by a cloaking ability."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being able to kill ragdoll-able bosses by flinging the boss off the map."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue causing ragdolled bosses to take a long time to revive."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players not properly entering a dive animation when jumping, sprinting, then firing their weapon."}},
        {tags={"Tonkor",},text={"*Fixed the Tonkor’s projectile preview arc appearing underneath other effects."}},
        {tags={"Daikyu",},text={"*Fixed the Daikyu killing the client if being drawn during a Host Migration."}},
        {tags={"Soma Prime","Prime","Dex",},text={"*Fixed an error that would occur when viewing Soma Prime in the Codex."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed player arrows not returning to their default visual when the Meer Arrow Customization is unequipped."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed player’s weapons becoming stuck when changing weapons after firing a bow."}},
        {tags={"Valkyr",},text={"*Fixed Valkyr’s Hysteria playing sounds from related to the player’s un-equipped melee weapon."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed an issue preventing the use of Mods shared by Sentinels when the player does not have a Sentinel equipped or active."}},
        {tags={"Excavation","Dex",},text={"*Fixed the description of Excavation Missions in the Codex having incorrect information on how to play the Mission type. This is the first of many revisions coming to inaccurate text as per this thread: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/466107-ongoing-obsolete-descriptions/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue preventing players from properly blocking AoE attacks as per this thread: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/468668-nolonger-able-to-block-grineer-radial-slam/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={"Paris Prime","Prime",},text={"*Fixed the Paris Prime showing covering up menu options."}},
        {tags={"Frost","Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing the UI to freeze after Zephyr casts Tornado."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue causing duplicate FX to occur, resulting in increased latency."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a hangup caused by opening the pause menu mid-hacking."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed fancy sparkly trails not showing up on all unique Arrow Skins."}},
        {tags={"Darvo",},text={"*Fixed a typo in Darvo’s VO present in the tutorial Missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Warframes with 100 base stamina showing as 8 stamina when equipping the Warframe in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed issues related to Sentinel movement that would cause performance problems in PhysX."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed water in Orokin-themed Conclave maps appearing as black."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed a crash in Conclave’s Annihilation when a Host Migration occurs after a fourth player joins a game mid-session."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end

{
["update number"]={"16.6.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"May 28th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/466144-hotfix-1661/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Annihilation",},text={"*Respawn timers are now independent for each player killed instead of respawning players in communal ‘waves’ in Annihilation."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Health orbs now heal for 50 HP in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Ember",},text={"*Reduced the range and damage of Ember’s World on Fire in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation",},text={"*Oro pickups in Annihilation are now colored red."}},
        {tags={"Braton","Conclave",},text={"*Braton stats fixed in the Conclave Arsenal."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Warframe Ability projectiles no longer deal double damage to players on headshots."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation",},text={"*The Forfeit Timer will now properly activate when there’s only one player in Annihilation."}},
},--misc end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Self Destruct",},text={"*Fixed a crash that would occur due to enemies self destructing."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where cosmetic attachments would not properly display their intended location."}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Fixed Nyx’s Mind Control removing armor from enemies."}},
        {tags={"Carrier","Vacuum",},text={"*Fixed items vacuumed in by the Carrier becoming stuck to the Carrier."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed Manic Grineer becoming impossible to kill due to continued teleporting."}},
        {tags={"Boar","Trial",},text={"*Fixed Trial Leaderboards not properly archiving."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed minor visual issues with Oro visuals in the Conclave dioramas."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed Annihilation Conclave matches not ending when only one player remains in the Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed players not properly being assigned to even teams in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed an error caused by a host leaving a solo game of Cephalon Capture."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed Energy Surge not properly activating in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing players to immediately pick up the Cephalon after dropping it in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Braton","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed the Braton’s stats not reading correctly in the Conclave Arsenal."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.6"},
["date"]={"May 27th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/465618-update-1660/"},
["image"]={"Update 16.6 Tenno Customizations.png"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'TENNO CUSTOMIZATIONS%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"New Liset Skins, Alternate Helmet and Augment Mods available now!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Vauban","Liset",},text={"Tenno, show your true colors with new Customizations! Give the outside of your Liset some flare with new Skins, style Vauban with a new Alternate Helmet and add power to your Warframes with new Ability Augments Mods."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"%'%'%'LISET SKINS%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"KOTARA: Cover your Liset in geometric forms inspired by nature’s order with the Kotara Liset Skin."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Liset",},text={"PAHTA: Add an explosive plumage design to your Liset with the Pahta Liset Skin."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Vauban",},text={"ARMISITICE VAUBAN HELMET: Add this uniquely-styled helmet to your Arsenal and give Vauban a new look."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Warframe Augments%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Chaos Sphere",},text={"*Nyx - Chaos - Chaos Sphere: For a PERZENTO duration of Chaos, any enemy entering the effect radius will be affected by *Chaos. This effect radius decreases over time."}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Sonic Fracture",},text={"*Banshee - Sonic Boom - Sonic Fracture: Enemies hit by Sonic Bomb have their armor reduced."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Weapons in the Conclave Arsenal will now display their Impact, Puncture and Slash values."}},
        {tags={},text={"*The amount of ammo collected from ammo pickups has been increased."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Ammo pool is now capped at the amount of ammo you start with."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Frost%'s Freeze damage has been reduced in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Ember",},text={"*Ember%'s Fireball damage has been reduced in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Mag%'s Pull damage has been reduced in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Volt%'s Shock damage has been reduced in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Excalibur%'s Slash Dash damage has been increased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Oberon",},text={"*Oberon%'s Smite damage has been decreased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Strun",},text={"*Strun’s damage has been increased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Strun",},text={"*MK1 Strun’s damage has been increased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Braton",},text={"*MK1 Braton’s damage has been decreased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Strun Wraith","Wraith",},text={"*Strun Wraith’s damage has been decreased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Latron",},text={"*All Latron series weapon damage has been decreased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Daikyu",},text={"*Daikyu’s damage has been decreased in PvP."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Trial",},text={"*Increased the time taken by AI to hack consoles in Trials from 2 seconds to 4 for the Vay Hek stage."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed Clients being unable to see Kubrow’s revive percentages or downed timers."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed red enemy markers appearing on the mini map/HUD after a Kubrow has been revived."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the visual quality of enemies dropping when fighting in thick fog."}},
        {tags={"Void","Derelict",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing Clients to spawn outside of the level when entering a Mission on a Void Derelict tileset."}},
        {tags={"Jackal",},text={"*Fixed the Jackal sliding around on the ground or getting stuck when firing its grenades."}},
        {tags={"Daikyu",},text={"*Fixed the Daikyu becoming locked in a full draw animation when sprinting."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an error causing Clients to be unable to see bonus Mission objectives through the pause menu."}},
        {tags={"Interception",},text={"*Fixed an error that caused the minimap to not display properly in Interception Missions."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Dera",},text={"*Fixed an issue that allowed the Dera to retain damage bonuses from Volt’s Electric Shield, resulting in higher than normal damage."}},
        {tags={"Vauban","Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed a visual bug on Kubrow dens that showed their homes being made of reinforced metal!"}},
        {tags={"Sentinel","Carrier",},text={"*Fixed Carrier Sentinels not sucking in Sigils and other item drops."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Fixed Navigation segments becoming unlocked if a player completes a quest on a segment that they have not unlocked normally."}},
        {tags={"Frost","Terminal Velocity",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing shots from a weapon equipped with the Terminal Velocity Mod to freeze in mid air, becoming impossible to shoot through."}},
        {tags={"Trial","Retribution",},text={"*Fixed Nullifier enemies appearing in non-Nightmare Law of Retribution Trials."}},
        {tags={"Hijack",},text={"*Fixed the objective in Hijack Missions not properly draining shields from players."}},
        {tags={"Rescue",},text={"*Fixed Rescue Mission Mastery Tests becoming unbeatable if the Rescue target falls off the level."}},
        {tags={"Retribution",},text={"*Fixed the Law of Retribution’s second phase becoming unbeatable if a player dies before the pressure-plate puzzle can be completed."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Clients seeing double experience when an enemy is killed."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed some Conclave-only Mods being usable in normal gameplay, and vice-versa."}},
        {tags={"Daikyu",},text={"*Fixed Daikyu’s charged shots causing odd animations when turning to aim."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed punch-through Mods sometimes not having a proper effect on weapon’s fire."}},
},--fixes end

This is intended to solve rampant out-of-memory errors for 32bit Windows, especially when entering large levels and relays. Memory reduction has also improved load times for all platforms."}},
