{
["update number"]={"16.5.6"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"May 19th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/461080-hotfix-1656/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%' "}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"*Players will no longer receive experience for returning the Cephalon in Capture the Cephalon."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players will now drop 1 energy orb for every 25 energy they had stored at their time of death."}},
        {tags={"Boar",},text={"*Oro obtained and kills earned will no longer be recorded on the scoreboard after victory has been declared and the match has ended."}},
        {tags={"Teshin",},text={"*All new mods that were not able to be equipped after purchasing from Teshin have been fixed"}},
        {tags={"Tigris",},text={"*Increased the damage of the Tigris in PvP."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Empty Energy Stations will instantly spawn orbs when Energy Surge is initiated."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation",},text={"*Energy Surge will now activate in Annihilation at 80 total kills or 20 kills for one player."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Oro will now move towards the player that damages another player just before they are killed by the environment."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation",},text={"*Changed the visual effect of Oro pickups in Annihilation."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Made refinements to the custom Liset interior color details."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Zephyr’s Turbulence visual effects will look slightly more transparent when cloaked."}},
        {tags={"Loki","Sentinel",},text={"*Loki’s Invisibility, when used with a Sentinel, will now give the Sentinel its proper energy color."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various localization updates."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Hema","Solar Rail",},text={"*Fixed a crash that would occur when editing a Solar Rail Schema."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a crash caused by weapons and abilities that caused radial damage."}},
        {tags={"Atomos",},text={"*Fixed a visual bug related to using the Atomos on certain enemies as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/458340-atomos-specific-infested-death-animation-visual-bug/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed incorrect channeling FX on Excalibur’s Proto Armor skin."}},
        {tags={"Nukor",},text={"*Fixed coloring on Nukor as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/457936-hotfix-1655/page-2#entry5097301 this thread%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some channeling FX not showing up correctly for Hosts and Clients."}},
        {tags={"Hydroid","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Hydroid’s Tempest Barrage not properly scaling in damage with rank."}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Oberon",},text={"*Fixed Oberon and Banshee’s Immortal Skin not properly applying to their shoulder pads."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Captura",},text={"*Fixed the Capture target becoming uncapturable if a player dies and then revives mid-capture."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed the player’s Warframe colors changing when viewing different Archwings in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Trial",},text={"*Fixed players receiving a ‘not eligible for reward’ message when joining a squad for a Nightmare Trial."}},
        {tags={"Hijack",},text={"*Fixed an issue in Corpus Hijack Missions that would prevent mission completion and stop all enemy spawns."}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"*Fixed an issue on Void tilesets that would break navigation markers and enemy navigation."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed a lighting issue appearing in a particular Grineer Spy Vault"}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed a lighting issue that would appear in Dojo hallways."}},
        {tags={"Helios","Deconstructor",},text={"*Fixed Helios not attacking enemies when equipped with the Deconstructor."}},
        {tags={"Teshin","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed multiple Conclave Mods that were not showing up as available for purchase through Teshin."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.5.5"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"May 14th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/457936-update-1655/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Proximity triggers for automatic doors in the Docking Bay map will have been made larger to ensure smoother navigation through the map."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Improved visual effects will make Conclave game mode selection look more obvious."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Grineer Sealab",},text={"*Cleaned up various assets on the Grineer Sealab tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*An audio cue has been added to accepting or rejecting a Squad invite."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue occurring in reverb audio settings."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some incorrect collision detection on the Ogma."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed Frost’s Snowglobe ability not properly getting a tint from the player’s energy color."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Sealab",},text={"*Fixed the broken minimap in the Grineer Sealab tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed energy colors not properly getting applied to Syndicate Syandanas."}},
        {tags={"Reflect",},text={"*Fixed Syndicate Syandanas not reflecting changes until you leave the attachments menu."}},
        {tags={"Clan","Liset",},text={"*Fixed players being able to bypass trade cooldowns by moving quickly between the Dojo and Liset."}},
        {tags={"Alad V",},text={"*Fixed issue during Alad V and Zanuka boss fight that would cause Zanuka to stop attacking."}},
        {tags={"Fusion Core",},text={"*Fixed Transmutation and Fusion cores appearing when players enable the “Show Maxed” filter in the Mod menu."}},
        {tags={"Darvo",},text={"*Fixed the hinges in Darvo’s shop floating about inappropriately."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed a hole in the Canyon Settlement Conclave Map."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed players trying to enter a Conclave map with group preferences set to “Solo” not being given an error message as to why they cannot do so."}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"*Fixed issues with Rhino’s Iron Skin as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/452952-iron-skin-texture-still-broken/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing bottom custom colors of the Liset not matching the top."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.5.4"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"May 14th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/457698-hotfix-1654/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Grineer Sealab",},text={"*Made various reverb and audio adjustments to the Grineer Sealab’s ambiance."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed several gameplay crashes."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed performance issues related to dual-core machines."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Daikyu",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Daikyu bow being pulled and causing clipping issues while sprinting."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a progression stopper in the DNA room of the Tubemen of Regor Operation."}},
        {tags={"Carrier","Vacuum",},text={"*Fixed Carrier’s Vacuum not picking up Pigments."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issues with some tips showing up as file paths on loading screens."}},
        {tags={"Hijack","Trial","Nightmare Mode","Retribution",},text={"*Further fixed players being able to trigger the Hijack mission of The Trials of Retribution Nightmare mode before completing the puzzle stage."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed revived players seeing themselves as friendly AI on the minimap."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Fixed an issue allowing players to use restricted weapons in Tactical Alerts."}},
        {tags={"Loki",},text={"*Fixed Loki’s Invisibility not being affected by energy colors as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/457479-lokis-invisibility-doesnt-get-affected-to-your-energy-colors-and-others/ this thread%]"}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.5.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"May 13th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/457163-hotfix-1653/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Nekros","Valkyr",},text={"%'%'%'The removal of Arcane Enhancements on Nekros’ Mortos Syandana and Valkyr’s Bonds is now live, and any attached Arcanes have been returned to your Inventory. As stated in Hotfix 16.5.2, this change is due to Nekros and Valkyr having an unfair advantage over other Warframes by having one additional Arcane slot.%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Players in Cephalon Capture will now be notified of which player stole their Cephalon."}},
        {tags={"Recover",},text={"*Oro will now be dropped by a player who self-kills. This Oro can also be recovered or claimed."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation",},text={"*The amount of Oro needed to claim victory in Annihilation modes has been reduced from 30 to 25."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Increased Conclave standing gains from XP to be 2x the amount of normal Syndicate standing."}},
        {tags={"Boar","Annihilation","Conclave",},text={"*Changed the Conclave Annihilation Scoreboards to better highlight how ranking works:"}},
        {tags={},text={"**We renamed the ‘Technique’ column Oro."}},
        {tags={},text={"**We now display Oro in the first column as it is the stat that determines rank. "}},
},--misc end
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Annihilation","Teshin","Conclave",},text={"*Added Teshin “Free For All” call out in Conclave Annihilation mode."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Grineer Sealab",},text={"*Removed unnecessary water volumes from the Grineer Sealab tileset."}},
        {tags={"Derelict",},text={"*Tweaked ambience sounds in the Orokin Derelicts."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Tweaked volume in numerous Gas City levels."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Galleon",},text={"*Updated reverb in Gas City, Forest, and Grineer Galleon levels."}},
        {tags={"Amprex",},text={"*PBR material has been added to the Amprex!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Reverb 2.0 has been improved, reverbs will feel less dense now, enabling you to hear enemies and weapons more clearly."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Hijack","Trial","Nightmare Mode","Retribution",},text={"*Fixed the final doorway not opening during the Hijack mission in The Trials of Retribution Nightmare mode. Please note that all keys removed from inventories will be reimbursed as this bug related to a lot of failed attempts - we are working on a script now."}},
        {tags={"Phobos",},text={"*Fixed Hellion Power Carries not spawning on Stickney, Phobos."}},
        {tags={"Hijack","Trial","Nightmare Mode","Retribution",},text={"*Fixed players being able to trigger the Hijack mission of The Trials of Retribution Nightmare mode before completing the puzzle stage."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed numerous NPC related crashes."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed the Navigation menu overlapping with a kiosk screen when opened in the Relays."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players receiving extra copies of the Operation: Tubemen of Regor badge each time a battle was completed."}},
        {tags={"Hive",},text={"*Fixed Hive Tumors being undestroyable."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Muzzle Flash",},text={"*Fixed Corpus Crewman muzzle flash not updating position."}},
        {tags={"Ember","Mesa","Vauban","Fireball Frenzy",},text={"*Fixed Ember’s Fireball Frenzy Augment causing Fireballs to bounce off of Mesa’s Shatter Shield."}},
        {tags={"Defense",},text={"*Fixed objective markers disappearing in Defense missions."}},
        {tags={"Dread",},text={"*Fixed Dread and Meer arrows not being colorable (for real this time!)."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Grineer Sealab",},text={"*Fixed players being randomly teleported for no reason in a Grineer Sealab hallway."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed persisting enemy markers after an enemy has died."}},
        {tags={"Invasion",},text={"*Fixed a script error when receiving Invasion Battle Pay (not related to the event)."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed players receiving a script error when crafting - this could result in a game freeze."}},
        {tags={"Teshin",},text={"*Fixed Teshin stating ‘5 Kills Remain’ even though the max score has been achieved."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed overlapping lines in the Chat window."}},
        {tags={"Tipedo",},text={"*Fixed the Tipedo not having Tenno script on it as intended."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed right side Transmissions being cut off due to having a low resolution."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed text issue with lengthy player names in the End of Mission and Mission Progress menus."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.5.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"May 13th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/456211-hotfix-1651/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Oberon","Conclave",},text={"*Reduced the number of projectiles and damage of Oberon’s Smite in the Conclaves."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Volt","Annihilation","Team Annihilation",},text={"*Slowed down magnetism speed of Oros in Annihilation and Team Annihilation."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Removed some collision volumes from the Freight Line conclave map."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Reduced the volume of Grineer music in the Conclaves."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Kubrow","Sentinel",},text={"*Overshields now apply to Specters (Max 1200), Kubrows, and Sentinels (max 600)."}},
        {tags={"Arbiters of Hexis","Bite",},text={"*Increased the brightness on Arbiters of Hexis Profile Icon."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Swapped cracked glass material in the Dojo for a new cracked look."}},
        {tags={"Cernos","Daikyu","Dera","Paris","Paris Prime","Prime",},text={"*Adjusted the draw radius and shot radius on Paris, Paris Prime, Cernos, and Daikyu. Before, players were able to hear you draw your bow from considerably far away distances that would give away your position."}},
        {tags={"Nekros","Valkyr",},text={"*The Nekros Mortos Syandana and Valkyr Bonds can no longer be Enhanced. This change is due to Nekros and Valkyr having an unfair advantage over other Warframes by having one additional Arcane slot. We%'re going to be removing Arcanes off these two attachments, and running a script to compensate players so the Enhancements can be used on intended Syandanas. We will notify everyone when this script has started."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed numerous stability crashes."}},
        {tags={"Hijack","Trial","Nightmare Mode","Retribution",},text={"*Fixed instances of shields being nuked to 0 as soon as the core is pushed during the Hijack portion of The Trials of Retribution Nightmare mode."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Shield Transference",},text={"*Fixed Mag’s Shield Polarize wiping any overshields you had regardless of having Mag’s Shield Transference Augment Mod or not as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/456684-has-mag-been-nerfed-or-is-it-just-a-bug/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed Shield Restores not working on Sentinels."}},
        {tags={"Vauban",},text={"*Fixed some of Vauban’s powers only lasting for 1 second as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/456530-vauban-abilities-only-last-a-few-moments/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed aliases not appearing in Operation Tubemen of Regor reward inbox messages."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed the Navigation menu not opening right away when in the Conclave mode selection menu."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Captura",},text={"*Fixed Capture Targets being uncapturable if you die and get revived during the mission."}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris",},text={"*Fixed incorrect spelling in Simaris Synthesis lore entry."}},
        {tags={"Teshin","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed Teshin over using the term ‘Begin!’ during a conclave match."}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"*Fixed the Prime Velorum Sigil sometimes having a black outline instead of the intended gold outline."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Mastery Sigils overriding future equipped sigils and all sigil previews in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Invasion",},text={"*Fixed not being able to select ‘Support’ in the Invasion UI due to having a smaller screen resolution."}},
        {tags={"Daikyu",},text={"*Fixed the Daikyu bow string not being properly placed in players hands until after initial shot."}},
        {tags={"Extend",},text={"*Fixed Operation Tubemen of Regor progress bar extending outside of the Navigation window."}},
        {tags={"Hind","Clan",},text={"*Fixed cracked glass in the Dojo not rendering properly if fluid was behind it."}},
        {tags={"Dex","Extend","Parry",},text={"*Fixed Polearm weapons not being extended in the Codex or when in the Parry pose in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed flashlights on bows and other weapons not following the camera."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Sealab",},text={"*Fixed enemies occasionally spawning in the Grineer Sealab ceiling."}},
        {tags={"Syndicates",},text={"*Fixed selecting the Navigation menu from Syndicates UI does not close the Syndicate UI."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed temporary color customization%'s being reverted when navigating through the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Dread",},text={"*<%s>Fixed the Dread arrow not applying your Energy color.<%/s> Not fixed, further investigation required!"}},
        {tags={"Hind","Simulacrum",},text={"*Fixed the Pause menu appearing behind the Simulacrum UI."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Retrieve",},text={"*Fixed a rare instance of the Drahk Master stealing your weapon and placing it in a teleport zone; making it irretrievable. Your weapon will now be moved to a safer zone where you can retrieve it."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Mod rewards being displayed as Rank 0, even if they are greater than Rank 1."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.5.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"May 12th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/456211-hotfix-1651/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Annihilation","Teshin",},text={"*Adjusted the time before Teshin’s VO intro to Annihilation begins."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Clarified description of Rare Resource Blueprints to state that these blueprints are permanent."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed a crash caused by crafting new weapon blueprints in the Dojo."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed multiple crashes."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed Oro pickups in Conclave not showing the correct visual flair color when being obtained."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation",},text={"*Fixed Oro taking some time to appear for Clients in Annihilation game modes."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation",},text={"*Fixed Squad HUD not properly displaying all players in Annihilation game modes."}},
        {tags={"Annihilation","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed chat in Conclave’s Annihilation not sending to all players."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Spectra",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Spectral Scream preventing players from being able to use quick melee."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some misaligned text in the World State Window."}},
        {tags={"Vauban","Trial","Retribution",},text={"*Fixed Nullifier auras from drones in the Law of Retribution Nightmare Trial not properly breaking lingering Warframe CC abilities such as Bastille."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed waypoint markers placed on elevators disappearing when map is toggled."}},
        {tags={"Nekros","Valkyr",},text={"*Fixed Arrow skins and Nekros & Valkyr’s unique attachments not using the player’s custom colors."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed the Freedom and Discovery Conclave Sigils having the same icon."}},
        {tags={"Interception",},text={"*Fixed waypoint markers in Interception not properly updating."}},
        {tags={"Arena","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed players being able to leave the arena boundaries in the Canyon Settlement Conclave map."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel","Shade","Ghost","Stalk",},text={"*Fixed the Shade Sentinel’s Ghost and the Hura%'s Stalk Ability not properly functioning."}},
        {tags={"Hive",},text={"*Fixed Hive nodes in the Hive game mode not taking damage from certain attacks."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed the Clan’s ‘Join Session’ button not functioning properly."}},
        {tags={"Glaive","Dex",},text={"*Fixed an issue causing Glaive weapons not to appear as full size when viewed in the Codex."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed buff display on the HUD not properly disappearing after the buff expires."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.5"},
["date"]={"May 12th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/455995-update-165-tubemen-of-regor/"},
["title"]={"Tubemen of Regor"},
["image"]={"Tubemen of Regor.png"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Event: OPERATION TUBEMEN OF REGOR"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'(We must first hotfix for general stability before we start the event, stay tuned for 16.5.1)%'%'%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'Tenno,"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Tyl Regor",},text={"You have the opportunity to strike at the heart of Tyl Regor’s secret cloning labs but this opportunity comes with a difficult choice."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Hate","Alad V","Nef Anyo",},text={"Alad V thinks these labs contain a cure for the Infestation that riddles his body and he wants us to find it. Nef Anyo hates Alad and will do anything to keep him from becoming strong again, including destroying the cure he seeks. Both are offering to lead us to the labs."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"You must decide which is the lesser of two evils.%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Lotus",},text={"- The Lotus"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'HERE%'S WHAT YOU%'LL NEED TO DO%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Uranus",},text={"*Visit the Grineer Ocean on Uranus."}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Nef Anyo",},text={"*You must decide if you wish to help Alad V or Nef Anyo. Helping Alad V means stealing the cure for his Infestation, while helping Nef Anyo means destroying the cure."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Locate the Pump Room. You will need to flood this facility."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Break open the pipes to allow pumped water to flood the room then start the pumping process at the nearby console."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Once you have activated the console, wait for the pumping process to complete. Be alert, Tenno! Enemies may attack. Keep them from interrupting the process."}},
        {tags={},text={"*When the pumping process is finished, use the elevator to access the caves below. Enemies may be waiting, so use caution."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Your next steps will be determined by the side you have chosen:"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Nef Anyo",},text={"Destroy (help Nef Anyo)"}},
        {tags={},text={"- Locate the Genetic Experiment. Attack and defeat it."}},
        {tags={},text={"- Proceed to Extraction."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Alad V",},text={"Steal (help Alad V)"}},
        {tags={"Sabotage",},text={"- Locate two Console areas in the DNA Room. Find nearby Toxin cells and apply them to each Console to extract important information and sabotage the cloning process."}},
        {tags={},text={"- Proceed to Extraction."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'WHAT YOU NEED TO KNOW%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Nef Anyo","Research",},text={"- You may choose to assist either Nef Anyo by Destroying Grineer Research Components or Alad V by Stealing Grineer Research Components."}},
        {tags={},text={"- This Operation starts *soon* until Wednesday, May 20 at 12 p.m. EDT."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'SCORING AND REWARDS%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Nef Anyo",},text={"Event Weapon –Fully support Nef Anyo or Alad V in at least 4 regional battles"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Dera","Dera Vandal","Nef Anyo","Lotus","Vandal",},text={"*%[%[Dera Vandal|DERA VANDAL%]%]: A customized version of the Dera, featuring a metallic finish and Lotus decal. (If Nef Anyo is victorious at the end of the Event.)"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Karak Wraith","Alad V","Assault","Wraith",},text={"*%[%[Karak Wraith|KARAK WRAITH%]%]: A different take on the Grineer assault rifle, the Karak Wraith features unique styling. (If Alad V is victorious at the end of the Event.)"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={"Valkyr","Conclave",},text={"*Valkyr is now available for use in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Ember","Conclave",},text={"*Ember is now available for use in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Oberon","Conclave",},text={"*Oberon is now available for use in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*New Title, Hurricane, available from Conclave Syndicate."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Chat channels and Waypoints are now Team Only."}},
        {tags={"Tempo Royale",},text={"*Tempo Royale stance has been temporarily removed from PvP."}},
        {tags={"Raptors","Four Riders","Malicious Raptor",},text={"*Malicious Raptor and Four Riders stances enabled in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Boltor","Boltor Prime","Dera","Dera Vandal","Karak","Dual Heat Swords","Fragor","Glaive","Glaive Prime","Heat Sword","Kestrel","Prime","Vandal","Wraith","Conclave",},text={"*The following weapons are now available for use in Conclave: Dera, Dera Vandal, Boltor, Boltor Prime, Tonfa, Heat Sword, Dual Heat Swords, Glaive, Glaive Prime, Kestrel, Fragor, Wraith Karak."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Players will now be timed out after 60 seconds of inactivity in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"*Rhino Charge damage increased."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Chroma’s Vex armour duration increased."}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Players under control of Nyx’s Mind Control or Chaos will no longer see the FX on their Warframe."}},
        {tags={"Braton",},text={"*MK1 Braton damage increased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Karak",},text={"*Karak damage increased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Grakata",},text={"*Grakata damage increased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Gorgon",},text={"*Gorgon damage increased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Magnus",},text={"*Magnus series damage increased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Sicarus",},text={"*Sicarus series damage increased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Aklato",},text={"*AKLato damage decreased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Skana",},text={"*Skana series damage increased in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Martial Magnetism mod effects only applied with the melee weapon equipped."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Headshot damage multiplier no longer applied to radial damage."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Projectile weapons now display their damage correctly in the stats section of the Conclave Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Slow debuffs now effect melee weapon speed."}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"*XP for kills in Capture the Cephalon significantly reduced."}},
        {tags={},text={"*XP for capturing the Cephalon raised to 5000 XP and is awarded to every member of the team."}},
        {tags={},text={"*2500 XP awarded for returning your team’s Cephalon."}},
},--misc end
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Grineer Sealab",},text={"%'%'%'New Tileset Preview: Grineer Sealabs%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Tyl Regor","Uranus",},text={"*Hidden under the toxic oceans of Uranus is Tyl Regor’s genetic labs. Built into the massive coral pillars rising up from the depths these twisting halls represent the latest in Grineer scientific achievement."}},
        {tags={"Uranus",},text={"*A preview of this tileset will be available during Operation Tubemen of Regor, so be sure to let us know what you think of Uranus’ latest expansion!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Trial",},text={"%'%'%'New Nightmare Trial Difficulty%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Boar","Trial",},text={"*New Trial leaderboards URL (switched %[%http://content.warframe.com/dynamic/trialNightmareStats.php Status%] raid.php to trial.php):"}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Trial",},text={"*Nightmare Trials are challenging encounters designed for experience groups of Tenno looking for a new challenge. Harder hitting enemies, new traps and tweaks to each of the existing encounters in the Trials of Vay Hek will push your squad’s coordination to the limit."}},
        {tags={"Nightmare Mode",},text={"*We want all Tenno to enjoy our first attempt at a Nightmare mode of this size and intensity. Please keep in mind that adjustments may be needed if the content proves to be too difficult. Good luck!"}},
        {tags={"Saryn","Hek","Vay Hek","Trial",},text={"*Players can purchase the Nightmare Trial of Vay Hek Key from the Marketplace for 100,000 Credits. Building a key costs 5000 Credits, 1000 Rubedo, 18000 Nanospores, and 2 Gallium."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%[%[File:TubeReinf.jpg|center%]%]"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Tenno Reinforcements%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Daikyu","Atomos","Boltace",},text={"Boltace Tonfa, Daikyu Bow and Atomos Particle Cannon available now!"}},
        {tags={"Daikyu","Atomos","Boltace",},text={"Tenno, master three new additions to the Warframe Arsenal! Equip the Boltace tonfa, Daikyu bow or Atomos particle cannon to bring destruction to the enemy."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Boltor","Boltace",},text={"*%[%[Boltace|BOLTACE%]%]: An intimidating Tonfa set designed to match the briary design of the Boltor."}},
        {tags={"Daikyu",},text={"*%[%[Daikyu|DAIKYU%]%]: The Daikyu takes great strength to draw back, but provides added power and range to every shot."}},
        {tags={"Saryn","Atomos",},text={"*%[%[Atomos|ATOMOS%]%]: This particle cannon generates a condensed beam of super-heated plasma designed to melt rock to ore, and enemies to molten slag."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Get these new in-game additions by visiting the Market today!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Accessories%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Caggro Sugatra: Expressing the martial finesse and skill of its master, the Caggro Sugatra is based on a Grineer design."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Adventus Arrow Skin Collection: A collection of unique arrow skins, compatible with any bow. Includes the Cattrail, Sylus and Meer Arrow Skins. Each Skin is also available separately for purchase."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Expansion%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Annihilation","Team Annihilation","Conclave",},text={"Two new game modes have been added to Conclave! Work with your fellow Tenno to shut down the enemy team in Team Annihilation, capturing Oro dropped from each player kill to earn points toward victory. Want to prove your superiority in combat alone? Join in Annihilation and work against 7 other tenno in a deathmatch free-for-all."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"New Maps:"}},
        {tags={"Annihilation","Team Annihilation","Conclave",},text={"The following maps are now available in the Annihilation and Team Annihilation Conclave map rotation!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Canyon Settlement"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Freight Line"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Navigation Array"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Docking Bay"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Mods%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Valkyr","Hysterical Fixation",},text={"*Valkyr: Hysteria - Hysterical Fixation- Decreases initial duration of Hysteria, but increases duration by 5 seconds for each kill."}},
        {tags={"Ember",},text={"*Ember: Fire Blast - Purifying Flame - Allies that enter the fire will have up to 4 debuffs removed."}},
        {tags={"Oberon","Defiled Reckoning",},text={"*Oberon: Reckoning - Defiled Reckoning - Health Orbs created by kills from Reckoning are invisible/unusable to enemies for 10 seconds"}},
        {tags={"Overcharge Detectors",},text={"*Overcharge Detectors - Exposes enemies within 30 meters, who are at their maximum energy capacity"}},
        {tags={"Surplus Diverters",},text={"*Surplus Diverters - Gain 6 energy, upon replenishing shields completely without interruption after they have been deactivated"}},
        {tags={"Recuperate",},text={"*Recuperate - 20PERZENTO Chance to restore health on kill (Pistol)"}},
        {tags={"Calculated Victory",},text={"*Calculated Victory - 20PERZENTO Chance to restore shield on Kill (Pistol)"}},
        {tags={"Stand Ground",},text={"*Stand Ground - Blocking reduces damage taken from enemy abilities by 60PERZENTO"}},
        {tags={"Recover",},text={"*Recover - 20PERZENTO chance to restore health on kill (Rifle)"}},
        {tags={"Vanquished Prey",},text={"*Vanquished Prey - 20PERZENTO chance to restore shield on kill (Rifle)"}},
        {tags={"Momentary Pause",},text={"*Momentary Pause - 20PERZENTO chance to restore health on kill (Shotgun)"}},
        {tags={"Prize Kill",},text={"*Prize Kill - 20PERZENTO chance to restore shield on kill (Shotgun)"}},
        {tags={"Follow Through",},text={"*Follow Through - 6 energy gained on enemy kill (Frame)"}},
        {tags={"Quick Charge",},text={"*Quick Charge - -20PERZENTO shield recharge delay (Frame)"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'General Additions%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Audio from Warframe will no longer play when the game is minimized or not in use. This feature can be adjusted in the Options menu, under Audio."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Players can now disable audio communications from the Liset Scanner in the Options menu."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Custom Interior Liset colors can now be edited under Customize Liset the Options. This feature is still a work in progress, but Tenno can preview the customization today!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Rare Resource Blueprints have been added to the Market! Use these permanent Blueprints to craft rare resources using more commonly acquired drops."}},
        {tags={},text={"*New Syndicate forum avatars are now available in the Market!"}},
        {tags={"Valkyr",},text={"*Warframes can now use Warframe-specific dangles at the same time as other Syandanas. This new option is available under Auxiliary, in the Arsenal%'s Regalia. (Example: Valkyr%'s Bonds + Syandana)"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Loading Screen Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Player tips created by your Design Council are live on Loading Screens!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*New loading bar and player loading icons are live!"}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Slightly adjusted the hitboxes on Corpus Drones to help hit detection."}},
        {tags={"Heat Sword",},text={"*The Heat Sword has been given a tremendous visual overhaul!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*A performance and physics audit has occurred on levels and abilities to ensure all content is using the most optimized physics types."}},
        {tags={"Grakata",},text={"*Grakata has been given the PBR treatment!"}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Updated some textures on the Nemesis Nyx skin."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*The Friends List UI has been given an update to better show what friends are online, offline, or what chat requests are pending."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Reduced the amount of Warframes that will appear in Cutscenes in order to minimize performance issues."}},
        {tags={},text={"*An update to NPC behaviours when blinded has been made as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/454451-blind-behaviors/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*General performance improvements have been made across Warframe."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Excavation",},text={"*Fixed excavation drills in the Excavation game mode not properly taking damage from enemy attacks."}},
        {tags={"Excavation",},text={"*Fixed players not being able to see the health of excavators in the Excavation game mode."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Syndicate Syandanas showing at minimum brightness when previewed in the arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Fixed the countdown timer in Survival being replaced by the ‘Extraction countdown timer’ that triggers when enough players step into the Extraction zone, even when players step away from Extraction to continue the Survival Mission."}},
        {tags={"Invasion",},text={"*Fixed players not getting paid for Invasions if they were still playing the final Invasion Mission when the Invasion ends."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed error that would occur when using a mouse scroll wheel to browse through Dojo Architect options in the Architect menu."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed players being unable to build a Specter if their equipped loadout is under the conclave requirement for the Specter they want to build."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue causing projectile weapons to not properly register extra damage on headshots."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed inconsistent upper and lower case descriptions on Knockdown related Mods."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed ability timers getting reset when players pick up a Mod."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end

["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with Traditional and Simplified Chinese characters being swapped in chat."}},
},--fixes end
