["date"]={"May 6th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/453342-hotfix-1645-16451/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Sigils appearing 90 degrees over-rotated."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.4.5"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"May 6th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/453342-hotfix-1645/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Cestra",},text={"*Reduced the damage of all Cestra weapons in PvP"}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Vauban","Tonkor",},text={"*The Tonkor can no longer bounce other players."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Tonkor",},text={"*Fixed the Tonkor being able to damage and kill teammates."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a number of issues with Sigil customizations not saving."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where rotating the position of a sigil would default the color to grey."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being unable to get back up after being knocked down by enemies with knockdown abilities."}},
        {tags={"Syndicates",},text={"*Fixed negative standing with Syndicates showing as blue when it should be showing as red."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Forest","Relay",},text={"*<%s>Fixed ambient ‘static noise’ that could be heard on Grineer Forest and Relay tilesets.<%/s>Coming Soon."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed crits on enemy heads giving inconsistent crit numbers depending on what ‘part’ of the head you hit. Headshots on enemies will now properly do a 2x damage multiplier regardless of what spot has been hit."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed crits on Infested Ancients being inconsistent depending on which non-headshot part of the body was hit. Hitting vulnerable points on limbs will now properly do 3x damage, consistently."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some inappropriate body contortions when sliding while shooting a bow."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Penta","Tonkor",},text={"*Fixed the Tonkor and Penta not being affected by Volt’s Electric Shield as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/446728-tonkor-does-not-seem-to-be-affected-by-electric-shield-tonkor-still-glitched-edition/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Tonkor",},text={"*Fixed the Tonkor not properly knocking the user in the air when impacting an area close to a nearby enemy target."}},
        {tags={"Tonkor",},text={"*Fixed Tonkor grenades not exploding on contact with ragdolled enemies."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players respawning with their secondary weapon due to dying in a bleedout state, instead of spawning with their primary."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where Sugatras would not update their colors properly."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a rare crash that would occur on loading screens during texture loading."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed some issues relating improper visuals on Kubrow."}},
        {tags={"Kogake","Obex",},text={"*Fixed positioning of melee decorations on the Obex and Kogake."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Uru and Pyro Sugatras not properly appearing on some two hand weapons."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed hitboxes on Leech Osprey’s not properly registering player hits."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.4.4.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 28th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/449323-hotfix-1644-16441/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a crash that would occur on AMD CPUs on boot (caused by 16.4.4, fixed right now)."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.4.4"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 28th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/449323-hotfix-1644-16441/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Added a stagger to Nyx’s Mind Control and Chaos ability in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Excalibur’s Radial Blind now gets half its duration on targets that are looking away."}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"*Added vertical force to players killed by Rhino’s Charge."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players have 6 seconds respawn invulnerability with 3 seconds or time left invulnerability when moving."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Invulnerability is now removed if you fire a weapon, melee or use a power."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"%'%'%'Nova Antimatter Drop Changes and Fixes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"Quite a few adjustments have been made to Nova’s Antimatter Drop that deserve special mention beyond our normal ‘fixes’."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed projectiles level 1-3 having double shimmer visual effects.."}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"*Fixed Antimatter Drop’s collision detection being affected by projectile scale on Host. Increased the collision detection radius for Clients to help compensate."}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"*Fixed Client projectiles doing 2x damage on contact (not related to Antimatter Drop’s explosion damage)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed base explosion damage to match the Ability’s scaling explosion damage (was 100, now 100/125/150/200)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed sometimes doing minimal damage when used by Clients, regardless of how much players pumped into it."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed low damage as Host or Client if the projectile hits where you are aiming (as opposed to hitting something while trying to get to your aim position)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed aiming at the projectile not causing it to slow down."}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"*Explosions from Antimatter Drop will no longer fuel the damage of other nearby AMD projectiles."}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"*Projectiles from Antimatter Drop will now get brighter as you do damage to it."}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"*Various changes have been made to improve the visual and audio fx of Antimatter drop."}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"*Nova now shows a proper animation during multiple casts of Antimatter Drop."}},
},--misc end
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Several Liset Radio Chatter contest winners have been included into the Liset’s audio rotation. Blazingcobalt, jprocks67 and Kalimando all now have their voices featured in game! Keep an ear out for new transmissions coming straight from community members."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Slightly increased the Isolation Bursa’s ability cooldowns."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Rhino","Hind","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed damage from Rhino’s Charge lagging slightly behind Rhino, causing him to slide past enemies in Conclave without hurting them."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed an issue in Conclave where players would never lose their damage immunity on respawn when joining a match."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed energy respawners in Conclave not creating energy orbs after a Host migration."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed teams in Cephalon Capture occasionally grouping players into 5v3 matches."}},
        {tags={"Mars",},text={"*Fixed a Mission Node on Mars reading as “Deimos” instead of “Hellas”"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the positioning of the wings on Shield Ospreys to ensure that they can now be properly shot at."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage",},text={"*Fixed Host migrations on Corpus Sabotage missions preventing all other clients from completing the missions."}},
        {tags={"Syndicates",},text={"*Fixed maxed out negative reputation with Syndicates showing as 0 reputation lost or gained when handing in Syndicate Tokens."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed locations on MOA enemies that were previously difficult to hit, including a small location between the ‘head’ and gun, and a spot on their MOA-butt that was tough to shoot."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Syndicate Assassins dropping blueprints that give more Spectres than intended."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Quanta",},text={"*Fixed Volt’s Electric Shield giving improper damage multipliers as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/446810-volt-shield-quanta-vandel-absurd-damage/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Corpus Ice Planet",},text={"*Fixed the distance that loot crates would visually appear being too short on Corpus Ice Planet tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a potential issue causing a rare deadlock on launch."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Muzzle Flash",},text={"*Fixed visual issue with muzzle flashes on weapons not properly following weapon barrels."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.4.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 24th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/446817-hotfix-1643-credit-reimbursement-2/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Credit Reimbursement%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"Credit Reimbursement #2 is complete. All affected Tenno will have an inbox message informing them of their reimbursement of Credits."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Event Fixes / Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Major Fix%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"%'%'%'Fixed players not receiving any credits / Operation completion if they participate in a Bursa MOA hack in a party with a player that had no Void Offerings in their inventory. Players affected by this will be reimbursed appropriate to Credits lost through this error.%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"Event Scoring will now allow for the level of the Bursa to act as a multiplier for your score. The higher level the Bursa, the greater your score."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Bursa MOA reinforcements not properly spawning to defend the Bursa MOA, and adjusted the levels of the enemy reinforcements."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Bursa MOA’s radial blind cooldown has been increased from 16s to 30s."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Bursa MOA’s radial blind range has been decreased from 90m to 15m."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Updated various audio effects on Bursa MOA’s attacks."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Sybaris",},text={"*Increased the damage of the Sybaris in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Made terrain adjustments to help make wall running smoother on The Lost Halls Conclave Map."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Cestra","Dual Cestra",},text={"*Made various audio adjustments to the Cestra and Dual Cestra."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Oberon",},text={"*Fixed Oberon’s Reckoning not lifting enemies when cast."}},
        {tags={"Tonkor","Terminal Velocity",},text={"*Fixed the Terminal Velocity Mod not being equippable on the Tonkor as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/446419-terminal-velocity-unequipable-for-tonkor/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players spawning in unsafe locations when using the /unstuck command."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Uranus",},text={"*Fixed Uranus missing an Archwing Mission node."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Capture",},text={"*Fixed an issue where players would load with the wrong items when playing Archwing after having finished the Zanuka capture mission."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed a potential crash that would occur in Conclave during a host migration."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed Spy missions giving out rewards randomly, instead of giving out rewards based on how many vaults have been cracked."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Tonkor","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Mirage’s Clones being able to hit Mirage with Tonkor shots."}},
        {tags={"Explosive Demise",},text={"*Fixed the Explosive Demise Mod harming the player that has it equipped."}},
        {tags={"Parry",},text={"*Fixed players getting stuck in a knockdown state after being hit by a knockdown attack just after a parry."}},
        {tags={"Quanta Vandal","Vandal","Dex",},text={"*Fixed the Quanta Vandal not appearing in the player Codex."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.4.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 24th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/446244-1642/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Event Fixes%'%'%' "}},
        {tags={"Void","Retrieve",},text={"*Fixed an issue that allowed you to use your Void Offerings via the Gear Menu when you were close to a Bursa. The intended way for your offering to be retrieved was via hacking the Bursa’s back panel, not by using the Gear menu. This is what lead to the majority of score-related & credit consumption bugs. Now that it is fixed, we will begin working on the reimbursement script."}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Stalk",},text={"*Fixed an issue where Stalker-like enemies could make the Event mission break."}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Reimbursement Script Details%'%'%' "}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"*The intent of the event was to only ever allow you risk 1 (one) Void Offering."}},
        {tags={},text={"*The bug with the Gear menu allowed you to spend >1."}},
        {tags={},text={"*We will be auditing for all times when >1 Offering was claimed in any denomination (1000, 10000, 100000) and returning it to you account."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"This script will take some time to write, but we will let you know when it%'s begun and when it%'s complete. "}},
},--misc end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Prova","Clan",},text={"*Fixed Clan emblems not being activated even though they had been approved. All clan emblems that should have been active (you received approval confirmation) will now be available in game! If you are still missing yours, let us know in this thread!"}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.4.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 23rd, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/446172-hotfix-1641-false-profits-psa/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'False Profits PSA%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"The first few hours of this event have led to immediate troubleshooting. On Prime Time we experienced one of the more commonly reported bugs, and are working on reproducing it for the next Hotfix (which will be 16.4.2)."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"-Currently there are other issues with the event that can lead to the following scenarios:"}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"-Host only getting scores (witnessed on Prime Time)."}},
        {tags={},text={"-UI ‘Transfer’ Indicators only working for Hosts."}},
        {tags={},text={"-Permanent Knockdown for Clients"}},
        {tags={},text={"-Offerings being consumed in quantities greater than 1 (we are working on reimbursement script for this)"}},
        {tags={"Lotus",},text={"-Errors in commands given by Lotus vs actions (killing Bursa when asked leading to failure)."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"If you have concise reproduction steps for an issue, please list them here in this thread."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"If you have an EE.log file that would help with a mission error (found in /PERZENTOlocalappdataPERZENTO/Warframe), please send it to support.warframe.com and post your ticket number in this thread. There is a category on the support desk for logs requested from us here in the forums. You can find it in “Logs and Metadata”, then “Files/Logs requested in Forums”."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Quanta Vandal","Vandal",},text={"As we monitor the attempts run on this event, we do see people are earning their Quanta Vandals already."}},
        {tags={},text={"We are looking to ensure all the possible cases that lead to progression stoppers are resolved - your frustration is our frustration and we’ll fix things as soon as we can."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"Please note that we made Clan scores larger by removing the divisor that was being applied. Positions are still the same, there are just bigger numbers now."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Anything nonconstructive will be removed as per our forum rules and guidelines."}},
},--misc end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Amprex",},text={"* Fixed Amprex not chaining to nearby enemies."}},
        {tags={"Prime","Ammo Stock",},text={"*Fixed Ammo Stock mod incorrectly appearing as its Prime counterpart across various UI screens."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*Fixed players unable to claim Arcane Enhancements from the foundry."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Design Council window flashing incessantly."}},
        {tags={"Trial","Retribution",},text={"*Fixed a particle effect that could cause performance issues during the Shipyards phase of the Law of Retribution Trial."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed missing graphic for Vex Armour augment."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed unreleased weapons appearing in Codex."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed placeholder text appearing in Game Invite panel."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed localization bugs."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various gameplay crashes."}},
        {tags={"Braton Vandal","Vandal",},text={"*Tweaked volume of Braton Vandal."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.4"},
["date"]={"April 23rd, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/445957-update-164/"},
["title"]={"Tenno Reinforcement: Tonkor"},
["image"]={"Tenno Reinforcements - Tonkor.png"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'TENNO REINFORCEMENT%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Tonkor",},text={"%[%[Tonkor%]%] Grenade Launcher available now!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Tonkor",},text={"Hurl mayhem and destruction at the enemy with the Tonkor! Grenades from this Grineer launcher explode on impact. Use their blasts to propel yourself to new heights!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"%'%'%'New Kubrow Mod%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Shelter",},text={"%[%[Shelter%]%]: Provides a shield when the owner is reviving an ally."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Augment Mods%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Nekros","Shield of Shadows",},text={"*Nekros - Shadows of the Dead - %[%[Shield of Shadows%]%]: Damage done to Nekros is shared with his Shadows."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Vexing Retaliation",},text={"*Chroma - Vex Armor - %[%[Vexing Retaliation%]%]: Taking a PERZENTO amount of damage will trigger a PERZENTO radius burst. Shield damage will cause a puncture proc while health damage will cause a blast proc."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'New Conclave Mods%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"The following mods are only usable in Conclave:"}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Singularity",},text={"*Nyx - Absorb - Singularity: Creates a ring every 3 seconds that drags in enemies at 9 meters per second."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Gradual Change: Diminishing Returns vs Pure Ability Immunity%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Vor","Lephantis","Grustrag Three",},text={"For those unfamiliar with the term, diminishing returns is when an ability become less and less effective against an enemy over time. When we original designed bosses and minibosses, a lot of them had pure ability immunity making your Warframe’s abilities ineffective in battle. We’ve slowly been experimenting with ‘diminishing returns’. Many bosses and minibosses in Warframe were affected by diminishing returns, including Captain Vor, Lephantis, the Grustrag Three, and more. Instead of being outright immune to crowd control these bosses will gain improved resistance to them with each successive use."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"This Update expands the number of Warframe powers that fall under diminishing returns to the new Bursas. With this change we will monitor power use across each Warframe to ensure that every form of crowd control is given fair play against enemies, whether the Ability is timer-based or relies on energy drain."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Alt Helmets!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Mesa",},text={"The %[%[Chroma Amaru Helmet%]%] and %[%[Ovis Mesa Helmet%]%] are now available in the marketplace"}},
},--misc end
["additions"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Using a waypoint marker in a Squad will now show numbers corresponding to each member of the Squad."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Squad HUD information now shows a corresponding number to each player to identify the Host as well as make waypoint markers easier to correlate."}},
        {tags={},text={"*New sound effects for hacking has been added!"}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"*A ‘default colors’ button has been added to each Arsenal customization colour (instead of a full restore to default), returning a weapon, Warframe or accessory back to its default colors. This will allow your Prime Attachments to shine in the default gold we showed them with. This will fix issues issues with energy colours not showing well when you use abilities."}},
        {tags={"Rescue","Spy",},text={"*Syndicate Allies can now be given ‘Hold Position’ and ‘Follow’ commands and can be issued with a context action (similar to giving Rescue targets your Secondary). This can be done at mission start as an option for players that wish to progress in a mission alone or coordinate the movement of their Syndicate Allies. Syndicate Allies have been re-enabled in Rescue and Spy Missions in case some users want them, but if not you can have them ‘Hold Position’ at spawn and proceed alone."}},
        {tags={},text={"*New Grineer Portrait Icons are available for purchase in the Marketplace!"}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Vor","Defense","Quest",},text={"*Increased the credit reward and available Mods in Vor’s Prize Defense Mission Quest."}},
        {tags={"Defense",},text={"*Players AFK during Defense Missions that fail to choose ‘Continue’ between rounds will be defaulted to ‘Extract’."}},
        {tags={"Kunai",},text={"*Slightly improved hit effects to Kunai impact visual."}},
        {tags={"Secura Dual Cestra",},text={"*Increase the visual effect of the Secura Dual Cestra’s shots."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*The limit on DNA Detoxifiers that can be applied to Kubrow on a given day has been removed."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various PBR updates to Grineer structures."}},
        {tags={"Raptors",},text={"*The Raptor will now drop Neural Sensors instead of Orokin Cells."}},
        {tags={"Vor","Quest",},text={"*Rebalanced enemy difficulty in the Vor’s Prize Quest."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Outpost",},text={"*Removed some of the larger connecting rooms from the Corpus Outpost tutorial tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Caches discovered in tutorial missions will now provide 500 Affinity."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Default color will now be kept with backing out of a color in the color picker."}},
        {tags={"Braton Vandal","Vandal",},text={"*Updated audio FX on the Braton Vandal."}},
        {tags={"Dual Raza",},text={"*Reduced the audio effects of the Dual Raza’s blade sounds."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Gas City","Corpus Ice Planet","Clan",},text={"*Made various reverb tweaks to the Dojo, Corpus Gas City and Corpus Ice Planet tileset."}},
        {tags={"EMP Aura",},text={"*The ‘unreleased’ EMP Aura Mod is no longer tradeable."}},
        {tags={"The Sergeant","Nef Anyo","Reflect",},text={"*Nef Anyo has been renamed The Sergeant and all VO elements of his encounter have been updated to reflect the change."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various localization changes."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*New Conclave Map: The Lost Halls."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Prime",},text={"*Nyx and Nyx Prime are now usable in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Braton Vandal","Vandal",},text={"*The Braton Vandal is now usable in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"*Added an audio cue when 4 total captures have taken place across the match, reminding players that all energy dispenser locations now give 50 energy."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Energy dispensers will now produce orbs of 50 energy at 5 minutes remaining in the match.."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Maps will now shuffle in PvP, giving more of an unpredictable variety."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased the respawn time for Energy Orbs from 25 to 45 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Lex",},text={"*Decreased the Lex’s damage in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Decreased the damage of Mag’s Pull in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Bo",},text={"*Increased Stamina consumption when blocking with all Bo weapons in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Bo",},text={"*Reduced the damage of all Bo weapons in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Gram",},text={"*Increase Stamina drain when blocking with the Gram in PvP."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased the Energy cost of channeled melee hits from 5 to 10."}},
        {tags={"Boar","Strun",},text={"*Increased the damage of all Strun and Boar weapons in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Dex Dakra","Dex",},text={"*Reduced the damage of the Dex Dakra in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Volt’s Electric Shield is now more visible when used with dark/black energy."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Melee weapons no longer get a headshot bonus in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Karak","Sybaris","Tigris","Angstrum","Kraken","Viper","Angst","Wraith",},text={"*The following weapons have now been balanced for use in PvP: Sybaris, Karak, Kraken, Viper, Angstrum, AKViper, WraithViper and Tigris."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Martial Magnetism Changes:"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Extra stamina cost is now applied to missed swings"}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"**Magnetism is not applied to a target if you do not have the full amount of Stamina required to attack"}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed the Kubrow using the wrong finisher on enemies."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed the Kubrow’s finisher animation on the Drahk Master making the victim immune to outside damage for the duration of the animation."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Fixed issue that would occur when using Mirage’s Eclipse that would prevent the player from using this Ability, or other powers, again."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed ugly textures that would display on some Grineer doors."}},
        {tags={"Nekros","Vor","Soul Survivor",},text={"*Fixed Nekros’ Soul Survivor Augment not properly tracking revives or counting towards revive challenges as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/443040-nekros-soul-survivor-does-not-count-as-a-revive/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed issue that would cause player’s map to break when trying to type during Ash’s Bladestorm animation."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a possible issue that caused windowed versions of Warframe to show a blank screen when the game is restarted after a crash."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players losing duplicate Arcane Helmets after trading one of the helmets."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue that would occur during an enemy ragdoll, causing a crash."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some instances where terrain would inappropriately meld into another room."}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris",},text={"*Fixed placement of hints during Simaris’ Scanning tutorial."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Hazards",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Effigy moving when in electric water or similar environmental hazards."}},
        {tags={"Clan","Reach",},text={"*Fixed the Dojo Polychrome Bot being unable to be removed after placement, or potentially being able to be placed in an area that cannot be reached."}},
        {tags={"Rush","Clan","Rush",},text={"*Fixed an issue that would not properly refund Platinum to the Clan Vault if a room painted via Polychrome that was rushed is destroyed."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue that would cause knee-slide audio done by Clients to play for the Host anywhere on the map."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some instances of AMD driver crashes that would occur when minimizing Warframe."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed an issue that would occur when timing out in the Dojo, causing the game to crash."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed an issue that would show Kubrows and Sentinels as wearing two badges."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Reflect",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Longhorn helmet not properly reflecting energy color."}},
        {tags={"Retribution",},text={"*Fixed AI pathing so that enemies no longer get stuck on pillars in the second phase of the Law of Retribution."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Galleon",},text={"*Fixed the minimap occasionally breaking in some rooms on the Grineer Galleon tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players having Primary and Secondary weapons unequipped after completing the tutorial."}},
        {tags={"Vor","Darvo",},text={"*Fixed Darvo’s VO not playing in the second mission in Vor’s Prize."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where weapon skins were not displaying properly on login."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed audio effects not properly going off when players capture a Cephalon in Cephalon Capture."}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"*Fixed some broken water physics that would occur in the Orokin Void tileset."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage",},text={"*Fixed an issue that would prevent Clients from being able to complete a Corpus Sabotage Mission after a Host Migration."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Arcane HUD indicators from showing as though they were debuffs from a Dragon Key."}},
        {tags={"Sargas Ruk",},text={"*Fixed a crash that would occur when fighting Sargas Ruk in melee."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Clients that get knocked down appearing as though they are sliding to Hosts and other Clients."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Fixed error that would allow Punch Through Mods to create unlimited multishot when a weapon is fired through Volt’s Electric Shield."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Ice Planet",},text={"*Fixed players being able to get stuck under elevators on the Corpus Ice Planet."}},
        {tags={"MK1-Furis",},text={"*Fixed MK1-Furis showing a price of 120 Platinum in the Market."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed players showing as having default skins and colors when in a Relay."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed issue where Clan Emblems would not show up until after logging in."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Uru and Pyro Sugatras not appearing properly on some weapons."}},
        {tags={"Lephantis",},text={"*Fixed an issue that caused Lephantis to become invisible and immune to damage."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Earth",},text={"*Fixed some water clipping through textures on Earth Defense tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Fixed a typo in the Patient Zero Quest line."}},
        {tags={"Gram","Defense","Dex",},text={"*Fixed some grammatical errors in the Codex description of Defense Missions."}},
        {tags={"Lephantis",},text={"*Fixed localization descriptions on Lephantis Nav Coordinates, they now refer to Lephantis instead of Golem."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed default colors on a Warframe not displaying properly in the Codex diorama."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed Kubrows who had their appearances altered in a previous Update."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage","Void",},text={"*Fixed Clients being unable to pick up Void Key in Void Sabotage Missions after killing the miniboss."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed held melee attacks being usable in mid-air, causing a ‘gliding slam’ onto an area. Slam attacks will now drop players directly to the ground."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.3.5"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 17th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/442724-hotfix-1635/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'AMD Fixes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Orokin Catalyst",},text={"With the generous assistance of several engineers from AMD we have implemented a possible workaround for a nasty crash that could occur when running multi-threaded DirectX 10 or 11 on the latest Catalyst 14.12 drivers. We are optimistic that this workaround will not only avoid the crash but may also eliminate other related problems like black-textures and other cases of memory-corruption."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Orokin Catalyst",},text={"More details on the crash can be found %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/433642-crashes-on-amd-catalyst-1412-omega-update/ here%]"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Orokin Catalyst","Rage",},text={"At this time we would encourage all players using AMD graphics cards to update to the latest Catalyst Omega 14.12 drivers and re-enable DirectX 11 in the launcher settings. We will be analyzing crashes reported over the weekend to confirm that this problem is indeed fixed and so as always we would appreciate it if you could submit all crashes to us."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Sicarus",},text={"*Increased the damage of Sicarus weapons in PvP."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed secondary weapons floating away from Chroma (for real this time)."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Nekros","Archwing","Itzal",},text={"*Fixed kills from Chroma’s Effigy, Nekros’ Shadows of the Dead and the Itzal Archwing’s Fighter Escort not counting towards player stats."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.3.4"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 16th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/442256-hotfix-1634/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'AMD Driver Issues:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"In response to continued crashes on AMD drivers we’re rolling back our Hotfix change that enabled our Launcher to automatically adjust Driver versions from DirectX 10 or 11. We apologize for the inconvenience and are working hard to get a permanent solution to this issue."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Orokin Catalyst",},text={"More information, including future status updates, can be found %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/433642-crashes-on-amd-catalyst-1412-omega-update/ here%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"|fixes= "}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed the Temple of Honor not appearing on the player map in Dojos."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed missing wall textures in Cephalon Capture Orokin level."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Ship",},text={"*Fixed a lack of collision on some wall textures on Corpus ship tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Assassination","Grineer Shipyard",},text={"*Fixed a door texture on Assassination Missions in Grineer Shipyards that had missing textures."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Ballistica","Rakta Ballistica",},text={"*Fixed the Rakta Ballistica not sitting on Chroma when holstered."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed Clan ranks not being properly localized."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed various broken textures in Archwing Missions."}},
        {tags={"Gram",},text={"*Fixed 7 day Affinity Booster not working correctly for accounts under the referral program."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Vauban","Perpetual Vortex",},text={"*Fixed Vauban’s Perpetual Vortex Mod no longer pulling in enemies after the initial Vortex time has ended."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Muzzle Flash",},text={"*Fixed muzzle flash not showing properly on weapons as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/397557-muzzle-flash-not-showing-on-weapons/page-2 this thread%]"}},
},--misc end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.3.3.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 15th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/441719-hotfix-1633-16331/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an unreleased cosmetic appearing in the market."}},
        {tags={"Braton",},text={"*Fixed MK1 Braton not being able to use skins."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.3.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 13th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/441719-hotfix-1633-16331/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Orokin Catalyst",},text={"If you are an AMD user who has experienced crashing, please note we have taken steps to ensure the game will run if we detect a driver version that is known to cause crashes. More information can be found here: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/topic/433642-crashes-on-amd-catalyst-1412-omega-update/?tab=comments#comment-4901825 forum post%]"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Augment Mods%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Vauban","Perpetual Vortex",},text={"*Vauban - %[%[Perpetual Vortex%]%]: Vortex%'s duration is increased by PERZENTO for each additional Vortex thrown into the initial Vortex."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Zephyr","Funnel Clouds",},text={"*Zephyr - %[%[Funnel Clouds%]%]: Tornado generates an additional X tornadoes, but tornadoes have reduced size and do not pull enemies."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Excalibur - %[%[Radial Finish%]%]: Blinded enemies are vulnerable to finishers, taking increased finisher damage."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"(Note: In reference to discussed Future changes to Excalibur in Devstream 50, we may adjust of some of his Augment Mods in the future)"}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Corpus Crewmen Enemies will sit through their death animations for longer, rather than cutting straight to ragdoll / fade away. This refers to %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/437046-coming-soon-devstream-50/?p=4843421 this particular question%]."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various network changes to help reduce latency in users that see high ping spikes."}},
        {tags={"Nikana",},text={"*Updated the Gemini Nikana Sheathe bundle to require Mastery Rank 4 to help prevent players from purchasing the item without owning a Nikana."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Inviting someone to a Squad that fails to join will now give a more specific message instead of ‘player has declined your invitation’."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed issue in Conclave where a Host’s group was not being cleared if players left his Squad in the lobby."}},
        {tags={"Venus",},text={"*Fixed issues with lighting and colour effects on certain Venus maps that could result in a very flat and desaturated look (as per screenshot)."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed a crash that would occur in the Relays as players joined and left."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various gameplay crashes."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.3.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 14th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/440564-hotfix-1632/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Soma","Soma Prime","Prime",},text={"*Slightly reduced the damage of Soma and Soma Prime in PvP."}},
},--misc end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed various issues causing Host and Client hangups on the loading screen in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Limbo",},text={"*Fixed an issue that would occur when using Ash’s Blade Storm when inside Limbo’s Rift."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the End of Mission ‘Summary’ screen showing an incorrect number of Mods obtained as per %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/438881-you-only-get-50-of-the-mods-collected-during-a-mission-ui-bug/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris","Sanctuary",},text={"*Fixed Simaris’ dialogue appearing twice when making a purchase in his Sanctuary."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed an issue where the HUD would freeze after using an Ability Power."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where some Upgrades were not properly showing stat changes after ranking up."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed instances of the Manic getting himself stuck in open areas."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Nekros",},text={"*Fixed Nullifier Crewmen not nullifying Chroma’s Effigy or Nekros’ Shadows of the Dead summons."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue in Archwing missions causing Missions to lock up mid-combat."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Fixed MOAs still being spawnable in the Blackout Tactical Alert."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.3.1.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 10th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/438351-hotfix-1631-16311-tactical-alert/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some issues with many in-game chat commands not functioning (/inv, /w, etc)."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed issue that caused Excalibur’s Radial Javelin to not kill enemies."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed Archwing components not showing up in the player inventory after crafting was complete."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.3.1.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 10th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/438351-hotfix-1631-tactical-alert/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Players can no longer summon friendly MOAs in the Blackout Tactical Alert."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Exterminate","Alert",},text={"*Fixed the Blackout Tactical Alert switching to Exterminate mid-mission."}},
        {tags={"Alert","Lotus",},text={"**To clarify, this Fix addresses the chance that every Mission normally has for the Lotus to change the Mission Objective. Previously it was possible for the Mission to be altered, thereby causing problems in the event, as per %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/438474-tactical-alert-mission-objective-change/?p=4863180#entry4863180 this thread%]"}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.3.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 10th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/438351-hotfix-1631-tactical-alert/"},
["title"]={"Tactical Alert: Blackout"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"%'%'%'Tactical Alert: Blackout%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Tenno,"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"My contact has informed me of a convoy of abandoned merchant vessels drifting in dead space. Seems like such a waste of good product to just leave it floating there when it could be on my shelves instead."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Reactivate the navigation consoles, and I can reroute them to a... secure location. After I take down the inventory of each ship, we can share the dividends, minus my usual finder’s fee of course."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Darvo",},text={"-Darvo"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Darvo",},text={"Help Darvo between 3:30 p.m. EDT and Wednesday, April 15 at 2 p.m. EDT and you’ll be rewarded with the following:"}},
        {tags={"Orokin Catalyst",},text={"*Complete Stage 1: Orokin Catalyst Blueprint"}},
        {tags={"Orokin Reactor",},text={"*Complete Stage 2: Orokin Reactor Blueprint"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Complete Stage 3: Stratos Emblem"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Soma Prime","Prime",},text={"*Soma Prime is now usable in PvP."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Reward descriptions have been added to the World State Window."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Added red lights to vent entrances on Spy Vaults to help make them stand out better."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various lighting updates."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Helios","Cephalon Simaris",},text={"*Fixed Helios’ Simaris skin using the wrong energy color."}},
        {tags={"Frost","Mag","Prime",},text={"*Fixed emblems not sitting properly on Frost, Frost Prime and Mag Prime."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some instances of player names being rejected due to ‘invalid characters’."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed Ash getting stuck in Bladestorm animations when used on Zanuka."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed Cephalon’s duplicating in Cephalon Capture after a Host Migration."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed issues in Spy Vault that would occur when G3 spawns."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed security cameras in Corpus Spy Vaults seeing players through some windows."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Galleon",},text={"*Fixed visual issues on exploding Grineer Galleon featured in tutorial missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed space skybox showing on some planetary tilesets."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a ‘black portal’ to a dead end room in the Corpus Gas Cities."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Spy",},text={"*Fixed Grineer Forcefields and Spy vault laser traps affecting Limbo while in Rift Walk - he should be immune to these traps while in the Rift."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed issues with some Archwing missions being unplayable if a full loadout wasn’t setup."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Zanuka/Harvester’s pin attack not working properly."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Frost",},text={"*Fixed a game freeze that could occur when using Excalibur’s Radial Javelin on ragdolled enemies."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed a visual issue where carried Cephalons turn into power cores after a Host Migration in Conclave."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.3"},
["date"]={"April 9th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/437166-update-163-the-new-protocols/"},
["title"]={"The New Protocols"},
["image"]={"Tenno Reinforcements - Aksomati & Dual Raza.png"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'TENNO REINFORCEMENTS%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Soma",},text={"New Soma-inspired weapons available now!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Soma","Aksomati","Dual Raza","Assault",},text={"Tenno, complement your Soma with the power of the Aksomati pistols and the razor-sharp Dual Raza hatchets. Get them separately or purchase them with the Soma assault rifle in a discounted bundle!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Soma","Aksomati",},text={"%'%'%'%[%[Aksomati|AKSOMATI%]%]%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Soma",},text={"The devastating power of the Soma rifle compacted into two elegant pistols."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Dual Raza",},text={"%'%'%'%[%[Dual Raza|DUAL RAZA%]%]%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Soma",},text={"These razor-sharp battle hatchets are designed to complement the Soma."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Soma",},text={"%'%'%'SUPREME SOMA SET%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Soma","Aksomati","Dual Raza","Assault",},text={"This bundle features the Soma assault rifle, Dual Raza hatchets and Aksomati pistols. Sharing a unique style, the weapons in this set will inspire awe in your allies and fear in your foes."}},
        {tags={},text={"Get these new in-game additions by visiting the Market today!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"%'%'%'Spy Expansion%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Tenno,"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Rage",},text={"Our scouts have returned from investigating Corpus and Grineer ships. They have discovered key details on new systemic overhauls to their data storage facilities."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Ivara","Infiltrate",},text={"Reports filed from our surviving agents show many of the classified vaults we have infiltrated have been relocated or given an extensive retrofit. Many have been upgraded to more advanced security systems. These new vaults were designed to counter forced entry. Increased patrols and movement-sensitive alarms will make infiltration difficult."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Use caution when accessing these secure data vaults. We must show our enemies there is no obscurity the Tenno cannot uncover."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Lotus",},text={"-Lotus"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*3 new Spy Vaults have been added to Corpus Spy Missions"}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*3 new Spy Vaults have been added to Grineer Spy Missions"}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Players will now see a greater variation in what Spy Vaults appear in Missions, with each tileset randomly generated from existing vaults."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Each Vault (old or new) contains randomly altered features that will make replaying a familiar vault less predictable but still open to multiple solutions."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"%[%[File:U16.3 Spy Expansion.png|center%]%]"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Augment Mods%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Ember","Firequake",},text={"%'%'%'Ember%'%'%' - World on Fire - %[%[Firequake%]%]: Enemies hit have a chance of being knocked down."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Explosive Legerdemain","Rage",},text={"%'%'%'Mirage%'%'%' - Slight of Hand - %[%[Explosive Legerdemain%]%]: Pickups are turned into proximity mines that deal damage with proc chance."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Trinity","Vampire Leech",},text={"%'%'%'Trinity%'%'%' - Energy Vampire - %[%[Vampire Leech%]%]: Excess energy replenishes shields."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Helmets%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%[%[File:U16.3 New Helmets.png|center%]%]"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Mag","Mirage","Rage",},text={"Mirage’s unique %[%[Trivelin Mirage Helmet|Trivelin Helmet%]%] and Limbo’s stylish %[%[Magrite Limbo Helmet|Magrite Helmet%]%] will give your Warframe a new look. Both helmets are available in the Market today!"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Cephalons in Cephalon Capture will now return to their spawn after 10 seconds after being dropped."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Made various localization updates to Conclave announcement messages."}},
        {tags={"Braton",},text={"*Increased the damage of all Braton weapons in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Gorgon",},text={"*Increased the damage of the Gorgon in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Magnus",},text={"*Increased the damage of all Magnus weapons in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Furax",},text={"*Increased the damage of the Furax in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Gram",},text={"*Increased the damage of the Gram in PvP."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Squads in PvP will only be shuffled for players who joined a match alone. Players who joined a queue together will remain together from match to match."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Reduced the radius of knockdown effects from slide attacks in PvP."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players can now view match progress while spectating in PvP by entering the pause menu."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made audio ambiance tweaks to all maps."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Increased the Energy drain of the Martial Magnetism in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Soma","Aksomati","Dual Raza","Conclave",},text={"*The Aksomati and Dual Raza are available for use in Conclave!"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Grineer Manic Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Regen",},text={"*Adjusted Grineer Manic to regen 2PERZENTO of health a second, after a 7 second delay."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Slightly lowered the damage of claw attack and the Manic’s Slash proc chance of his attacks."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Increased the amount of decorations that can be placed in the Liset to 40."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added ‘Invite Squad Member’ label to the Squad UI."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Notifications for events shown in the Liset’s Navigation console will now update their time remaining at 24h, 12h, 1h, 30min, 15min, 5m and 0s."}},
        {tags={"Spy","Quest",},text={"*Lowered the difficulty of the Spy Missions in the Stolen Dreams Quest."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Hackable consoles can no longer be used by Limbo while in the Rift."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Corpus security cameras now only activate laser grids within 20m of their position."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Sentinel","Spy",},text={"*Kubrow and Sentinels will no longer set off laser alarms in the Spy game mode."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Life Support in Survival no longer spawn from crates or lockers before the Life Support drain begins or after the Life Support drain ends."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various adjustments to the /unstuck command’s parameters."}},
        {tags={"Spy","Grineer Shipyard",},text={"*Reduced the number of co-op hack doors on Spy Missions on the Grineer Shipyards tileset."}},
        {tags={"Spy","Grineer Asteroid",},text={"*Reduced the amount of backtracking necessary in Spy Missions on the Grineer Asteroid tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Reduced the radius of knockdown effects from slide attacks in PvE."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Added small lights to consoles in Corpus Spy Vaults."}},
        {tags={"Ember",},text={"*Adjusted Ember’s Accelerant effects to show its range more accurately."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Emblems can now be used on either shoulder."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Made small adjustments to Chroma’s Effigy to be less intrusive."}},
        {tags={"Kohm",},text={"*Made small adjustments to Kohm audio and visual effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various audio adjustments for surround sound and stereo."}},
        {tags={"Darvo",},text={"*Made audio adjustments to Darvo and Grineer Manic."}},
        {tags={"Soma","Soma Prime","Prime",},text={"*Updated audio on Soma and Soma Prime."}},
        {tags={"Research","Cephalon Simaris","Sanctuary",},text={"*Research displayed in Simaris’ Sanctuary will now show the top 5 contributors."}},
        {tags={"Spy","Reach",},text={"*Made adjustments to Rare Spawn scanning targets to prevent them from spawning in difficult to reach places such as Spy Vaults, Extraction or near turrets."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Sigils dropped from bosses no longer consume the boss resource drops, and will be given in addition to the bosses resource."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where players with high latency could turn the UI invisible, preventing them from being able to select Missions."}},
        {tags={"Survival","Void",},text={"*Fixed rare and ultra-rare loot crates using the regular drop table in Survival and Void Survival."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed kill messages not displaying properly in Cephalon Capture for Clients."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed players being unable to navigate the End of Mission tabs in Conclave when using a gamepad or controller."}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"*Fixed visual effects not appearing on the Edo Prime chest armor."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed an error occurring at the end of Conclave matches that was preventing the End of Mission results screen from loading."}},
        {tags={"Reflect",},text={"*Fixed players not getting their Friends List updated to reflect a Friend having completed the Mission they were in."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a graphical issue on some transparent surfaces as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/433651-graphics-bug-black-dots-on-some-transparent-surfaces-update/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Fixed various issues with AI pathing in Orokin Survival."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr","Grineer Forest",},text={"*Fixed an issue on Grineer Forest tilesets on where players using Zephyr can float above the level using Tail Wind, but then become unable to get back to the ground."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed an issue in Cephalon Capture where players would be unable to join a session in-progress that’s gone through two Host Migrations."}},
        {tags={"Rage",},text={"*Fixed lighting issue in elevator connector room on Corpus Power Storage tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Loki",},text={"*Fixed issue that would cause Loki’s Invisibility texture to fill the entire screen as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/435669-loki-invisibility-texture-filling-entire-screen-glitch/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris",},text={"*Fixed an issue where affinity earned from Cephalon Simaris scans was not visible on the End of Mission screen."}},
        {tags={"Simulacrum",},text={"*Fixed a crash that would occur when using the /unstuck command in non-Mission levels as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/433555-bug-unstuck-command-related-crash-in-simulacrum/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed the Archwing loadout section of the Arsenal appearing for players who have no Archwings."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue that was causing Mods to not drop properly from enemies."}},
        {tags={"Kogake",},text={"*Fixed the Kogake’s slide attack sweeping from the left hand instead of the left foot. Also adjusted the timing to better match the animation."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed issue where using Chroma in a Mastery Test would cause enemies to stop spawning."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed Mastery Rank Sigils not displaying properly in Relays."}},
        {tags={"Void","Derelict",},text={"*Fixed broken Navigation markers not working properly in Void Derelict hallways."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed Proto-Excalibur helmet not working with other skins."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed players who purchased the Proto-Excalibur skin not receiving the Proto-Excalibur helmet."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue in the Grineer Manic behavior that would prevent the Manic from catching up to fleeing players as easily."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Fixed the Martial Magnetism Mod not functioning properly."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed clients being able to ignore the Grineer Manic’s cloak ability."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the announcement for a player joining a Squad displaying for the player who is joining the session."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture","Recover",},text={"*Fixed the Cephalon in Cephalon Capture being unrecoverable after a Host Migration."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed players being unable to capture the Cephalon in Cephalon Capture after a Host Migration."}},
        {tags={"Dragon Nikana","Nikana",},text={"*Fixed attachments to the Dragon Nikana’s sheathe not displaying properly."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed visual bug that would occur in turret animation in Corpus Spy Vaults."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed enemies being able to easily see players in the upper catwalks of some Corpus Spy Vaults."}},
        {tags={"Machete",},text={"*Fixed the Grineer Scorpion Machete attack not hitting stationary players all of the time."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed emblems appearing on shoulder rotated incorrectly."}},
        {tags={"Sanctuary",},text={"*Fixed an issue where players using their 4th Warframe Ability during a Mastery Rank Test in Sanctuary would stop spawning enemies, making it impossible to complete the Mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some issues with players scrolling up and down through items in the Inventory."}},
        {tags={"Soma",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Soma where the weapon clip would sit in the wrong position."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Prime","Rage",},text={"*Fixed visual issue with the Edo Prime Chest Piece when equipped to a Mirage using Eclipse."}},
        {tags={"Quest","Clan",},text={"*Fixed players in the Tutorial level being able to use the Friends or Clan member list to join another player’s session, effectively breaking the tutorial and preventing the player from being able to use any Warframes, weapons or Quests."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed Host Migration in Conclave opening the team selection screen."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed players being unable to swap the polarity of their ‘Aura’ slot for Sentinels."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being unable to see items owned in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed players still showing on the enemy team when switching sides in Cephalon Capture."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed player emblems not showing properly on right shoulder."}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Fixed an error that would occur when using Nyx’s Absorb."}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Fixed Nyx’s Chaos sometimes ending early when a target under Chaos dies."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed the First Daily Win reward being given in Conclave on loss."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where a downed player could not be revived while moving upwards on an elevator."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed Conclave host migration occurring with only one player left in the match."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Clients sometimes getting stuck/frozen when getting revived after killing a member of the G3."}},
        {tags={"Conclave","Tactical Reload",},text={"*Fixed the Tactical Reload Conclave Mod being equipable by Bows."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed XP locked weapons in the Market not showing their cost."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed enemies detecting players through player flashlight lighting."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed typo in Mesa’s Longhorn Helmet blueprint."}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Trial",},text={"%'%'%'Trials of Vay Hek Fixes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Hijack",},text={"*Fixed an issue where a player sitting on the Revive / Forfeit menu during the Hijack portion of the second phase would cause the Mission to stop progressing."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.2.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 7th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/436486-hotfix-1622/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Survival","Void",},text={"*Removed some procedural rooms from Void Survival that would not spawn Survival points."}},
        {tags={"Cernos",},text={"*%[%[Cernos%]%] has been given the PBR treatment!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players can now use the /unstuck command when in Solo mode."}},
        {tags={},text={"**The /unstuck command is now usable in any chat channel, but only when a player is in a Mission."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed crash that would occur for players using AMD drivers when loading into missions."}},
        {tags={"Ceres",},text={"*Fixed a location on Ceres, Exta where players could become stuck against terrain and the level ceiling."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue in Gifting caused by selecting a negative quantity, also prevented players from Gifting negative amounts in the future."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.2.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"April 6th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/435632-hotfix-1621/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Regen",},text={"*Removed health regeneration from the Grineer Manic."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Improved the responsiveness of the Grineer Manic’s reactive teleport."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Radiation and Toxic procs are now less effective on the Grineer Manic."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various localization changes."}},
        {tags={},text={"*The unstuck command will now only function in the Squad chat. Please note that this command is still in the WIP stage, and will be tweaked as necessary!"}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed crash at boot or when loading into Missions experienced by specific AMD hardware when using DirectX 10 or 11."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various gameplay crashes."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed Mastery Rank test in the Relay assuming the player selected ‘Qualify’ when entering the pause menu on the ‘Qualify / ‘Practice’ option."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed issue with ‘unstuck’ command when used in Archwing."}},
        {tags={"Sargas Ruk","Seeker",},text={"*Fixed Sargas Ruk doing a flashy T-pose on death, instead of dying like a normal Grineer. What an attention seeker!"}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Fixed Clients displaying incorrect HUD Transmissions during Quest missions."}},
        {tags={"Vauban","Boltor Prime","Latron Prime","Prime",},text={"*Fixed Latron Prime Blueprint dropping from Reinforced Orokin Loot Crates instead of Boltor Prime Blueprint."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the navigation waypoint not properly pathing to extraction points."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.2"},
["date"]={"April 1st, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/432384-update-162/"},
["image"]={"Update 16.2 Augments.png"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Added an ‘unstuck’ command to help players escape from areas they would otherwise have had to leave the Mission to escape. The ‘unstuck’ command can ONLY be used when the player is alive, and will teleport the player to their last good position."}},
        {tags={},text={"**The command can be used simply by typing /unstuck"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Once the Mission has been completed, you will be required to restart Warframe so that we may receive an appropriate bug report to help identify these problem locations and monitor the use of the unstuck command."}},
        {tags={"Clan","Liset","Conclave",},text={"**The ‘unstuck’ command does not currently function in the Liset, Dojo or Conclave."}},
        {tags={},text={"**There is a 2 minute cooldown between use."}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'OROKIN COLOR PICKER%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Add the colors of the Orokin to your Warframe and Weapons! This new color picker features colors derived from the relics of the Orokin Empire."}},
        {tags={},text={"Visit the Market to purchase it today!"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'AUGMENT MODS%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Nova","Volt","Escape Velocity",},text={"*%'%'%'Nova%'%'%' -- Escape Velocity (Worm Hole Augment): Allies that travel through Worm Hole gain a 30PERZENTO speed bonus for 4 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Valkyr","Eternal War",},text={"*%'%'%'Valkyr%'%'%' -- Eternal War (Warcry Augment): While active, Warcry%'s duration is increased by 1.25 seconds for each melee kill."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Capacitance",},text={"*%'%'%'Volt%'%'%' -- Capacitance (Overload Augment): Converts 3PERZENTO of damage dealt into shields split between Volt and allied Tenno."}},
        {tags={"Syndicates","Reach",},text={"Get these new Augment Mods now by reaching top rank with select Syndicates."}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'NEW NOGGLES%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Nekros","Nova",},text={"*Nova and Nekros Noggles have been added to the Market today!"}},
},--additions end
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Increased Frost’s Snow Globe slowdown effect in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Increased Mag’s Pull range in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Increased Volt’s Overload field expansion speed, but reduced its range and duration in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"*Rhino is no longer invulnerable when performing Rhino Charge in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Excalibur is no longer invulnerable when performing Slash Dash in PvP."}},
        {tags={},text={"*A respawn timer will now show on the player HUD when dead and spectating."}},
        {tags={"Retribution",},text={"%'%'%'The Law of Retribution Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek",},text={"*Vay Hek now has immunity from Warframe powers when he is immune to damage."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Added various performance improvements for low spec computers."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Made various lighting adjustments and visual improvements in the Relays."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various audio adjustments to weapon attacks."}},
        {tags={"Mars","Phobos","Saturn","Neptune","Pluto","Sedna",},text={"*Made improvements to lighting effects on Phobos, Saturn, Neptune, Sedna, Pluto and Mars tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*The Installed Mods section of the Mod foundry will now show Mods on both the PvE and PvP versions of a weapon."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Shocking Speed",},text={"*Volt’s Shocking Speed effect now has accompanying audio."}},
        {tags={"Glaive",},text={"*Improved visuals on the Glaive’s mid-flight explosion effect."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players will no longer be able to keep Syndicate Medallions found during a Mission if the Mission is not completed."}},
        {tags={"Ripkas",},text={"*Made some adjustments to the Ripkas elemental effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Updated the sound of Team Restore items."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Volt","Prime",},text={"*Fixed Volt Prime’s blurry textures."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed error that would prevent Chroma from using powers after deactivating Effigy."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed match timer in Cephalon Capture resetting back to 15 minutes on Host migration."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Fixed issue caused by players disconnecting while holding the Cephalon in Cephalon Capture."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed Ash’s Bladestorm looping on a single target if that target became immune to the ability after it Bladestorm had begun as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/427783-ash-bladestorm-and-nullifiers/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Mirage’s clones from Hall of Mirrors sometimes shooting in the wrong direction."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Shipyard",},text={"*Fixed incomplete textures around door frames and skybox on the Grineer Shipyards tileset."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed issue that would allow for more than 8 players in a Conclave match."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed ugly texture on Grineer elevators."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed profile information being wrong the first time a player profile is viewed in a Mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue that would cause a crash on Host migration."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Mirage’s Winter Skin disappearing when Eclipse is activated."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed the team select UI not being usable between Conclave rounds."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Fixed projectiles affected by Mag’s Bullet Attractor not doing any damage."}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"*Fixed tinting on Edo Prime Armor and Kazeru Prime Sugatra."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed players joining Conclave in a pre-made squad being separated when entering Cephalon Capture."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed an issue caused by rapidly selecting the Kubrow Imprint menu, causing the menu to become stuck."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue preventing players to bind Warframe powers to buttons on a gamepad / controller."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed the pause menu staying open when dying in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed Frost’s Avalanche showing duplicate effects for Clients."}},
        {tags={"Vor","Lech Kril",},text={"*Fixed Lech Kril’s Sigil dropping from Orokin Vor."}},
        {tags={"Assassination","Corpus Outpost",},text={"*Fixed black screen that would occur when launching Assassination Missions on the Corpus Outpost tileset."}},
        {tags={"Sanctuary",},text={"*Fixed Sanctuary Rare Spawn targets spawning in Mastery tests."}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris","Sanctuary",},text={"*Fixed Simaris’ text dialogue appearing twice and overlapping whenever a purchase is made in Sanctuary."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed enemies becoming immune to melee attacks when hit with Excalibur’s Radial Javelin."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue in the tutorial caused by navigating the Menu mid-Mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue causing pigment pick up messages to repeat over other items picked up during a mission."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed areas of the Relay concourse where players were able to escape into space."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed graphical issues with some decorations in Relays."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some issues with Synthesis Rare Spawn fleeing behavior when scanned."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.1.6"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 31st, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/431380-hotfix-1616/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Added packet-loss tolerance to the network analysis to avoid bogus Firewall errors (evidently Singapore is difficult for some people to communicate with)."}},
},--fixes end
["localizations"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Made various localization changes for Portuguese and Chinese."}},
},--localizations end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.1.5"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 31st, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/430816-hotfix-1615/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Volt","Prime",},text={"*Improved the texture details on Volt Prime."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Removed the tutorial versions of enemies from Codex, as they are identical to normal enemies."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Boss Sigils missing from the drop tables. Our apologies on how long this took to investigate."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Spectra",},text={"*Fixed Chroma playing idle animations when using Spectral Scream as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/428896-chroma-does-idle-animations-during-spectral-scream/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"*Fixed Rhino’s Iron Skin not being deactivated when entering bleedout, preventing players from bleeding out."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed some areas in the Relay where players could escape in the Navigation room."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed extensive audio when using Frosts’ Augment Mods."}},
        {tags={"Maroo",},text={"*Fixed issue preventing players from being able to complete their scans for Maroo."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various gameplay crashes. "}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.1.4.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 29th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/428325-hotfix-1614/"},
["fixes"]={

        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"**does not affect the extra credits received from enemies that were killed by Effigy during the mission."}},
