},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.1.4"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 27th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/428325-hotfix-1614/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Dex Dakra","Dex","Conclave",},text={"*Dual Sword melee combos have now been enabled in Conclave (ie- Dex Dakra)"}},
},--misc end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issues with matchmaking if players had UPnP and NAT-PMP explicitly disabled."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue that would incorrectly show that NAT-PMP had been detected."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Volt",},text={"*Fixed animation timing for Excalibur’s Radial Javelin when used with cast speed Mods."}},
        {tags={"Darvo",},text={"*Fixed Darvo’s Market display not properly updating when transitioning between deals."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.1.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 26th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/427542-hotfix-1613/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Cephalon Capture now has a round length of 15 minutes. Once the timer expires a team will win / lose depending on how many captures have been completed."}},
        {tags={},text={"*A mission timer has been added to the HUD."}},
        {tags={},text={"*All Warframes now have a base max energy of 100."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Removed ‘Matchmaking Mode’ drop down from Conclave. All matches played are ‘public’ for now."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Lowered breakable vent health in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Dex Furis","Dex Dakra","Dex","Conclave",},text={"*Dex Dakra and Dex Furis have been enabled in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Rhino","Conclave",},text={"*Increased the damage radius of Rhino’s Stomp in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Conclave",},text={"*Increased the range of Excalibur%'s Radial Javelin in Conclave."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Conclave",},text={"*Slowed the activation / casting animations of Excalibur%'s Radial Javelin in Conclave."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players will now receive a notification of what team they’re joining when entering a match mid-session."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Made ‘Unidentified Items’ in Spy 2.0 read more like drops, to make it clear that you’ve picked up an item from the Mission and not a bug."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Proc resistance will now trigger for all proc types simultaneously. This means an enemy with 50PERZENTO resistance in two damage types will have a 50PERZENTO chance to resist both, instead of a 50PERZENTO chance to resist each one individually."}},
        {tags={"Frost","Ice Wave Impedance",},text={"*Added audio cue to Frost’s Ice Wave Impedance Mod effect."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Volt’s Shock will now apply his energy color to the electrocuted proc that targets receive."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Altered some text instances of ‘Transmutations Cores’ to ‘Transmute’."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Corpus Nullifiers are now only immune to ability damage, instead of all radial damage."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Effects on Mag’s Bullet Attractor now match her energy color."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Recovery","Liset","Recover",},text={"*Fixed players being unable to access the Zanuka recovery Mission from their Liset."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Fixed broken Harkonar physics on Volt."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Fixed the quest, The New Strange start and completion diorama automatically closing after a few seconds if the player is in a squad."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed weapon skins and weapon cosmetics from being purchasable multiple times."}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Trial",},text={"*Fixed an issue in the Trials of Vay Hek that cause the consoles in Phase 2 to become unhackable."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture","Lua",},text={"*Fixed an error in Cephalon Capture showing Sun / Moon Cephalon as Sun / Moon Flag."}},
        {tags={"Skana",},text={"*Fixed Skana not sitting properly in the player’s fist."}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"*Fixed Grineer Capture targets showing as Corpus capture targets."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Liset",},text={"*Fixed Kubrow not appearing correctly when in the Liset Arsenal loadout."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*Fixed Arcane enhancements showing visible in the Foundry when players are unable to craft one."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed an issue that would occur when receiving the Sentinel Experience login reward if the player did not have a Sentinel."}},
        {tags={"Ordis",},text={"*Fixed Ordis audio not matching his subtitles."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed possible issue with players being unable to abort a Conclave session."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed players closing the End of Mission screen and being forced to leave a Conclave session."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some inconsistencies with Augment descriptions."}},
        {tags={"Glaive","Dex",},text={"*Fixed Glaive not showing in up Melee 2.0 Codex."}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"*Fixed channeling not projecting correctly on the Edo Prime chest attachment as per %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/426856-edo-prime-chest-piece-channeling-pattern-visual-bug/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed typo that would appear when selecting an Arcane."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed detonation projectiles not dealing damage for Clients."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Clients not seeing properly colored weapon trails from their weapons."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Shield Transference",},text={"*Fixed Mag’s Shield Transference Mod not applying the players energy color to the ability."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Liset",},text={"*Fixed some instances of the Liset showing washed out textures."}},
        {tags={"Helios","Dex",},text={"*Fixed an issue where reviving would cause Helios to scan targets with complete Codex entries as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/423544-bug-helios-scans-completed-scan-targets-after-reviving-yourself/?hl=helios this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed some instances of black textures appearing on Frost’s Snow Globe."}},
        {tags={"Entropy Spike",},text={"*Fixed the Mod description of the Entropy Spike Mod to clarify its intended purpose."}},
        {tags={"Nekros",},text={"*Fixed the timing on Nekros’ Terrify casting animation visual effects."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Effigy being hostile to players if spawned while Chroma is under the effects of the Radiation proc."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Fixed players being unable to accept The New Strange Quest with a controller."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.1.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 25th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/426353-hotfix-1612-anniversary-gifts/"},
["title"]={"Second Anniversary"},
["image"]={"Warframe 2nd Anniversary.png "},
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Titania",},text={"Second Anniversary Gifts are in! Thank you for your patience as we distribute the goods, and thank you all for your support these past two years."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Available on Login:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Dex Dakra","Dex",},text={"*%[%[Dex Dakra%]%]"}},
        {tags={"Dex Furis","Dex",},text={"*%[%[Dex Furis%]%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Available in Market:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*%[%[Excalibur Proto-Armor Skin|Excalibur Proto Armor Skin%]%]"}},
        {tags={"Glaive",},text={"*%[%[Proto-Glaive Skin|Proto Glaive Skin%]%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'PvP Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Excalibur’s Radial Javelin will now double check that enemies are within range before launching."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Quick Melee will no longer do full damage on last hit in PvP, slightly reducing the amount of damage dual weapons deal."}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Updated Breeding Grounds trophies to use PBR."}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Fixes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed issue that would occur when Grineer Rollers would enter Limbo’s Cataclysm."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed insignias showing on invisible targets in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Incursion","Clan",},text={"*Fixed tint coloring on Dojo trophies from the Mutalist Incursion event."}},
        {tags={"Trial",},text={"*Fixed <%nowiki><%RAID><%/nowiki> appearing in the in-game invitation popup instead of <%nowiki><%Trial><%/nowiki>."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed error that would occur when trying to change Clan tier."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed crash that would occur when NPCs tried using abilities when ragdolled out of bounds."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Warframe level up notifications appearing partially offscreen in some resolutions."}},
},--additions end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.1.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 25th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/425719-hotfix-1611/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'PvP Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Ability projectiles in PvP soft lock such as Frost’s Icicle now have a slightly improved lock-*on."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Improved velocity and lockon strength of Frost’s Icicle in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Akmagnus","Magnus",},text={"*Slightly increased the damage of the AKMagnus in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Braton",},text={"*Slightly decreased the damage of the MK1 Braton in PvP."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Quest","Liset",},text={"*Liset transmissions relating to The New Strange quest will no longer continue to play while players are crafting parts."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players can no longer purchase multiple copies of item skins from the Market."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Reduced the minimum distance to use the ‘collector’ by 50 meters"}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Relay","Darvo",},text={"*Fixed crash that could occur when accessing Darvo’s Market in the Relay."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Arcane enhancements being set to base rank on Clients."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed Zephyr’s Tornado causing FPS issues on lower-end PCs."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue caused by uninstalling a Mod when performing a Mod fusion."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Orokin enemies not properly getting cut into pieces, instead forming multiple bodies."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue causing default helmets not loading in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Odonata Prime","Prime",},text={"*Fixed Odonata Prime parts not being tradable."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed Frost’s Icicle not following proper flight paths in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Trial",},text={"*Fixed further textures to be color blind friendly in PvP and the Trial of Vay Hek."}},
        {tags={"Loki","Void","Prime",},text={"*Fixed Loki default helmet appearing as Prime version in Void."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.1"},
["date"]={"March 25th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/425416-update-161-volt-prime/"},
["title"]={"Volt Prime"},
["image"]={"Volt Prime Access.png"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Volt","Prime",},text={"Volt Prime and the newest Prime Gear is here!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Volt","Prime",},text={"A shocking agent of death, Volt Prime speeds into Prime Access."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Volt","Odonata Prime","Prime",},text={"Get Volt Prime Access now and make the newest Primes yours - instantly - including Volt Prime, Odonata Prime, Edo Prime Armor and the Kazeru Prime Sugatra. Plus, stock up on discounted Platinum and get Exclusive Gear available only through Prime Access!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"Packages include:"}},
        {tags={"Volt","Prime",},text={"%'%'%'NEW VOLT PRIME WARFRAME%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Volt","Prime",},text={"Harness electricity and bring high damage with Volt Prime, the newest addition to the ranks of elite Prime Warframes."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Prime",},text={"%'%'%'NEW PRIME ARCHWING%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Odonata Prime","Prime",},text={"Take flight with Odonata Prime."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"%'%'%'PRIME ACCESSORIES%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Volt","Prime",},text={"Modify your Warframe with the Edo Prime Armor and Kazeru Prime Sugatra. Add Exclusive Volt Prime Avatars to your online presence! Speed up your in-game progress with 90-Day Affinity and Credit Boosters."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'DISCOUNTED PLATINUM%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"Give your Platinum supply a boost at a great price."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"Click %[%https://warframe.com/prime-access here%] to learn more about Prime Access now! "}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Volt","Prime","Hunt",},text={"You can begin to hunt for Volt Prime parts using %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/425415-prime-gear-drop-locations/ our drop tables%] for guidance."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'PvP Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Bo","Bo Prime","Prime",},text={"*Reduced Bo Prime’s damage to be closer to the other Bo weapons."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Volt’s Overload is now more noticeable in PvP, particularly for players using black energy."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Syndicates","Cephalon Simaris",},text={"*Increased the Standing cap for Simaris to be the same as other Syndicates."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Rewards for Synthesis scanning now count towards your daily Standing limit."}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Trial",},text={"*Added colorblind pipe variants for the Trials of Vay Hek."}},
},--additions end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Sigils overlapping on shoulder attachments."}},
        {tags={"Simulacrum",},text={"*Fixed players earning experience from Simulacrum kills."}},
        {tags={"Simulacrum",},text={"*Fixed players earning ability experience from Simulacrum kills."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed login errors occurring in new player accounts."}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"*Fixed players without a Synthesis Scanner equipped receiving mid-Mission notification of a rare capture target."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Fury",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Vex Armor increasing for shield / health decreases caused by Elemental Ward ending as per %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/425092-permanent-fury-on-chroma/ this thread%]."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Synthesis scans not displaying how much standing has been earned."}},
        {tags={"Ripkas",},text={"*Fixed Ripkas not taking the proper color customizations from players."}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Trial",},text={"*Fixed rare Synthesis targets spawning in Trials of Vay Hek."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Mk1 Bo not being usable in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s pelt not functioning properly for Clients."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed inconsistent headshot multipliers in PvP, all weapons now have 2x crit multiplier on headshots."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Effigy giving players a stacking credit boost. One Effigy per Chroma."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Nyx",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Ash’s Bladestorm when one of his targets becomes Mind Controlled mid-Bladestorm as per %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/425163-blade-storm-mind-control-horrible-bug/ this thread%]."}},
        {tags={"Ember","Frost",},text={"*Fixed Frost’s Icicle and Ember’s Fireball auto-targeting the enemy’s torso when aiming manually at a target."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some instances of crashes related to players using AMD drivers."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed melee attacks not properly aligning to camera for Clients entering a Mission."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.0.4"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 23rd, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/424657-hotfix-1604/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'PvP Balance Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Equinox","Capture","Cephalon Capture","Equilibrium",},text={"The recent soft launch of Cephalon Capture has allowed us to make changes for the better from your feedback and concerns. It was clear that certain weapons/abilities/maneuvers/etc were not balanced properly for this new game mode, and we thank you for constructively helping us continue to harmonize Cephalon Capture. The following changes have been made to help create and maintain that equilibrium:"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Slide attacks in PvP will now only use the base damage of the melee weapon wielded. Previously, slide attacks used a damage multiplier; this was shifting the balance in a negative direction."}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"*Rhino’s Iron Skin is no longer a permanent buff, and will begin to slowly decay after a short period of time."}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"*Made adjustments to Rhino’s Charge damage in PvP."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Arcane Helmets have been removed from PvP."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Mods that increase maximum clip size may now be used in PvP."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased DPS for all Primary and Secondary weapons in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Increased the damage of Frost’s Freeze ability in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Increased the range of Mag’s Pull ability in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Increased the amount of overshield from Mag’s Shield Polarize ability in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Reduced the damage of Volt’s Shock ability in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Paris",},text={"*Reduced the damage of all Paris bows in PvP."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'PvP Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Updated water effects in Orokin Cephalon Capture level."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Respawning Cephalons now play a sound on respawn."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*The Gear wheel has been removed from the Conclave Arsenal menu."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Building Arcane Enhancements now costs 200k credits."}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"*Gave Rhino’s Stomp a slight audio boost."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Removed dead animation time at the end of Excalibur’s Radial Javelin and radial Blind casting animations."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Spy",},text={"*Tweaked various defenses in Corpus Spy vaults."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Spy",},text={"*Tweaked various defenses in Grineer Spy vaults."}},
        {tags={"Ambulas","Simulacrum","Sanctuary",},text={"*Ambulas has been removed from the Sanctuary Simulacrum."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Multiple items can now be purchased at once from the Syndicate Market UI."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Increased timers in Grineer Spy vaults."}},
        {tags={"Void","Clan",},text={"*Made various environmental PBR updates to the Orokin Void and Tenno Dojo."}},
        {tags={"Ignis",},text={"**Increased the brightness of flames produced by the Ignis."}},
        {tags={"Embolist",},text={"*Improved the smokey particle effects on the Embolist’s weapon fire."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players without a Synthesis Scanner equipped during a scan will no longer receive Synthesis progress."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Energy orbs worth 50 energy are now larger than orbs worth 25 energy."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various localization updates."}},
        {tags={"Nekros",},text={"*Added casting trails on Nekros’ abilities."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed Warframes not holding a weapon in the Melee training portion of the Codex"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue causing players to respawn players with 100PERZENTO health after Host migration in PvP"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the Grineer Manic’s animations not properly deactivating Warframe powers, giving him unintended power immunity pre-attack. Tackles from the Grineer Manic will now properly disable Warframe powers as intended."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Spectra",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Spectral Scream being affected by rift status."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Elemental Ward fire AoE being affected by rift status."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed lighting effects on glowing MOA spawners in Corpus Spy vaults."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed issue in Spy missions where failing the main console hack would cause the ‘data destruction’ timer to stop and be unusable for the remainder of the Mission."}},
        {tags={"Parry",},text={"*Fixed players being unable to parry the Grineer Heavy Gunner’s melee slam."}},
        {tags={"Trial",},text={"*Fixed being able to use 8-player squads in other non-Trial Missions."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture","Conclave",},text={"*Fixed players being able to bypass Conclave loadouts by joining Cephalon Capture via invite or entering a match via the Join in Progress button."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed a light not properly casting shadows in the Grineer Spy vault."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Noble animations displaying a strange hand positioning."}},
        {tags={"Bo Prime","Prime",},text={"*Fixed Bo Prime not being usable in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Sanctuary",},text={"*Fixed Sanctuary’s Mastery Test practice simulations giving Credit rewards."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed login error caused by conflicting update versions tagged on an account."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Elemental Ward not properly removing ‘old’ buff when 2+ Chroma activate the ability."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed an issue where players who once equipped mustaches will still have mustaches equipped when changing weapon skins within the Liset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed certain texture in CTF Gas City Map that was causing interaction issues."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed accounts that had lost their loadout information upon logging in."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed loadout error that could occur in PvP Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Simulacrum","Sanctuary",},text={"*Fixed item drops appearing from enemies in Sanctuary Simulacrum."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.0.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 23rd, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/424333-hotfix-1603/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Void","Solar Rail",},text={"*Doubled Dark Sector credit rewards again (a bug was doubling these rewards previously and fixing the bug resulted in an unintended nerf. For clarity, Void credits were never touched.)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*The cooldown delay for Raid Rewards was reduced to 23 hours to make it slightly easier to coordinate daily runs with friends."}},
        {tags={"Vauban",},text={"*Increased Vauban’s Bounce Pad duration to 5 minutes (from 15 seconds)."}},
        {tags={"Trinity",},text={"*Trinity’s Well of Life will no longer waste energy on invulnerable targets."}},
        {tags={"Trinity","Hek","Vay Hek",},text={"*Vay Hek is no longer vulnerable to Trinity’s Energy Vampire (like other bosses)."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture","Orokin Catalyst","Orokin Reactor","Forma",},text={"*Removed Orokin Catalyst/Reactor and Forma from Cephalon Capture reward manifest."}},
},--misc end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Trinity",},text={"*Fixed Trinity’s Well of Life inadvertently causing damage to certain enemies."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Trinity",},text={"*Fixed Trinity’s Well of Life and other powers for clients targeting invulnerable enemies after Mag’s Bullet Attractor had expired."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed bug that would cause some PvP sessions to be marked private."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed loot lockers and crates dropping 25 energy pickups instead of 50."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various gameplay crashes."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.0.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 21st, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/421728-hotfix-1602/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"*Reduced the HP of Rhino’s Ironskin in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Paris Prime","Prime",},text={"*Reduced the damage of Paris Prime in PvP."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Made various lighting lighting improvements to Cephalon Capture’s Orokin tileset."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Improved textures of Cephalon Capture objectives."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture","Teshin",},text={"*Teshin will now speak a little more quickly in Cephalon Capture."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Chroma","Quest",},text={"*Chroma’s alt helmet will no longer appear in player inventories after completing The New Strange Quest."}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Retribution",},text={"*Removed exits from Vay Hek boss fight in Law of Retribution."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Improved audio quality on Grineer hacking minigame."}},
        {tags={"Banshee",},text={"*Made various audio adjustments to silenced weapons."}},
        {tags={"Liset","Ordis",},text={"*Reduced Ordis’ conversational tendencies when in the Liset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Updated Mod and Market icons for Transmutation Cores."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Updated various materials to PBR quality."}},
        {tags={"Rescue",},text={"*Rescue Missions will now provide more experience at higher levels."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Reduced the level of ambient noise in Relays."}},
        {tags={},text={"*The Manic Grineer will now disappear after killing a player in Solo mode."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*The Manic Grineer is no longer affected by Bladestorm."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Optimized matchmaking server code to handle load better (may fix some invitation failures)."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Reduced memory footprint in the Relays to avoid some crashes on 32-bit systems."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed issue where players could enter Conclave with no valid PvP gear during a Host migration, causing a crash."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed default PvP loadout containing non-PvP items."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Spectra",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Spectral Scream not equipping a weapon once deactivated if the ability was used while holding a scanner."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed some instances where players could move out of bounds on Relays."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed auto-target abilities accidentally targeting player Sentinels."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed Limbo being able to trigger pressure plates while in the Rift."}},
        {tags={"Retribution",},text={"*Fixed various exploits in the Law of Retribution encounters."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed rain particles looking pixelated."}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"*Fixed Arcane Enhancements appearing with Prime part in the Inventory."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed error that would occur when trying to equip a scanner while unarmed."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed ‘Logout’ button not bringing players back to Login screen."}},
        {tags={"Steel Meridian",},text={"*Fixed material on Steel Meridian Medallions not displaying correctly."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed various texture issues in Relays."}},
        {tags={"Soma","Soma Prime","Prime",},text={"*Fixed additional ammo clips showing on Soma and Soma Prime."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue causing the Manic Grineer to become stuck on objects."}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Fixed mind controlled targets not becoming immune to Mind Control when granted immunity to powers and abilities."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an ugly white line showing up on the floor of Grineer tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Ogris",},text={"*Fixed Ogris charging effects and firing animation not displaying properly."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed players not being able to unequip Sentinels after removing primary and secondary weapons."}},
        {tags={"Liset","Ordis",},text={"*Fixed Ordis’ ‘idle’ Liset transmissions interrupting other communications."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel","Quest",},text={"*Fixed Sentinel NPCs, voice acting, or animations showing for Clients in the first mission of The New Strange Quest."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Fixed Chroma’s Elemental Ward buff showing as 1PERZENTO off in the HUD in some instances."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed melee finishers adjusting the player camera in an unusual way."}},
        {tags={"Gammacor",},text={"*Fixed visual issue with Gammacor where weapon would open while holstered after melee or a quick roll."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Huras","Stalk",},text={"*Fixed Huras Kubrow’s Stalk not properly applying to Players."}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Relay",},text={"*Fixed Vay Hek still broadcasting his threats to Relays."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed Codex and Library scanners still showing as being equipped during a Host migration."}},
        {tags={"Ordis","Teshin",},text={"*Fixed both Ordis and Teshin transmission not matching corresponding subtitles."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed pistol and melee slots not being auto-equipped for default PvP loadouts."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Shipyard",},text={"*Fixed an error that would occur with Grineer Shipyards cranes."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.0.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"March 20th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/421286-hotfix-1601/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Improved the music cue in PvP."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added sound effect to procs trigged from Arcane enhancements."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Squads starting a PvP Mission without the appropriate Conclave loadout will now be taken to the Arsenal to equip the correct gear."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Hek","Vor","Vay Hek",},text={"*Fixed crash that could occur during Vay Hek and Vor boss fights."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed login failure if your account has old pending recipes that contained weapon skins."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed players losing Kubrow customization on account update."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed players losing Kubrow shape/pattern info on update."}},
        {tags={"Trial","Foundry",},text={"*Fixed <%RAID> showing up in foundry for Trial blueprint."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Foundry",},text={"*Fixed Excalibur skins showing up as fusible items in Arcane Foundry."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed Ash%'s Teleport targeting player sentinels."}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris",},text={"*Fixed issue caused by Simaris’ community scanning."}},
        {tags={"Fortitude","Sure Footed",},text={"*Fixed text on “Sure Footed” and “Fortitude” mods."}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Trial",},text={"*Fixed Halo Badge not being handed out when completing the Trials of Vay Hek."}},
        {tags={"Loki",},text={"*Fixed Loki’s Invisibility creating particle effects for every equipped attachment."}},
        {tags={"Recovery","Recover",},text={"*Fixed other knocked-down enemies using the Manic Grineer’s knockdown recovery animation."}},
        {tags={"Soma",},text={"*Fixed weapon skins not properly applying to the Soma."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed idle animations not properly playing when players are inactive."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"16.0"},
["date"]={"March 19th, 2015"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/420448-warframe-sanctuary/"},
["image"]={"Update 16 Sanctuary.png"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"%'%'%'New Quest: The New Strange%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris","Hunt",},text={"Work with Cephalon Simaris to find the source of a mysterious voice. Something is out there, and it is hunting. Work with Simaris to track it down and discover its true nature."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'WHAT YOU SHOULD KNOW%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Quest","Liset","Dex","Cephalon Simaris","Sanctuary",},text={"In order to obtain this new Quest, players above Mastery Rank 5 must first visit Cephalon Simaris in his Sanctuary after completing the Stolen Dreams Quest. From there players can acquire The New Strange and accept it from the Codex once they return to the Liset."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"%'%'%'New Warframe: Chroma%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"A master of the deadly elements, Chroma can alter his damage output by changing his energy coloring."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"Chroma’s powers include:"}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Spectra",},text={"*Spectral Scream: Exhale a deep breath of elemental destruction. Chroma%'s energy color determines the element."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Elemental Ward: Depending on Chroma%'s elemental alignment, an offensive area-of-effect is created. Chroma and its nearby allies are imbued with defensive energy."}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"*Vex Armor: When shields are damaged, Chroma%'s armor grows stronger, when health is damaged, weapon damage output increases. Active for a limited duration, the strength of this ability corresponds to rank."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Ivara",},text={"*Effigy: Chroma turns his pelt into a massive sentry that strengthens nearby allies and engulfs enemies in elemental attacks."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Retribution",},text={"%'%'%'New Mission: The Law of Retribution%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Assault","Relay","Lotus","Hunt","Protect",},text={"Tenno are out for vengeance, and Grineer Councilor Vay Hek has nowhere left to hide. Hot on his trail after the assault on Relays during Eyes of Blight, the Lotus will guide a precision strike force to hunt down and put an end to Vay Hek once and for all. Be wary Tenno. Numbers alone will not protect you when diving into the heart of Grineer military power."}},
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek",},text={"*Create a squad of 4 to 8 Tenno and work through three challenge scenarios as you track down Vay Hek across the Solar System."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Hek","Vay Hek",},text={"*Earn Arcane enhancements from defeating Vay Hek, each of which provide a unique bonus Warframes can unleash during combat."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"**In order to equip an Arcane enhancement players must construct it in their Foundry under the Miscellaneous tab."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Players will then need to select what Helmet or Syandana they would like the Arcane to be attached to."}},
        {tags={},text={"**After construction the Arcane Helmet or Arcane Syandana will appear in their Arsenal under Attachments."}},
        {tags={"Retribution",},text={"*The Law of Retribution Key Blueprint is available for purchase in the marketplace for 100,000 Credits."}},
        {tags={},text={"*This Mission can be completed with a minimum of 4 Tenno for an additional challenge. Enemies will not scale down in difficulty with fewer players present in the Mission."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture","Teshin","Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'Conclave Syndicate, Teshin, and Cephalon Capture%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture","Liset","Conclave","Sanctuary",},text={"Cephalon Capture is our latest PvP game mode available on the Conclave Console in the Liset, and with its arrival come a slew of new changes to our existing PvP system. The following are a list of general changes to PvP immediately available in Update: Sanctuary:"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players will only be allowed to select Warframes from an existing list of Warframes balanced exclusively for PvP 2.0. This list will be expanded upon in future Updates and currently includes:"}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"**Mag"}},
        {tags={"Chroma",},text={"**Chroma"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"**Excalibur"}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"**Frost"}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"**Rhino"}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"**Volt"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players will be limited to Weapons and Mods that have balanced exclusively for PvP. This list will be expanding upon in future Updates."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Players can access their unique Conclave loadouts from the Arsenal by selecting ‘Select Mode’, followed by ‘Conclave’."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Cephalon Capture has three maps on launch that will rotate between rounds!"}},
        {tags={"Capture","Cephalon Capture",},text={"*Cephalon Capture now features Challenges that can be unlocked while fighting against your fellow Tenno."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Vor","Trial","Lotus","Teshin","Conclave",},text={"With our expansion to PvP 2.0 comes a new figure to guide Tenno through the Conclave’s trials and toward the path of enlightenment. Teshin, a survivor of the Orokin Era, disagrees with the Lotus’ policies and strives to see Tenno break free of that which restrains their potential."}},
        {tags={"Relay","Teshin","Conclave",},text={"Teshin and the Conclave Syndicate have established their presence in the Northern Wing of any Relay, and offer unique Syndicate rewards for Tenno who represent Teshin when participating in Conclave PvP."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris","Sanctuary",},text={"%'%'%'Simaris and Sanctuary%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Relay","Dex","Cephalon Simaris","Sanctuary",},text={"In the cradle of space on Tenno Relays across the Solar System Cephalon Simaris has constructed a Sanctuary with the goal of eternally preserving all life. But Simaris needs your help, Tenno. Using Simaris’ advanced Synthesis Scanner you must track down unique targets to scan in a Codex shared by Tenno all over the world, and help unlock exciting new lore and Syndicate rewards."}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris",},text={"*Simaris is a Neutral Syndicate and can be joined by any Tenno regardless of current Syndicate allegiance."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Dex",},text={"*Synthesis Scanners can be equipped like regular Gear, and are used in the same fashion a Codex scanner is."}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris","Hunt",},text={"*Running a Mission that features a targeted enemy will prompt a notification from Simaris, making it a little easier to hunt targets."}},
        {tags={"Dex","Cephalon Simaris",},text={"*Enemies scanned will be tracked by Simaris unlock a global Codex of lore, in addition to providing affinity toward Simaris’ unique Syndicate rewards."}},
        {tags={},text={"*While playing in Missions Rare Spawn enemies with unique abilities may appear. These enemies will be more difficult to scan, and Synthesis can have dangerous side-effects on targets scanned."}},
        {tags={"Syndicates","Cephalon Simaris",},text={"*Scanning targets will earn affinity with Simaris, and earning affinity with Simaris will not decrease Standing earned with other Syndicates."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Enemy%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"Grineer Manic- Dashing in for quick strikes before falling back to the shadows the Grineer Manic is difficult to catch, and deadly to ignore."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Mods:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Ability Augment Mods%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Loki","Hush","Hushed Invisibility",},text={"*Loki: Invisibility: Hushed Invisibility - Weapon noise is reduced by 100PERZENTO while invisible."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Vor","Fracturing Crush","Rush",},text={"*Mag: Crush: Fracturing Crush - Survivors have their armor decreased by 50PERZENTO and become unable to move for 7 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Frost","Chilling Globe",},text={"*Frost: Snow Globe: Chilling Globe - Enemies that enter have a 50PERZENTO chance to become frozen solid for 8 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Spectra","Afterburn",},text={"*Chroma: Spectral Scream: Afterburn - On deactivating this ability Chroma will launch an elemental projectile dealing 100 damage for each second the ability was active, to a maximum of 500 damage."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"%'%'%'New Conclave Mods%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Night Stalker","Stalk",},text={"*Pistol Mod: Night Stalker - Target visible on map for +6 seconds if hit"}},
        {tags={"Apex Predator",},text={"*Rifle Mod: Apex Predator - Target visible on map for +6 seconds if hit"}},
        {tags={"Bounty","Bounty Hunter","Hunt",},text={"*Shotgun Mod: Bounty Hunter -Target visible on map for +6 seconds if hit"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Purging Slash",},text={"*Excalibur Augment: Purging Slash - Allies in the path of Slash Dash have 4 debuffs removed and 100 shields restored."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Signal Flare",},text={"*Excalibur Augment: Signal Flare - Enemies blinded by Radial Blind are marked on the minimap for 12 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Reach","Sapping Reach",},text={"*Mag Augment: Sapping Reach - Pull steals up to 25 energy from the target"}},
        {tags={"Mag","Regen","Shield Overload",},text={"*Mag Augment: Shield Overload - Increases the time for a target’s shields to regenerate by 9 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Kinetic Collision",},text={"*Volt Augment: Kinetic Collision - Running into an opponent increases the duration of speed by 4 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Recharge Barrier",},text={"*Volt Augment: Recharge Barrier - Allies that pass through have 100 shields restored."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Eject Magazine",},text={"*Pistol Mod: Eject Magazine - +20PERZENTO Clip Reloaded per Second when Holstered."}},
        {tags={"Tactical Reload",},text={"*Rifle Mod: Tactical Reload - +20PERZENTO Clip Reloaded per Second when Holstered."}},
        {tags={"Lock and Load",},text={"*Shotgun Mod: Lock and Load - +20PERZENTO Clip Reloaded per Second when Holstered."}},
        {tags={"Lex","Reflex Draw",},text={"*Pistol Mod: Reflex Draw – +80PERZENTO holster rate"}},
        {tags={"Twitch",},text={"*Rifle Mod: Twitch - +80PERZENTO holster rate"}},
        {tags={"Soft Hands",},text={"*Shotgun Mod: Soft Hands – +80PERZENTO holster rate"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Simaris",},text={"%'%'%'New Cephalon Simaris Mods%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Relay","Cephalon Simaris","Sanctuary",},text={"The following Mods can be purchased through Cephalon Simaris in the Relay Sanctuary with Standing."}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Helios","Research","Detect Vulnerability",},text={"*Helios: Detect Vulnerability - Upon completing research on an enemy subsequent scans will reveal their weak points. This ability works similar to Banshee`s sonar."}},
        {tags={"Madurai Transmute Core","Madurai",},text={"*Madurai Transmute Core - Ensures transmuted Mod is of the Madurai polarity and eliminates credit cost."}},
        {tags={"Vazarin Transmute Core","Vazarin",},text={"*Vazarin transmute Core - Ensures transmuted Mod is of the Vazarin polarity and eliminates credit cost."}},
        {tags={"Naramon Transmute Core","Naramon",},text={"*Naramon Transmute Core - Ensures transmuted Mod is of the Naramon polarity and eliminates credit cost."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Weapons%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Kohm","Kohmak","Ohma",},text={"*Kohmak - Like the larger Kohm, this hand-shotgun doubles its volley with every successive shot."}},
        {tags={"Ripkas",},text={"*Ripkas - Motorized saw fists usable in close combat."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Stance%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Four Riders",},text={"*Four Riders - A claw Stance capable of fast strikes and powerful slams."}},
},--misc end
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Hek","Vay Hek","Tyl Regor","Jackal","Nef Anyo",},text={"*Added Boss Sigils for Nef Anyo, Jackal, Vay Hek and Tyl Regor."}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Vauban",},text={"*Banshee and Vauban Noggles have been added to the Market!"}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Tigrol Gene Masking Kit for Kubrow has been added to the Market!"}},
        {tags={"Lecta","Archwing","Conclave",},text={"*Players can now select between Cooperative, Archwing, and Conclave loadouts independently. Conclave loadouts will not be selectable in Cooperative and vice versa."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players can now see a Warframe’s Power Strength, Range, Duration and Efficiency stats in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={},text={"*A new Sigil has been added to the inventory of our current Blade and Gun Champions. Congrats, and good luck to future participants!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players will now see a spinner under Join Session to help better display loading times."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*A ‘Missions’ section has been added to the Codex, describing Warframe’s various game modes."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Energy, Health and Affinity orbs now have received a visual update. Same colored orb, new stylish look!"}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Treasury Ship Alerts will now appear on the Star Chart! These Missions offer greater Credit rewards at a higher level of difficulty than normal Missions."}},
        {tags={"Liset","Ordis",},text={"*New Ordis transmissions have been added to the Liset!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Further improvements have been made to Reverb effects in Missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Melee impact audio on all weapons have been adjusted - hitting enemies and being hit should sound much more pronounced!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added new sound effects for bullet impacts"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Revised Warframe footsteps audio on all surfaces for walking, running and sliding."}},
        {tags={},text={"*New refined damage/affinity numbers."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added audio details to Arsenal -- weapons should now display their loudness to help better distinguish between Silent or Alarming weapons fire."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Warframe Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Improved Volt’s Overload FX to more accurately show the range of the attack."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Improved the quality of Volt’s shock FX."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Excalibur has been given a visual update via PBR!"}},
        {tags={"Mag","Rush",},text={"*Mag`s Crush now ticks three times during its casting animation. New enemies that enter within the range will be caught within Crush during each tick."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Rush",},text={"*Enemies that survive Mag`s Crush will be ragdolled at the end of the animation."}},
        {tags={"Reach",},text={"*Warframe’s will now show a special visual effect when reaching 0 Stamina, similar to Shields."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Ability Augment Mod Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'The following Augment Mods can be used in PvP%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Frost","Ice Wave Impedance",},text={"*Frost - Ice Wave Impedance"}},
        {tags={"Rhino","Iron Shrapnel",},text={"*Rhino - Iron Shrapnel"}},
        {tags={"Chroma","Afterburn",},text={"*Chroma - Afterburn."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Excavation",},text={"%'%'%'Excavation Mission Changes:  %'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Excavation","Dig",},text={"*The Excavation scanner has been removed from the Mission instead giving 3 dig sites at once."}},
        {tags={"Dig",},text={"*Dig sites will appear in an order that gives players a reasonable tour through the map starting at the closest dig site."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added a small ramp-up to the spawns so that players don%'t get jumped right away when entering the Mission."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Mission Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Life Support Modules now have a chance to spawn from lockers and breakable containers in Survival."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Made a small boost to Life Support Module drop rate in Survival when playing in Solo."}},
        {tags={},text={"*After completing a Mission a ‘Repeat Mission’ button will be available on the Last Mission Results screen. This will take you directly to the Star Chart node selection."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Weapon Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Synoid Gammacor",},text={"*Synoid Gammacor now consumes 7.5x more ammunition."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Synoid Gammacor",},text={"*Synoid Gammacor magazine capacity has been reduced."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Cold Damage Mods drain increased to 11 to be on par with other elemental damage mods"}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Rebalanced values for Survival enemy spawn loop and drop chance."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Adjusted traps, camera drone patrols, and enemy patrols of various Vaults in Spy Missions."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Adjusted lighting in Corpus Spy Vaults to remove shadows from laser grids."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Various weapons have been given the PBR treatment!"}},
        {tags={"Twin Vipers","Viper","Wraith",},text={"*The Viper, Dual Vipers and Wraith Twin Vipers have been given a visual and audio overhaul!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made a wide range of audio adjustments to improve the sound quality of most sidearms."}},
        {tags={"Soma Prime","Prime",},text={"*Slightly reduced Soma Prime audio."}},
        {tags={"Void","Derelict",},text={"*Several tweaks were made to spawn rooms and tileset connectors in Void Derelict missions."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Made several adjustments to room generation on the Infested Corpus Survival tileset."}},
        {tags={"Survival","Corpus Gas City",},text={"*Made several adjustments to room generation on Corpus Gas City Survival tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*8 Syndicate Medallions are now guaranteed to appear in any Syndicate Mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Login rewards now have ‘Blueprint’ descriptor where applicable."}},
        {tags={"Reflect",},text={"*Made tweaks to Boss health to better reflect the balance of Host and Clients on Mission start."}},
        {tags={"Syndicates","Sacrifice",},text={"*Syndicates no longer require multiple sacrifices if players drop from a higher tier to a lower one. Players will only need to make one sacrifice ever when moving between tiers."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed collision in Corpus Spy vaults to prevent players circumventing trap doors in the floor."}},
        {tags={"Helios","Dex",},text={"*Fixed an issue where if Helios restarts his scan, players would lose all Codex information gained in the Mission so far."}},
        {tags={"Spy","Grineer Asteroid",},text={"*Fixed an impassible doorway on the Grineer Asteroid Tileset that would appear in Spy Missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed stealth weapon XP multipliers increasing the stealth combo xp multipler as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/400667-stealth-kill-affinity-bonus-is-being-incorrectly-calculated/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Frost","Survival",},text={"*Fixed an issue where picking up Life Support Modules in Survival at 0PERZENTO caused the bar to freeze."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Syndicate allies using throwing weapons not having holsters for their weapons."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed player`s Warframe Mission Summary not appearing correctly after aborting a Mission, instead showing a missing experience bar."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some instances of inappropriate collision at the End of Mission screen."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue causing some space skybox to show a visible cube shape in the distance."}},
        {tags={"Loki",},text={"*Fixed Loki`s Decoy showing broken decorations or special effects."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed various issues with navigation markers in Grineer Spy Missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed incorrect audio cue that could occur when picking up items in a Mission."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed force fields in Grineer Spy vaults not appearing for Clients."}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"*Fixed missing reverb effects in the Orokin large fountain room in Void tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Loki",},text={"*Fixed Loki`s switch teleport being able to get him stuck into the wall."}},
        {tags={"Rescue",},text={"*Fixed a dead Rescue target`s health appearing in the HUD."}},
        {tags={"Lecta",},text={"*Fixed the weapon trail placement on the Lecta."}},
        {tags={"Void","Derelict",},text={"*Fixed a portion of the ceiling on Void Derelict maps that players could clip through."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue caused by being able to access the Pause Menu from within the Mods Loadout screen."}},
        {tags={"Nekros",},text={"*Fixed inappropriate sound replication occurring when Nekros` Soul Punch hits an enemy."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue that would occur when uninstalling a Mod that would exceed energy capacity while fusing."}},
        {tags={"Parry",},text={"*Fixed an issue where a constant stream of bullets would not knock players out of parrying as stamina is reduced to zero."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue causing players Warframe / Appearance in Arsenal showing as ‘No Helmet Selected’ under all categories."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed the Relay minimap missing portions of the Relay."}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Arena",},text={"*Fixed issue caused by Zanuka being launched out of the Alad V fight arena, making it impossible to finish the boss fight."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Panthera",},text={"*Fixed an error that would occur when using Mesa’s Shatter Shield to deflect Panthera blades."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed Spy Rewards displaying more information than just an unidentified item."}},
        {tags={"Helios",},text={"*Fixed the energy color of Helios not applying to his center ‘eye’ until you back out of Appearance in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Clients getting stuck in voting stage of Mission Select if Host leaves during pre-mission countdown."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Host’s Mission select / countdown being cancelled when a Client leaves the squad."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Shipyard",},text={"*Fixed incorrect lighting on Grineer shipyards causing some areas of the floor to appear pitch black while others are fully lit."}},
        {tags={"Rescue",},text={"*Fixed the objective marker not leading to extraction in Rescue Missions after freeing the captive."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Peacemaker not playing its firing sounds correctly."}},
        {tags={"Vauban",},text={"*Fixed enemies not becoming released from Vauban’s Bastille when a Nullifier bubble passes over them."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various exploits that could be used on the obstacle course."}},
        {tags={"Hydroid",},text={"*Fixed opening the menu while using Hydroid’s Undertow causing the menu to appear in the wrong location."}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Suda",},text={"*Fixed four squares appearing in place of allies on the Cephalon Suda Genius Syndicate daily Mission."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed Corpus Nullifiers being affected by Limbo’s Cataclysm."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed enemies still firing their weapons while ragdolled."}},
        {tags={"Hijack",},text={"*Fixed an issue in Hijack where the objective will travel backwards if it encounters a door faster than the door can open."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel","Carrier",},text={"*Fixed the Carrier Sentinel dragging items that have not (or cannot) be claimed, following the player forever."}},
        {tags={"Hind","Wyrm",},text={"*Fixed Wyrmius respawning behind a wall if the player died behind a wall."}},
        {tags={"Dragon Nikana","Nikana",},text={"*Fixed the Dragon Nikana’s Hilt appearing as orange even with no color selected."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue causing players getting spammed with ‘Player has received your invitation’ messages after sending an invite."}},
        {tags={"Ballistica","Red Veil",},text={"*Fixed Red Veil allies in Syndicate Missions using the incorrect skin on their Ballistica."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed grey lines that appear on various Immortal Skins."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being unable to see proper values of items being traded in Trade UI."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*Fixed players being unable to spend Platinum to buy weapon slots from the Foundry after being prompted with a message to do so."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed users being unable to compare Archwing stats in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Fixed players not receiving their reward for completing the Once Awake quest."}},
        {tags={"Spy",},text={"*Fixed Grineer Spy Vault alarms triggering after the Vault has been completed or failed."}},
        {tags={"Sancti Castanas",},text={"*Fixed Sancti Castanas looking blurry."}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Fixed enemy Eximus Toxic auras proccing on players when the enemy is under the effect of mind control as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/395695-ability-exception-nyx-mind-control-on-ancient-corrupt-eximus/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed an issue where selecting Enter Clan Dojo from the Star Chart would not bring you to the Dojo."}},
        {tags={"Panthera",},text={"*Fixed the Panthera’s alt fire being able to damage squad mates."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a broken selection under Audio in the Options menu."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Kubrow","Howl",},text={"*Fixed the Kubrow Howl ability affecting targets that should be immune due to existing effects like Chaos or Disarm."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Gear equipped in Arsenal not properly saving to the ‘Gear Wheel’, making it impossible to use any items."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue caused by closing a queued Inbox Transmission that would cycle the Transmission into an infinite loop."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed an issue where players could open multiple Sell windows at the Ducat Kiosk in Relays."}},
        {tags={"Physique",},text={"*Fixed the Attachments options in the Arsenal / Appearance overlapping the Physique menu."}},
        {tags={"Gram","Nikana",},text={"*Fixed grammatical error in the Gemini Nikana’s Market description."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed melee auto targeting overestimating range as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/376612-melee-autotargetting-overestimating-melee-range/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Missions not being properly translated on Nodes featuring multiple Missions."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed an issue that would occur when trying to accept a Clan invite without having built a Clan key."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the chat bar overlapping the Purchase button on the Syndicate Offerings page for Sigils."}},
        {tags={"Lotus","Maroo",},text={"*Fixed the dialogue between Lotus and Maroo at the final mission of Stolen Dreams being out of order."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Ship",},text={"*Fixed certain loot caches always showing up (no matter what) on Corpus ship tilesets."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue that would occur when players are taxi’d to a node they had not yet unlocked, causing the Star Chart to lock requiring a forced restart to escape."}},
        {tags={"Vauban","Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed Bastille not working on neutral enemies such as the Kubrow or Sand Skate."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed VO not triggering when in the Relay."}},
        {tags={"Defense",},text={"*Fixed clients receiving a ‘Button Label’ text when joining a Defense Mission at the exact moment the Host completes the 5th wave."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed Host getting stuck on a loading screen when returning to Liset from a Mission."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Outpost",},text={"*Fixed terrain that was causing spawn problems on the Corpus Outpost tileset"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a Corpus flare that would not disappear when created."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Smoke Shadow",},text={"*Fixed Ash’s Smoke Shadow Augment Mod not functioning properly."}},
        {tags={"Vauban",},text={"*Fixed Vauban’s Vortex not properly gathering nearby items."}},
        {tags={"Helios","Dex",},text={"*Fixed issue with Helios caused by viewing another player’s profile then entering a Mission, causing Helios to count scans from other player’s completed Codex."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed crash that would occur when launching Warframe."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various localization issues."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.7.2.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"December 19th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/369418-update-1572-15721/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Removed Weapon and Sentinel experience Login Rewards providing experience to a Weapon or Sentinel in the Arsenal if the player logs in with a fully ranked Weapon. We will revisit this as a feature after more testing to ensure tradable weapons are not affected!"}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being unable to sell weapons."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Interception","Void",},text={"*Fixed enemies not spawning in Void Defense and Interception Missions."}},
        {tags={"Interception",},text={"*Fixed black screen when loading into an Interception Mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed error that would occur when scrolling through Inbox message list before Inbox had finished retrieving all messages."}},
        {tags={"Nova","Prime","Dex",},text={"*Fixed Nova Prime not appearing in the Codex for all players."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue that would occur when selling an item you have more than one of."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed NPCs not correctly aiming projectile weapons."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the Grineer Ogma being unable to hit players."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed player HUD disappearing when a Corrupted Key is equipped."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr","Archwing",},text={"*Fixed issue with host migration in Archwing Missions where players using Zephyr are no longer able to move."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.7.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"December 19th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/369418-update-1572-15721/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"%'%'%'New Archwing -%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Itzal","Clan",},text={"ITZAL ARCHWING: Designed for quick clandestine attacks, the Itzal Archwing excels at striking from the darkness of space."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Trinity","Archwing","Itzal",},text={"*Blink: The Itzal Archwing teleports a short distance, dropping all enemy target locks."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Penumbra: Activates a cloaking field that hides the Warframe and any nearby allies who remain stationary."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Volt","Rush",},text={"*Cosmic Crush: Forms a miniature black hole that sucks in all nearby objects before rupturing in a massive shock wave."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fighter Escort: Deploys drones that fight alongside the Warframe, each detonating in a destructive blast when killed."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"%'%'%'New Archwing Weapons%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Fluctus",},text={"FLUCTUS: An Archwing energy weapon that sends waves of deadly plasma crashing into enemies.<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Centaur",},text={"CENTAUR: Part sword, part shield, the Centaur excels at charging headlong into enemy fire."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Itzal","Fluctus","Centaur","Clan","Research",},text={"Start your Research for the Itzal and Fluctus in the Clan Dojo, and purchase the blueprint for the Centaur from the Marketplace today!"}},
},--misc end
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Liset interior effects will now change color depending on player Liset energy customization."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added various effects and NPC animations in Syndicate Rooms."}},
        {tags={"Crushing Ruin","Rush","Shattering Storm",},text={"*Added a new melee combo to Shattering storm and Crushing Ruin Stances."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Resource%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Tellurium: This rare metal is foreign to the Origin System, broken loose from asteroids that have made the long journey from other stars. Tellurium can only be obtained in Archwing Missions as a drop from any slain enemy."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Critical Deceleration","Tainted Shell",},text={"*Reduced negative penalty on Tainted Shell and Critical Deceleration’s fire rate adjustment to avoid purely negative fire rates on shotgun weapons."}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"*Reduced bloom effect on a particular room within the Void that was shining too brightly."}},
        {tags={},text={"*How many shots it takes to destroy a Nullifier Crewman’s shield is no longer determined by the number of players in a mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Joining a Syndicate Mission mid-progress will now show how many Medallions are remaining in the Mission, updating appropriately if Medallions in the Mission have already been picked up."}},
        {tags={"Incursion",},text={"*Reduced the bloom on the Mutalist Incursion Event Badge to make the icon more visible."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Corpus Outpost",},text={"*Toned down the bright white snow on Corpus Outpost Defense Missions."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Large Energy Pickup added to Archwing Missions."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Increased the frequency of Energy Pickups dropped in Archwing Missions."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Weapon and Sentinel experience Login Rewards will now provide experience to a Weapon or Sentinel in the Arsenal if the player logs in with a fully ranked Weapon."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*The Host of a Dojo will no longer be able to pause the game if their matchmaking has been set to Solo."}},
        {tags={"Despair","Kunai",},text={"*Adjustments made to Kunai and Despair holster to remove redundant visual attachments."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Events in the World State Window will now be sorted by time remaining on the event."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*Players that have multiple of an item with a limit of 1 will now be given an appropriate message when attempting to craft said item in the Foundry."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added confirmation message with all standing changes when turning in Medallions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*When creating piles of items to sell, all identical items will automatically be sorted into the same pile."}},
        {tags={"Derelict","Anemic Agility",},text={"*Removed the Anemic Agility Mod from the Grineer Ballista%'s drop table. This was only ever intended to drop in Orokin Derelict Vaults."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Adjusted visual effect from heat generators on Ice Planet tileset."}},
        {tags={"Coolant Leak",},text={"*Toned down visual effect from the Coolant Leak Mod."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Updated the look of the Conclave Team Select screen."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Oxium Ospreys now drop more Oxium!"}},
        {tags={"Kohm",},text={"*Added spin down effect to Kohm."}},
        {tags={"Europa",},text={"*Rebalanced Missions on Europa to allow for a greater variety of Missions."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Sabotage","Hive",},text={"*Hives in Hive Sabotage are now immune to Warframe abilities that cause push / pull effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Damage numbers will now appear when destroying environmental objects."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Warframe Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Nova","Vasto Prime","Prime",},text={"*Textures updated on the Pyra Prime Syandana and Vasto Prime now match Nova Prime’s metallic texture!"}},
        {tags={"Zephyr","Jet Stream",},text={"*Added a 3 second delay on players losing Zephyr’s Jet Stream Mod buff once stepping out of range of Turbulence."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Made small tweaks to Mesa’s Peacemaker pistol draw / holster animations."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"%'%'%'Archwing Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Auxiliary Power",},text={"*Buffed effect of Auxiliary Power’s Mod."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Smaller Archwing enemies now have a slightly larger hitbox."}},
        {tags={"Velocitus",},text={"*Increased Velocitus’ damage from 1200 to 1800."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Trinity","Protect",},text={"*Fixed the Nullifier Crewman’s protective bubble not removing Trinity’s Link."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Hive",},text={"*Fixed Nyx’s Chaos causing Hives in Hive Infestation to become invincible one Chaos expires."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed water in Dojo fountain turning black when players stand in the water."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed host migration issues occurring when picking up Syndicate Medallions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Syndicate Medallions disappearing during a host migration."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Mod search bar numerical entries causing issues as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/368500-typing-in-mod-search-field-causes-game-to-be-frozen-in-mod-station/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Miter",},text={"*Fixed Miter’s charge loop sound continuously playing after charge has been completed."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed lack of sound related to zoning issue in Corpus tileset room as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/341425-broken-sound-in-corpus-tileset-room/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Sabotage",},text={"*Fixed issue with objective marker and AI spawns in the Archwing Corpus sabotage tileset."}},
        {tags={"Lecta","Steel Meridian",},text={"*Fixed Steel Meridian Syndicate Missions not being selectable due to Star Chart being occupied by other factions."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed Dojo paint colors not properly showing up after being implemented."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed visual issue caused by player Inbox being forced to open on login during loading process."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed previewing multiple paint colors in Dojo causing a lock on the player’s camera."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed incorrect player stats appearing when viewing Warframe in Liset Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Mobile Defense",},text={"*Fixed visual issue with textures on Ice Planet Mobile Defense."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed issues with animation on Archwing primary weapons."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed grey square appearing at bottom of screen instead of text when picking up resources."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Mirage’s Eclipse Augment effects not working correctly when applied to other Warframes."}},
        {tags={"Liset","Relay",},text={"*Fixed issue that caused players to be stuck holding their melee weapon in an area where weapons are disabled. (ie - Liset, Relay)"}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*Fixed issue in Foundry where player cannot purchase additional inventory slots after having just purchased and filled inventory slots."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various localization issues."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed numerous holes and collisions."}},
        {tags={"Nekros",},text={"*Fixed an error caused by Nekros’ Desecrate."}},
        {tags={"Nova","Prime",},text={"*Fixed an error caused by Nova’s Molecular Prime."}},
        {tags={"Valkyr",},text={"*Fixed Valkyr’s Hysteria tagging dead enemies with its visual special effect."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Silva & Aegis","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Silva and Aegis losing texture when used by Mirage during Hall of Mirrors as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/365315-silva-aegis-skin-gets-messed-up-casting-hall-of-mirrors/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Mag","Nyx",},text={"*Fixed Nyx’s Mind Control causing Brood Mother Maggots to attack enemies after Mind Control ends."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed exploit that gave players infinite ammo provided they used the mouse scroll wheel as their weapon fire button."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage","Void",},text={"*Fixed the miniboss in Void Sabotage missions not spawning."}},
        {tags={"Nekros",},text={"*Fixed Infested Boiler’s spawned enemies improperly attacking players while under the effect of Nekros’ Shadows of the Dead."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being unable to pick up a Syndicate Medallion until another player in the mission picked it up first."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Mirage’s Prism never going away when cast outside of a Nullifier Crewman’s bubble, just before stepping into the bubble before the throw animation is complete."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Fixed issue occasionally caused by Mirage’s Hall of Mirrors."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed broken lighting effects on Syndicate Medallions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue caused by melee whip animations."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Alad V",},text={"*Fixed additional instances where Alad V’s Mind Control ability would cause issues for the player."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed not being able to swap the polarity of a slot on Kubrows and Sentinels."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed selecting an item in the Marketplace followed by fast-navigating to another terminal on the Liset causing the item you were previously looking at to become stuck on screen."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where items with no color customization will display as grey, instead of default colors."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed Zephyr’s Turbulence not being dispelled when entering a Nullifier Crewman’s shield."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Fixed potential issue in Survival game mode that prevented the spawning of new enemies."}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"*Fixed some weapons not correctly applying damage to Antimatter Drop as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/363806-novas-antimatter-drop-is-not-working-with-projectile-weapons/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"*Fixed some weapons with puncture damage registering too many hits on Antimatter Drop."}},
        {tags={"Nova","Prime",},text={"*Fixed Nova Prime’s Antimatter Drop not correctly applying unique visual effects to non-host players."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue with weapon skins not properly applying to weapons."}},
        {tags={"Invasion","Corpus Ship",},text={"*Fixed enemies not spawning properly on Corpus ship during Grineer Invasion."}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"*Fixed being unable to properly gift Void Keys."}},
        {tags={"Nekros",},text={"*Fixed Nullifier Crewman not properly deploying shield when resurrected by Nekros’ Shadows of the Dead."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Lato",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Regulator Pistols persisting on non-Mesa Warframes when switching loadouts in Arsenal. "}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
