["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/367247-hotfix-1571/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Void",},text={"*Void Mission rewards are no longer included in caches upon finding all 3. Please note we are working on a way to return this feature, but exploits prevent us from leaving it active at this time."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Clan","Research",},text={"*Fixed personal contributions to Dojo color Research ingredients not being properly counted in the Dojo if you had not found any ingredients during our previous Update."}},
        {tags={"Nova","Prime",},text={"*Fixed Nova’s Antimatter Drop being uncontrollable at Rank 1 as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/367119-nova-prime-antimatter-drop-broken/ this thread%]"}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.7"},
["date"]={"December 17th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/367069-update-1570-nova-prime/"},
["title"]={"Nova Prime"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Nova","Prime",},text={"%'%'%'Update 15.7: Nova Prime%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Nova","Prime",},text={"%'%'%'Nova Prime and the newest Prime Gear%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Nova","Prime",},text={"%'%'%'A torrent of offensive destruction, Nova Prime bursts into Prime Access.%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Nova","Soma Prime","Vasto Prime","Prime",},text={"Get Nova Prime Access now and make the newest Primes yours - instantly - including Nova Prime, Soma Prime and Vasto Prime. Plus, stock up on discounted Platinum and get Exclusive Gear available only through Prime Access!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Packages include:%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Nova","Prime",},text={"%'%'%'NEW NOVA PRIME WARFRAME%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Mag","Nova","Prime",},text={"Harness electromagnetic energy and command control with Nova Prime, the newest addition to the ranks of elite Prime Warframes."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"%'%'%'NEW PRIME WEAPONS%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Soma","Vasto Prime","Prime",},text={"Add the deadly Soma and Vasto Primes to your Arsenal."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"%'%'%'PRIME ACCESSORIES%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Nova","Volt","Prime",},text={"Modify your Warframe with the Pyra Prime Syandana and the Velorum Prime Sigil. Add Exclusive Nova Prime Avatars to your online presence! Speed up your in-game progress with 90-Day Affinity and Credit Boosters."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'DISCOUNTED PLATINUM%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Give your Platinum supply a boost at a great price."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"%[%https://warframe.com/prime-access Click here%] to learn more about Prime Access now!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Enemies%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"More Corpus and Grineer have been corrupted by the Void!"}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"*Corrupted Butcher has been added to the Orokin Void Mission types."}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"*Corrupted Rocket Bombard and Corrupted Nullifier have been added to T3 and T4 Void Missions"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Weapon Change%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Kohm",},text={"*Increased the Kohm’s weapon damage from 15 to 25."}},
        {tags={"Kohm",},text={"*Decreased the Kohm’s Ammo Consumption."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Nova","Prime","Hunt",},text={"You can begin to hunt for Nova Prime parts using our drop tables for guidance:  %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/172911-prime-gear-drop-locations/ drop table%]"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Corpus Nullifier shields will now decrease in size depending on incoming damage."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Players can no longer sell Large Restore Blueprints of any variety for credits."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Volt","Conclave","Antitoxin","Diamond Skin","Flame Repellent","Insulation","Lightning Rod","Resilient Focus","Shock Absorbers","Sure Footed","Focus",},text={"Adjusted Conclave rating for resistance Mods. The following Mods are now 15 Conclave points when fully ranked: Lightning Rod, Flame Repellent, Insulation, Shock Absorbers, Diamond Skin, Antitoxin, and Resilient Focus. Sure Footed is now 10 Conclave points."}},
},--misc end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Invasion",},text={"*Fixed Invasion Battle Pay not being correctly awarded to players. Please note we have retroactively given all ‘missed’ payments."}},
        {tags={"Redeemer",},text={"*Fixed the Redeemer playing the wrong sound when fired."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel","Sanctuary",},text={"*Fixed friendly fire damaging Sentinel’s Sanctuary shield."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed issue caused by destroying enemy drones in Archwing."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Defense",},text={"*Fixed Kubrows getting stuck on the Galleon Defense tileset."}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Resonance",},text={"*Fixed Banshee’s Resonance Augment Mod not functioning."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Shield Transference",},text={"*Fixed Mag’s Shield Transference Augment Mod not properly rotating with Mag as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/366159-shield-transferences-shield-does-not-respect-warframe-rotation/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Lato",},text={"*Fixed unique case of Mesa’s Regulator pistols still being usable after Peacemaker ends."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Archwing","Vacuum",},text={"*Fixed the Archwing’s vacuum attempting to pull in Mods you’ve already picked up."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed various collision issues in the Relay Station’s Mission Control room."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed screen never returning from blackout after using Mesa’s Peacemaker while falling into a pit."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being unable to purchase the Spearmint Scythes weapon skins from the Marketplace."}},
        {tags={"Deception","Grineer Shipyard",},text={"*Fixed elevator not functioning properly on Deception in Grineer Shipyard tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Tar-Mutalist MOA’s projectile still causing Radiation damage. Also removed area damage effect from attack."}},
        {tags={"Scoliac",},text={"*Fixed an infrequent lockup that would occur when using jump attacks with the Scoliac."}},
        {tags={"Lex Prime","Prime",},text={"*Added Orokin Ducat cost to Lex Prime Blueprint."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue that would occur when a player joins a Mission just as the host is going back to a lobby."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue with G3 that would cause Mission to be impossible to complete when players were spread out too far from one another."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed Feral Kubrow dropping Oxium."}},
        {tags={"Gammacor","Synoid Gammacor","Ruinous Extension",},text={"*Fixed the Ruinous Extension Mod not being compatible with the Gammacor and Synoid Gammacor. "}},
},--fixes end

},--update end

        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed issue where changing loadout in a Relay Station gives Tenno back their powers. Relays are a no power zone!"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Vacuum",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Archwing’s pickup vacuum that would cause items you couldn’t use to be destroyed, or would keep certain items around indefinitely."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue when selling the same item in multiple stacks."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed some holes in Relays that players could escape through."}},

        {tags={},text={"*Baro Ki’Teer, after complaining of some claustrophobia, will now stand a little higher on his own custom soap box."}},

{

},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Boar","Clan",},text={"*Fixed Dojo Obstacle Course leaderboards not properly holding all player times."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed issue caused by changing loadout when in the Relay."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed issues caused by logging out while stuck on loading screen when entering a Relay."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage","Scorch",},text={"*Fixed only Nullifier Crewman and Scorching Osprey spawning after the portal is destroyed in Orokin Sabotage."}},
        {tags={"Clan","Research",},text={"*Fixed elements of the Dojo’s color research UI panel not displaying correctly."}},
        {tags={"Cephalon Suda",},text={"*Fixed issue that would allow players to jump out of Cephalon Suda’s Syndicate room."}},
        {tags={"Lecta","Alert",},text={"*Fixed Alerts and Syndicate Daily Missions as being a selectable choice for Mission auto-repeat."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Medallions not appearing in Syndicate Daily Missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed UI issue that allowed for incorrectly priced Mod fusion."}},
},--fixes end

{
