        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed an issue with mods of similar effect not being equippable simultaneously (channeling and Sentinel mods). "}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed various crashes and issues with Relays (including forced logouts). "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with unfinished content appearing for purchase in the Market. "}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed an issue with pressing “I” to inspect causing issues on Companions (Kubrows/Sentinels) "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Spanish and Portuguese localization for the latest augment mods."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.6"},
["date"]={"December 11th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/363216-update-1560/"},
["title"]={"Tenno Reinforcements: Kohm and Tenno Relays"},
["image"]={"KohmSplashArt.jpg"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Tenno Reinforcement:%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Mag","Kohm",},text={"Kohm: The Kohm is a primary plasma shotgun with a unique held-trigger function. For every shot fired in rapid succession the Kohm releases an additional bolt, growing more lethal with every pull of the trigger!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"%'%'%'Tenno Relays are now open to all!%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Relay","Prime",},text={"These past few weeks many of our Founders, Prime Access supporters, and players with Mastery Rank 10+ have had the opportunity to help us test what our Relays were capable of handling. With our preliminary testing of Tenno Relays coming to a close we invite all Tenno to take part in our next phase, keeping in mind that Relays may still experience some adjustments over the coming weeks."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"Please be aware that there may still be some fine tuning that needs to be done in making Relay Stations a safe place for Tenno to congregate. Rest assured we will notify everyone immediately of any downtime Relays may require during this time. "}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Ash","Relay",},text={"If you discover any bugs during your exploration of these Relays please help us squash them by reporting all issues to the following: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/363241-relays-known-issues/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"To make Tenno Relays a little easier to navigate, here’s a list of currently available features that range from brand new content to tips that will help make your stay on the Relay a little more pleasant:"}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Relay Stations are available on many planets, and are a great way to meet other Tenno looking to complete Missions in that sector."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Players can create or join a squad while inside the Relay, then use the Relay’s Mission Control in the Northern Wing to start a mission."}},
        {tags={"Reflect",},text={"*Players can also alter their loadout in Mission Control, should they need a different build for a more specialized mission. These changes will reflect to other nearby Tenno."}},
        {tags={"Liset","Relay",},text={"*Each Syndicate has their own room on Tenno Relays, and are more than happy to trade their usual rewards in-person instead of through the Liset’s console."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Syndicate rooms each have a special representative that collects Medallions, a new Currency for earning Standing."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Various art and lighting updates have been made to Tenno Relays! Be sure to explore and take in the new look and explore each of the multiple floors available in the Concourse."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Various NPCs within Tenno Relays will be a little more lively now that they have animations."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"%'%'%'Void Trader%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Void","Relay",},text={"Baro Ki’Teer is a man of rare and luxurious tastes, willing to trade a wide variety of his acquired goods in exchange for the unique items that Tenno often discover when traveling the Void. While he is often in demand his appearance on a Relay is rare, heralded by Ducat Kiosks in the main Concourse."}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"*Players can trade in unused Prime parts for Orokin Ducats, the only currency that Baro Ki’Teer accepts."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Orokin Ducats can be traded to Baro Ki’Teer alongside a sum of credits for unique Mods, weapons, and cosmetic items."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Baro Ki’Teer’s presence on a Relay Station is random limited to only a few days."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Some of Baro Ki’Teer’s goods are tradable with other Tenno."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Darvo",},text={"%'%'%'Darvo’s Market%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Relay","Darvo",},text={"While exploring the Relay Tenno may discover Darvo’s Market, which is sadly not open for business at this time. Even still, Darvo is never one to turn down a good opportunity to advertise! Be sure to say hello should you stumble upon his developing storefront."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Syndicate Medallions%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Medallions are now available on Syndicate Missions. Medallions act like their own currency, similar to Credits. Each Syndicate’s Medallion may have their own unique look, but regardless of their appearance all Medallions count toward the same currency pool: Standing! "}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"When you’re ready to turn in Medallions simply head to your Tenno Relay of choice, visit your Syndicate trader and select how many you wish to turn in. Different ranks of Medallions can provide greater Standing than others; so keep an eye out for Medallions and plan ahead when you spend them, Tenno."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Warframe Mod Augments:%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={},text={"We’ve been working with our Design Council to put more Augments together, as well as adding some of our own! The following Mod Augments are now available through the Syndicate Offerings:"}},
        {tags={"Ash","Seeking Shuriken",},text={"*Ash: Shuriken: %'%'%'Seeking Shuriken:%'%'%' Hits expose weaknesses on enemies, reducing their armor by 35PERZENTO/45PERZENTO/55PERZENTO/70PERZENTO for 2/4/6/8 seconds"}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Resonance",},text={"*Banshee: Sonar: %'%'%'Resonance:%'%'%' Killing an enemy by shooting the weak spot will trigger another sonar for 50PERZENTO/65PERZENTO/80PERZENTO/100PERZENTO of remaining duration."}},
        {tags={"Ember","Fire Fright",},text={"*Ember: Fire Blast: %'%'%'Fire Fright:%'%'%' Each tick has a 40PERZENTO/60PERZENTO/80PERZENTO/100PERZENTO chance for a panic proc."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Surging Dash",},text={"*Excalibur: Slash Dash: %'%'%'Surging Dash:%'%'%' Each enemy hit has a chance to increase the melee counter by 1/2/3/4."}},
        {tags={"Frost","Ice Wave Impedance",},text={"*Frost: Ice Wave: %'%'%'Ice Wave Impedance:%'%'%' Leaves a frozen trail that will slow down enemies that walk over it for 4/6/8/10 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Hydroid","Tidal Impunity",},text={"*Hydroid: Tidal Surge: %'%'%'Tidal Impunity:%'%'%' Clears procs and grants proc immunity for x seconds for yourself and allies that come in contact with it for 3/4/5/6 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Rift Torrent",},text={"*Limbo: Rift Surge: %'%'%'Rift Torrent:%'%'%' Damage multiplier is increased by 5PERZENTO/10PERZENTO/15PERZENTO/20PERZENTO for each enemy in the rift."}},
        {tags={"Loki","Irradiating Disarm",},text={"*Loki: Radial Disarm: %'%'%'Irradiating Disarm:%'%'%' Enemies will be affected by radiation for 3/6/9/12 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Protect","Shield Transference",},text={"*Mag: Shield Polarize: %'%'%'Shield Transference:%'%'%' Creates a small shield dome around Mag that protects for 15PERZENTO/25PERZENTO/35PERZENTO/50PERZENTO of depleted enemy shields."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage","Total Eclipse",},text={"*Mirage: Eclipse: %'%'%'Total Eclipse:%'%'%' The effect applies to nearby allies within a 3/3/4/5 meter radius for 25PERZENTO/50PERZENTO/75PERZENTO/100PERZENTO effect."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Muzzle Flash",},text={"*Mesa: Shooting Gallery: %'%'%'Muzzle Flash:%'%'%' Shooting Gallery does a blinding burst within 5/6/7/8 meters for 4/5/6/7 seconds before switching players."}},
        {tags={"Nekros","Despoil",},text={"*Nekros: Desecrate: %'%'%'Despoil:%'%'%' Desecrate no longer consumes energy, but consumes 80/70/60/50 health instead."}},
        {tags={"Nova","Nyx","Antimatter Absorb",},text={"*Nova: Antimatter Drop: %'%'%'Antimatter Absorb:%'%'%' Antimatter Drop absorbs enemy bullets, increasing its damage when it explodes. 2/3/4/5 meter absorb bubble radius."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Mind Freak",},text={"*Nyx: Mind Control: %'%'%'Mind Freak:%'%'%' Mind controlled target does 60PERZENTO/120PERZENTO/200PERZENTO/300PERZENTO increased damage."}},
        {tags={"Oberon","Hallowed Eruption",},text={"*Oberon: Hallowed Ground: %'%'%'Hallowed Eruption:%'%'%' Casting a second blessed ground will consume the first one dealing all the damage remaining in a burst. 30PERZENTO/40PERZENTO/50PERZENTO/60PERZENTO proc chance on burst."}},
        {tags={"Rhino","Iron Shrapnel",},text={"*Rhino: Iron Skin: %'%'%'Iron Shrapnel:%'%'%' Detonate Iron Skin dealing 40PERZENTO/60PERZENTO/80PERZENTO/100PERZENTO of its remaining health as puncture damage. 30PERZENTO/40PERZENTO/50PERZENTO/60PERZENTO proc chance."}},
        {tags={"Saryn","Regen","Regenerative Molt",},text={"*Saryn: Molt: %'%'%'Regenerative Molt:%'%'%' After casting Molt, Saryn regenerates a percentage of health over time. Lasts 8 seconds, with healing occurring every 2 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Trinity","Abating Link",},text={"*Trinity: Link: %'%'%'Abating Link:%'%'%' Reduces armor by 15PERZENTO/20PERZENTO/25PERZENTO/30PERZENTO on enemies targeted by Link."}},
        {tags={"Valkyr","Swing Line",},text={"*Valkyr: Rip Line: %'%'%'Swing Line:%'%'%' After using Rip Line, the next 1/2/3/4 rip lines while remaining airborne will have no energy cost."}},
        {tags={"Vauban","Repelling Bastille",},text={"*Vauban: Bastille: %'%'%'Repelling Bastille:%'%'%' When at max capacity, has a 50PERZENTO/65PERZENTO/80PERZENTO/100PERZENTO chance to repel new enemies that enter the radius."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Shocking Speed",},text={"*Volt: Speed: %'%'%'Shocking Speed:%'%'%' Enemies touched while sprinting under the effects of speed will take 75/100/125/175 electrical damage with a guaranteed proc."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Zephyr","Jet Stream",},text={"*Zephyr: Turbulence: %'%'%'Jet Stream:%'%'%' Turbulence increases movement by 15PERZENTO/20PERZENTO/25PERZENTO/30PERZENTO and projectile speed by 50PERZENTO/65PERZENTO/80PERZENTO/100PERZENTO for Zephyr and her allies."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Game Mode:%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Hijack","Europa",},text={"%'%'%'Corpus Hijack is now live on Sorath-Europa!%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Lua","Europa","Recover",},text={"The Corpus have begun salvaging their crashed ships on Europa for valuable goods, loading up heavy all-terrain vehicles with cargo for shipment off planet. Their most treasured shipment of recovered goods has just been readied for extraction, and it’s your job to make sure those secrets go to Tenno hands."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Ship",},text={"*Locate the Corpus shipment, then guide it safely to extraction through the frozen remains of a Corpus ship’s crash site."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Ship",},text={"*The Corpus shipment’s vehicle has been re-routed to draw power from Warframe Shields. Defending the shipment (and one another) from weapon’s fire is the key to victory."}},
},--misc end
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Polychrome (Dojo Painting) is here! How to get started? Read on!"}},
        {tags={"Research",},text={"*Research colours in the Tenno Research Room. Each colour requires Pigments to complete. Pigments are dropped by a listed enemy only during the time that Research is selected."}},
        {tags={"Research",},text={"*Once your colour is researched and contributions complete, use the “Paint” option for a given room and contribute resources to begin the painting process! "}},
        {tags={"Void","Derelict",},text={"*Orokin Void and Derelicts dynamic music is now live! Make sure to have your music enabled when listening for our latest update to Orokin Void and Derelict tilesets, or if you’d like to hear the WIP preview check out our post in the Developer Workshop subforum: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/340678-new-music-preview-derelict-void/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Market Batch Purchasing is here! Now you can buy in quantities from the Market instead of 1 at a time!"}},
        {tags={"Boar","Clan",},text={"*The Dojo Obstacle Course now has leaderboards!"}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Updated Mesa’s Longhorn helmet icon in Marketplace."}},
        {tags={"Sniper Ammo Mutation",},text={"*Players can now upgrade Rifle, Arrow, Shotgun and Sniper Ammo Mutation Mods from max Rank 3 to max Rank 5 with increased effectiveness."}},
        {tags={},text={"*‘Dangle’ cosmetics will now use the same collision detection as Syandanas."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Lato",},text={"*Mesa’s Regulator pistols will now need to be holstered before her Peacemaker Ability is considered to be finished / deactivated."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Mutalist Navigation Coordinate now has an updated icon."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Lowered the damage of Tar-Mutalist MOA’s tar damage over time effect."}},
        {tags={"Defense",},text={"*Defense objectives are now be immune to Tar-Mutalist MOA’s tar damage."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Changed Tar-Mutalist MOA’s damage type from Radiation to Corrosive."}},
        {tags={"Protect",},text={"*Reduced the number of shots required to shrink the Nullifier Crewman’s protective shield."}},
        {tags={"Conclave","Natural Talent",},text={"*The Natural Talent Mod has had its Conclave Rating adjusted to 10 at max range."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Large Team Ammo Restore items now give 33PERZENTO more ammo than Medium Restore items."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Void",},text={"*Addition additional terrain elements to Orokin Void Defense."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Rare Loot Crate scan requirement has been reduced to 5, and Ultra Rare has been reduced to 3."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"%'%'%'Archwing Changes:%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Dual Decurion",},text={"%'%'%'Dual Decurion Buff%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*The latest Archwing Reinforcement will be receiving boosts on stats for damage and clip size."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Firing rate has been changed from semi-automatic to automatic."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Velocitus",},text={"%'%'%'Velocitus Buff%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Increased Magnetic Damage from 500 to 1200"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased Critical Multiplier from 2 to 3"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Vacuum",},text={"%'%'%'Innate Vacuum for Loot pickup%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Flow","Vacuum",},text={"*All players in Archwing game modes will now have a default radial ‘vacuum’ effect for loot, making pick-ups easier to grab. This should also improve the general flow of movement in Archwing Missions."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Interception",},text={"%'%'%'Archwing Interception Rewards%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Interception",},text={"*Interception Rewards are now exclusively Archwing Mods. No other mods will be presented in the Reward Table. "}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Stunlock Reduced%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Overall stunlock intensity from enemy attacks reduced in combat."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Buffed Mods%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Critical Chance Mods for Archwing melee and rifles have been buffed."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Health/Shield/Armor Mods for Archwing have been buffed."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"%'%'%'Overall Archwing Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Warframe Stats and Mods no longer carry over onto Archwing performance. All Warframes will now perform equally well while piloting an Archwing, eliminating the need to use a specific Warframe for optimum Space Ninja performance."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*All Archwings now have different base Health/Shield/Armor stats that can be modded (with the newly buffed Mods)."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Warframe Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Mag","Saryn","Seeker",},text={"*Saryn’s Molt now removes Brood Mother Maggots, Nanite Swarms, Seeker Latchers and Leeches."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed visual overlap on some objects within the Dojo."}},
        {tags={"Void","Clan","Research",},text={"*Fixed players being able to see into the Void when in the Dojo’s Tenno Research Lab."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the Marketplace not properly updated item quantity when player enters a new amount they wish to purchase."}},
        {tags={"Derelict",},text={"*Fixed several locked doors in the Orokin Derelicts showing up in green."}},
        {tags={"Liset","Relay",},text={"*Fixed player Liset decorations floating in midair in the docking station of the Relay Station."}},
        {tags={"Relay",},text={"*Fixed players waiting longer than necessary to enter a Relay when in a party."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Fixed players not being able to see any visual effects when picking up an oxygen tank in Survival."}},
        {tags={"Clan","Relay",},text={"*Fixed players being unable to enter a Dojo through the Clan menu when in a Relay."}},
        {tags={"Defense",},text={"*Fixed enemies only appearing in small groups of 2-3 when playing on Ice Planet Defense."}},
        {tags={"Trinity",},text={"*Fixed enemies targeted by Trinity’s Energy Vampire becoming stuck in an animation loop."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue that would occur from leaving a squad at the exact moment the host disbanded the squad."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed certain Syndicate Mods not functioning correctly, or not displaying new buffed modifiers as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/361795-broken-syndicate-mods-thread/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed load time delays during Archwing Mission deployment cinematics."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed all visual issues related to Warframe Reddit thread: %[%http://www.reddit.com/r/Warframe/comments/2oxq2n/im_sorry_once_again_ude_killerkarpfen_i_have/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Shooting Gallery buffing enemy players in Dark Sector PvP."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed players becoming stuck in Mesa’s Peacemaker after repeated use of the Ability."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed Ash’s Bladestorm targeting enemies immune to Abilities after the initial target is killed."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Sabotage",},text={"*Fixed some Archwing weapons dealing no damage to Sabotage Mission objective."}},
        {tags={"Liset","Foundry",},text={"*Fixed Liset Foundry flashing an update for players staying on the Liset during a content Update."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed the Tenno Lab not appearing on the minimap of the Clan Dojo."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5.9"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"December 8th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/361470-hotfix-1559/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Mesa’s Peacemaker will now automatically target enemies that enter her range when holding down the trigger button. Ramp up time will not reset when all nearby enemies are killed."}},
        {tags={},text={"*The Drahk Master will now only steal your weapon if you have a pistol or rifle equipped."}},
        {tags={"Vauban",},text={"*Sent Nullifier Crewmen into Cerberus to help reinforce the Corpus forces there."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Adjusted spawn rates for the new Infested enemies in some of the earlier Missions."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Reduced stun rate of various NPC weapons used in Archwing Missions."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Defense","Interception",},text={"*Fixed some cases where the last reward shown in a defense or interception mission was not given to some players."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Fixed crash that would occur when Mirage used Hall of Mirrors with a Torrid equipped as a client."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed players not seeing Dojo decorations when entering a Dojo."}},
        {tags={"Invasion",},text={"*Fixed Invasion Missions not counting toward Battle Pay when quota for Invasion is completed when mid-Mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed chat connection problems from machines with various operating system services disabled."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issues with gray square appearing in place of obtained item details (at the bottom of the screen)."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Oberon","Lato",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Regulator pistols not being removed upon deactivation if the player has no other weapons (e.g. stolen by Beastmaster)."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Nyx","Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"*Fixed Mesa becoming stuck in Peacemaker while being mind controlled by Mutalist Alad V."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various crash-related issues."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various localization issues related to Mods and Mastery Sigils."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5.8"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"December 5th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/359419-hotfix-1558/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Emotes are now able to be interrupted by other emotes."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Alad V",},text={"*Infested Alad V’s mind control ability should no longer attack the players in a bleed-out state and should instead return to Alad V."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Enemies that spawn from enemies already inside of Limbo’s Rift will now also be considered within Limbo’s Rift (eg: Boilers)."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Made various animation tweaks to the Kubrow Charge Ability."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Mission nodes will now always display the specific mission type during an Alert / Event when players highlight a node affected by an Alert / Event."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Lato",},text={"*Made various audio tweaks to Mesa’s Regulator pistols, in addition to some visual tweaks to Peacemaker’s effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Optimized the performance of beam weapons when fired at groups of enemies."}},
        {tags={"Glaive",},text={"*Improved Glaive ricochet targeting."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Trinity",},text={"*Trinity:"}},
        {tags={"Trinity",},text={"**Changes to Energy Vampire are being made as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/357844-trinity-energy-vampire this thread%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Damage dealt is now direct-to-health damage instead of Slash (ignoring armor)."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Damage is now only scaled off of HP instead of HP + Shields."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Damage will scale off of their current HP or ¼ of their max, whichever is greater."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Damage will always be at least 5 HP per pulse."}},
        {tags={},text={"**You will no longer be able to cast on targets that won’t take damage from it; this specifically applies during the invulnerability phases of certain bosses."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Multiple Trinities can no longer drain energy from a single target simultaneously."}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Nyx",},text={"**Players can no longer gain energy while in Nyx’s Absorb or Mesa’s Peacemaker."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a number of issues with NVIDIA® Optimus™ and DirectX 11."}},
        {tags={"Gammacor",},text={"*Fixed the non-Syndicate Gammacor being a tradeable weapon."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"*Fixed various issues that Mutalist Alad V’s mind control power would cause on player Warframe Abilities."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"*Fixed various issues with Mutalist Alad V’s mind control power affecting players that are mid-air when hit."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Alad V",},text={"*Fixed an issue when a player uses Alt-F4 when affected by Alad V’s mind control power, preventing Alad V from using the collar for the rest of the fight"}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed Dojo decorations not appearing immediately when you first load into a Dojo."}},
        {tags={"Gammacor",},text={"*Fixed Gammacor’s laser not appearing when fired."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed newly spawned enemies being able to attack Limbo when he is in inside the Rift, but not Cataclysm."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players not being able to trade Arcane Helmets."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various issues with opening / sorting through Gear inventory."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various issues on Ice Planet, including zoning and elevator related problems."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various improper localization on Mastery Rank 15 / 16 test completion."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed UI displaying friends in an incorrect mission, if their mission was on a node with a simultaneous event occurring."}},
        {tags={"Gammacor","Barrel Diffusion","Lethal Torrent","Seeker",},text={"*Fixed issue with Gammacor caused by using the Barrel Diffusion, Lethal Torrent and Seeker Mods all at once."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various crash-related issues making space a safer place for us all."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue where using a Thrown weapon when on a ledge would cause Tenno to step off the ledge."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players being unable to scroll beyond 54 Warframe slots."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"*Fixed Mutalist Alad V’s mind control message not appearing in appropriate language for host."}},
        {tags={"Rhino","Prime","Dex",},text={"*Fixed Rhino Prime Codex entry showing as not properly localized to German."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed Daedelus Shoulder Plates not attaching to Mesa properly."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*Fixed both Attackers and Defenders seeing a ‘Mission Complete’ at the end of a Solar Rail Conflict match regardless of outcome."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5.7.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"December 3rd, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/357864-hotfix-155715571/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Warframe launching/minimizing in full-screen by reverting prior DirectX 11 fixes. "}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed Conclaves being unplayable."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5.7"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"December 3rd, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/357864-hotfix-1557/"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Emotes are now live for use in missions! With the Stress Testing of ‘Tenno Hubs’ progressing well, we have now added functioning emotes (host/client) to the game which can be activated via your ‘Gear’ menu."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added emote for ‘Follow’!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Syndicate Weapons can now be traded! Players may now trade ONLY unranked and unused Syndicate weapons with one another."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Made various Localization updates."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Infested Tar Mutalist MOA, Swarm Mutalist MOA, Boiler and Brood Mother have all been added to regular Infested missions throughout the star chart!"}},
        {tags={"Nightmare Mode","Quest",},text={"*Removed Nightmare Mode from final quest mission in Patient Zero."}},
        {tags={"Boar",},text={"*Accessing the Gear / Emote wheels has now been changed! Using a keyboard players can access their inventory and emotes by holding down the appropriate key (defaulted to Q), while anyone using a controller / gamepad will be able to toggle the menu."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Weapon Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Braton Prime","Prime",},text={":%'%'%'Braton Prime:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased Physical Damage from 25 to 35"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased Status Chance from 10.0PERZENTO to 20.0PERZENTO"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased Crit Multiplier from 1.5 to 2.0"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased Fire Rate"}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Increased Magazine Size from 50 to 75"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Decreased Ammo Capacity to 375"}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed emotes allowing players to move freely when used during an Ability. Emotes will no longer play when a Warframe is in an already existing animation."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Interception",},text={"*Fixed Archwing Interception missions force extracting players still within mission bounds."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Peacemaker displaying ‘Invalid Target’ where there are no enemies nearby to fire at."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Mirage’s Eclipse removing her festive holiday skin."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed issue caused by falling off the edge during the Mastery Rank 15 test, causing players to be caught in a teleport loop."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Peacemaker not applying camera shake effect when firing."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Peacemaker shooting toward player crosshair instead of directly at enemies."}},
        {tags={"Vauban",},text={"*Fixed performance issues that would occur in long sessions where Vauban%'s Vortex was often used, resulting in FPS issues."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5.6"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"December 2nd, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/357182-hotfix-1556/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Swarm Mutalist MOA’s nanite cloud visual effects will now only become thicker when attaching to players, not enemies as well."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Made ambient Archwing music a little quieter."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Added controller / gamepad support for Liset customization screen."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various visual improvements to ‘Tenno Hub’ concourse skybox."}},
        {tags={},text={"*<%s>Consumables and Gear will no longer be usable when in the ‘Tenno Hub’.<%/s> Coming later."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Various adjustments have been made to AI footstep audio."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Missions that have been aborted or failed no longer reward players with Syndicate Standing."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Infested Brood Mother not being able to get up after being knocked down."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Infested Boiler infinitely spawning enemies."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed Excalibur’s Radial Javelin not stunning enemies who survive the attack."}},
        {tags={"Gammacor",},text={"*Fixed Gammacor’s beam visual effect persisting when using melee during a slide."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Fixed Infested Maggots latching on to invisible targets, or targets that are too far away from their leap."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed incorrect calculation on Fire Rate for charged weapons when equipping Mods that affect Fire Rate."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various broken objective markers on Ice Planet tileset."}},
        {tags={"Mobile Defense",},text={"*Fixed enemies shooting through crates surrounding Mobile Defense console on Ice Planet tileset."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Fixed error caused by aborting a Survival mission mid-mission when not hosting."}},
        {tags={"Gammacor",},text={"*Fixed Gammacor’s continuous fire sometimes using default energy color when players stop running while shooting."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed holes in Archwing Corpus tileset as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/355930-archwing-corpus-tilesets-hole/?p=3966508#entry3966508 this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Velocitus",},text={"*Fixed Velocitus weapon fire sound ending prematurely, also corrected various weapon fire animations."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed navigation bars in the Codex overlapping when backing out of a selection under Enemies."}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Mutalist Alad V","Recover",},text={"*Fixed players getting stuck in the air when recovering from Mutalist Alad V’s control."}},
        {tags={"New Loka",},text={"*Fixed issue with sprinting being activated after entering the New Loka Syndicate pond in the Hubs. The fountain of sprint is no more!"}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5.5"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"December 1st, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/356440-hotfix-1555/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'“Tenno Hubs” Stress Test:%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Relay","Prime",},text={"Vesper Relay stress testing continues! “Tenno Hubs” are now available to any player that has supported development through Prime Access, as well as any player that is Mastery Rank 10 and up! Remember:"}},
        {tags={"Venus","Relay",},text={"*Eligible Tenno can access the Vesper Relay on Venus."}},
        {tags={},text={"*There may be some hitches, please be patient as we continue to fine-tune hubs! "}},
},--misc end
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Clan","Liset",},text={"Holiday decorations have been added to the Dojo and Liset! Check out some of our festive additions to the Marketplace: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/356307-new-winter-bundle-and-free-liset-decorations/ here%]."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Weapon Changes:%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Paracyst","Gammacor","Synoid Gammacor","Redeemer",},text={"The Paracyst, Gammacor, Synoid Gammacor, and Redeemer have all received stat increases as indicated below:"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Redeemer",},text={"%'%'%'Redeemer:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Base Slash damage increased from 40 to 60."}},
        {tags={},text={"*“Gunshot” damage increased in range and deadliness, from 15 to 30."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Gammacor","Synoid Gammacor",},text={"%'%'%'Gammacor/Synoid Gammacor:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased damage dealt in beam per second."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased fire rate of beam."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Reduced ammo consumption."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Synoid Damage: Now 210 Magnetic."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Gammacor",},text={"*Gammacor Damage: Now 50 Magnetic"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Synoid Status/Second: 20PERZENTO"}},
        {tags={"Gammacor",},text={"*Gammacor Status/Second: 5PERZENTO"}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={"*Synoid Magazine: 75"}},
        {tags={"Mag","Gammacor",},text={"*Gammacor Magazine: 50"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Synoid Crit Multiplier/Chance: 2x/10PERZENTO"}},
        {tags={"Gammacor",},text={"*Gammacor Crit Multiplier/Chance: 1.5x/5PERZENTO."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Paracyst",},text={"%'%'%'Paracyst:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased burst fire rate from 200 to 500 as well as reduced the delay between delay between burst rounds."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased Critical Chance to 5.0PERZENTO"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased Critical Damage Multiplier from 1.5 to 2.0"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Reduced required ammunition for secondary fire."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased rate of fire for secondary fire."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Significantly decreased the charge time required on secondary fire."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Tightened controller / gamepad timing for using the crouch / dodge maneuver."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Decreased the amount of explosive damage dealt by Tar-Mutalist Moa’s projectiles."}},
        {tags={"Hive",},text={"*Increased the radius of Infested Hive ambient sounds."}},
        {tags={"Redeemer",},text={"*Updated animations on the Redeemer finishing attacks."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Updated animations on new Thrown weapon combos."}},
        {tags={"Mobile Defense",},text={"*Updated map navigation to upper areas of Ice Planet, Mobile Defense that was previously unavailable."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added extra security terminals to Ice Planet tileset to help prevent players becoming trapped in any rooms."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Exiting the Tenno hubs should no longer trigger an End of Match screen."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Removed ally markers from various NPCs located in the Tenno hub."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Adjusted the timing on Tenno emotes."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Weapons that have secondary fire abilities can no longer use their primary fire while the secondary is active. This fix affects weapons that require the secondary fire button to be held down."}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Nyx’s Mind Control is now a toggable ability."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Updated artwork and backdrop in Tenno Hub Hangar."}},
        {tags={},text={"*When opening the Gear or Emote menus the previously used item / emote will automatically be highlighted."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Ballistic Battery effects showing as being used when activating the ability."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Shooting Gallery buff animation pathing incorrectly when standing near a wall."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an error that would occur when ranking up a Syndicate playing in Russian."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*Fixed an error on Dark Sector battlepay that inadvertently caused Defender information to be erased once there was no reserve pay available."}},
        {tags={"Corrosive Projection",},text={"*Fixed Infested Swarm Armor buff overriding player’s Corrosive Projection Aura."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed uncommon issues with players falling out of Ice Planet tileset."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed hole in Archwing Corpus Trench Run tileset as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/355930-archwing-corpus-tilesets-hole/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the main menu occasionally becoming blocked by other text elements on screen (ie - objectives, player names, markers)"}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed Tar-Mutalist Moa’s goo damaging Limbo when he is in the rift."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed pathing on enemies walking through Ice Planet tileset ship tunnels."}},
        {tags={"Paracyst",},text={"*Fixed Paracyst not using burst fire animation when fired."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Mesa","Nyx",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Peacemaker, Nyx’s Psychic Bolts, and Limbo’s Cataclysm affecting enemies that are immune to Tenno abilities."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed weapons with fire rate below 1 not accurately displaying stat changes when Mods are applied."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where several weapon types did not have a proper slide-attack event hooked up, resulting in a ‘broken’ feel when attempting to use advanced maneuvers."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed an issue where Mesa’s Alt Helmet was given, for free, to anyone who crafted the frame or purchased her independently. Helmets acquired this way are no longer in inventories."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5.4"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 29th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/354796-hotfix-1554/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Made Hub use FOV attenuation instead of override so user setting still works"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Optimized Hub servers to allow more concurrent players"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Removed unfinished Nullifier Spaceman from spawn tables"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added sound effect for weapons being jammed"}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a several client-side crashes when hanging out in Hubs"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Removed some hitches and optimized memory usage for connecting to Hubs"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed cloth physics"}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed some sound problems with Mesa’s Ballistic Battery and Shooting Gallery"}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 28th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/354003-hotfix-1553/"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Venus","Relay",},text={"*Tenno Hubs: Players that are Founders, Mastery Rank 15 or higher can now can access our Player Hub stress test the Vesper Relay available on Venus. Please keep in mind this test does not represent the final version of the hubs, and we appreciate the help participating in the testing process! A few reminders:"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Make sure your Matchmaking is set to ‘Solo’"}},
        {tags={},text={"**There may be some hitches, please be patient!"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Be mindful of falling off of ledges! "}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Defense",},text={"*Tar-Mutalist Moa’s slow goo will no longer affect Defense objectives"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Infested minions will no longer make sounds after dying."}},
        {tags={"Quest","Lotus",},text={"*Reduced frequency of Lotus transmission notifications. Lotus will now remind players about unstarted quests once every three days."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Multiple NPCs can now use the zipline at once."}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"*Renamed Infested Alad V Nav Coordinate to Mutalist Alad V Nav Coordinate."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed leveling up and Power Strength Mods making Mesa’s Shatter Shield damage reduction less effective."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Interception",},text={"*Fixed issue in Archwing Interception that would cause enemy team to gain points when owning no territories."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Mag",},text={"*Fixed Infested Brood Mother Maggots not detaching from Limbo once he enters the Rift."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"*Fixed Mutalist Alad V’s powers affecting players in Limbo’s Rift, when Alad V is not in the Rift."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Infested jumping off level to their death after a host migration as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/350502-infested-mass-suicide-glitch/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed an error causing a false entry in the Codex"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Hive",},text={"*Fixed an issue in Hive Infestation that caused Excalibur’s Radial Javelin to cease functioning."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Clients not counting achievements that track deaths due to damage dealt."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a rare instance of item keys falling through the floor."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Void",},text={"*Fixed enemy drones getting stuck on objects / running in circles. This should fix the common cases in Void Defense where Orokin Drones get stuck at spawn."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed enemies aiming at your actual position if they cannot see the player."}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"*Fixed some terrain geography that was causing Mutalist Alad V to get stuck."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed crash in the new launcher when when pushing play button."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed error caused by Grineer Scorpions."}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed placement of Dendra Armor on Mesa."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed numerous unusual AI navigation issues."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 27th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/353150-hotfix-1551/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Cache Optimization thresholds reduced to not occur as frequently. The launcher will now only Optimize Cache if approximately 1/3rd of the data is in need of optimization."}},
        {tags={"Gammacor",},text={"*Made additional tweaks to Gammacor firing sounds."}},
        {tags={"Glaxion","Redeemer",},text={"*Added in stealth takedown sounds for the Redeemer when used on enemies frozen via the Glaxion."}},
        {tags={"Dex","Astral Twilight",},text={"*Removed unreleased/unfinished mods from the Codex (Vermillion Storm/Astral Twilight)."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Nekros","Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed Nekros’ Shadows of the Dead ability when spawning Kubrows. Kubrows spawned now have the correct visual effects and will die once Shadows of the Dead has expired."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Mag",},text={"*Fixed an issue where Brood Mother Maggots stayed on players that enter Limbo’s Rift."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with missing back buttons for Dioramas/messages which would require players to reload game."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Interception",},text={"*Fixed players seeing two ‘C’ points during Archwing Interception after a host migration."}},
        {tags={"Nekros","Valkyr",},text={"*Fixed energy efficiency Mods not applying to Nekros’ Desecrate and Valkyr’s Rip Line."}},
        {tags={"Mobile Defense",},text={"*Fixed enemies not spawning as frequently on Ice Planet Mobile Defense missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Infested Ancients not melee attacking players."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed transmutations not working when using more than one type of Mod."}},
        {tags={"Quest","Eris",},text={"*Fixed error that would occur when attempting to select Patient Zero quest through World State Window if player had not yet unlocked Eris."}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Capture",},text={"*Fixed Warframe Ability timers ceasing to update after players have been captured / released by Alad V’s collar."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"*Fixed an issue with players getting hurt by Mutalist Alad V attacks while in Limbo’s Rift."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Fixed rewards from aborted Alert missions appearing in future Mission Results screens. This used to occur after a player would abort an Alert and then proceeded to start a new mission."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 27th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/353150-hotfix-1551/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Increased the range on Tar-Mutalist Moa’s ‘slow goo’ deactivation once destroyed."}},
        {tags={"Skana Prime","Prime","Bright Purity",},text={"*The Bright Purity Augment Mod can now be used on Skana Prime."}},
        {tags={"Gammacor",},text={"*Made various sound tweaks to Gammacor shots."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Event name will now display on star chart node for Event Alerts."}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"*Mutalist Alad V can no longer be stun locked with electric damage procs."}},
        {tags={"Vauban",},text={"*Vauban’s Ability grenades have received their own unique texture!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Hiding Beep Boop Paint Bot, wasn%'t meant to be live yet! Those of you with a fresh coat of sneak-peek paint, enjoy!"}},
        {tags={"Gammacor",},text={"*Added new hip fire poses to the Gammacor."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a hole on Ice Planet that players could fall into."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed UI Codex dioramas displaying unintended elements of the UI."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Fixed Patient Zero quest not completing for some players."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a positioning error on Warframe Launcher"}},
        {tags={"Mobile Defense",},text={"*Fixed floating NPC spawn points on Ice Planet Mobile Defense missions"}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Fixed Mesa’s Peacemaker costing zero energy to use, in addition to power allowing players to still move during bleedout."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed enemy pathing that would cause enemies to sometimes to leap into pits to their death."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Grineer Asteroid",},text={"*Fixed Infested Ancients getting stuck at spawn points on Grineer Asteroid Defense tileset."}},
        {tags={"Dual Decurion","Clan","Research",},text={"*Fixed Dual Decurion blueprint appearing in Clan Tech, removed blueprint from Clan Tech Research. This is a Market blueprint for you to craft once you obtain the parts!"}},
        {tags={"Gammacor","Lato","Dex",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Gammacor appearing in the codex as a duplicate ‘Lato’ entry."}},
        {tags={"Helios",},text={"*Fixed issue with Helios not scanning properly."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.5"},
["date"]={"November 27th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/352963-mesa-update/"},
["title"]={"Mesa Update"},
["image"]={"Mesa Update.jpg"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={"Incursion",},text={"%'%'%'Operation: Mutalist Incursions"}},
        {tags={},text={"Stop the Infestation!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Alad V",},text={"A message from Alad V:"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'“Inhabitants of the Origin System! It is my singular pleasure to announce that today is the first day of a new empire. An empire populated not by Corpus, not by Grineer and certainly not by the Tenno. No, the Mutalist Empire will be populated by you. A glorious new you, baptised by Mutalist flesh. You’ve called this a disease, an infestation? I call it Unity. One army under me. Welcome you to your new species.”%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Alad V",},text={"– Alad V"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Incursion",},text={"%[%[File:Operation-Mutalist-Incursions-splash.jpg|frame|center%]%]"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Incursion",},text={"%'%'%'Mutalist Incursions:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*The Infested are taking over areas of the Solar System. You must try to stop them, Tenno!"}},
        {tags={"Bounty","Lotus",},text={"*You will receive an Inbox message from the Lotus with instructions on how to earn the Bounty for four different Infested enemies – Tar Mutalist MOAs, Swarm Mutalist MOAs, Brood Mothers and Boilers."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Infested Alerts will begin appearing in the Solar System. Visit the areas of the Solar System that are under attack to take down the Infested and earn the required amount of enemy kills for each of the four Bounties."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'WHAT YOU SHOULD KNOW%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Bounty",},text={"*Each Infested you take down will contribute a point to your Bounty totals."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Required totals:"}},
        {tags={},text={":Tar Mutalist MOA: 100"}},
        {tags={},text={":Swarm Mutalist MOA: 100"}},
        {tags={},text={":Brood Mother: 50"}},
        {tags={},text={":Boiler: 50"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Bounty",},text={"*Upon completing each Bounty, you will receive a Reward."}},
        {tags={"Ember",},text={"*This event is on now and ends on Wednesday, December 3 at 12 PM EST."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'REWARDS%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"Complete all four Bounties to unlock access a special Survival Mission! How long can you Survive? Make it past 30 minutes and you’ll be rewarded with a New Puncture Mod Set!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Boar","Survival","Clan",},text={"Clans can compete on this Survival mission for the leaderboards! The top 3 clans in each tier will receive a Trophy."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"The Tethra’s Doom Mods are also available as drops in this event within the Survival Mission."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Alad V",},text={"Tenno…the more familiar you become with these enemies, the better you’ll fare against Alad V. Get out and take down the Infested, Tenno!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Gunslinger",},text={"%'%'%'Mesa: The Gunslinger%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"Ballistic Battery: When activated, this power stores damage caused by guns. When triggered again, that damage is channeled through the next gunshot."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"Shooting Gallery - Gives an ally extra damage while jamming the guns of nearby enemies. This power shifts between team members."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Reflect",},text={"Shatter Shield - Envelops Mesa in a barrier of energy, reflecting back incoming bullet damage."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Lato","Focus",},text={"Peacemaker - With intense focus, Mesa draws her Regulator pistols, shooting down her foes in rapid succession."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Mesa","Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"*Obtain Mesa through defeating Mutalist Alad V!"}},
        {tags={"Mesa",},text={"*Longhorn Mesa Alt Helmet can be found in the Market today!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"%'%'%'Mutalist Alad V!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"%'%'%'New Quest: Patient Zero%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Mutalist Alad V",},text={"%'%'%'New Boss: Mutalist Alad V%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Alad V",},text={"Consumed entirely by Infestation, Alad V has thrown the Solar System into turmoil using his new Infested experiments. Be warned, Tenno – Alad V is the likely cause of this new outbreak and must be stopped at all costs. Use the deadliest tools at your disposal to bring down the renegade Corpus madman!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Alad V","Quest","Lotus",},text={"You can encounter Alad V by beginning the PATIENT ZERO Quest. This Quest will take you through the steps to locate and fight what Alad V has become! Find Mutalist Beacons through Infestation Outbreak rewards. The Lotus needs 3 Beacons to triangulate Alad V’s position."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Weapons%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Redeemer","Killing Blow",},text={"*New Melee Weapon: Redeemer. Striking with massive twin blades, the Redeemer fires the killing blow with its built-it shotgun.<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Gammacor","Vaporize",},text={"*New Weapon: Gammacor. Designed to quickly vaporize minerals for content analysis, users discovered its powerful beam was equally efficient at dispatching hostiles.<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Paracyst","Quanta",},text={"*New Weapon: Paracyst. Completely overwhelmed by Infested biology, this former Quanta now fires organic ordinance and a harpoon like appendage.<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Dual Decurion","Vacuum",},text={"*New Archwing Weapon: Dual Decurion. Delivering twin streams of highly accurate, rapid-fire ordinance, the Decurion are specifically designed for combat in the vacuum of space.<%br>"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Reflect",},text={":New Syndicate Weapons: Each weapon has an adjusted stat and built-in Justice / Truth / Entropy / Sequence / Purity / Blight to better reflect its Syndicate’s nature."}},
        {tags={"Vaykor Marelok","Steel Meridian",},text={":*Steel Meridian: Vaykor Marelok"}},
        {tags={"Telos Akbolto","Arbiters of Hexis","Bite",},text={":*Arbiters of Hexis: Telos Akbolto"}},
        {tags={"Synoid Gammacor","Cephalon Suda",},text={":*Cephalon Suda: Synoid Gammacor"}},
        {tags={"Secura Dual Cestra","The Perrin Sequence",},text={":*Perrin Sequence: Secura Dual Cestra"}},
        {tags={"Sancti Castanas","New Loka",},text={":*New Loka: Sancti Castanas"}},
        {tags={"Ballistica","Rakta Ballistica","Red Veil",},text={":*Red Veil: Rakta Ballistica"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Offer standing to your Syndicate of choice to obtain these new Syndicate-specific weapons!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Melee Stances:%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Redeemer","High Noon",},text={"Redeemer: High Noon. Bullets spray between wicked slash attacks."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Infested Enemies:%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={":New Enemy - Tar-Mutalist Moa: Discharges tar-like mucus slowing its prey."}},
        {tags={"Saryn","Volt",},text={":New Enemy - Swarm-Mutalist Moa: Discharges swarms of infested spores."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={":New Enemy - Brood Mother: Spawns maggots that attack its target."}},
        {tags={"Mag",},text={":New Enemy - Maggot: Overwhelms prey with sheer numbers."}},
        {tags={},text={":New Enemy - Boiler: Riddled with boils that erupt into spawn pods when hit"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"New Customization - Liset Exterior<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"Players can now customize the exterior of their Liset using New Skins and color palettes!"}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"Customize your Liset by selecting Equipment, Decorate Liset, then Customize Hull."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={":Hima Liset Skin"}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={":Kuza Liset Skin"}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={":Zikha Liset Skin"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Enemy Accuracy Changes:%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Enemy accuracy will now be affected dynamically depending on what movements players are doing. Sprinting, sliding, jumping, and wall-running will all decrease enemies accuracy, and the faster you move the better that penalty becomes."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Corpus Ice Planet",},text={"%'%'%'Level Expansion - Corpus Ice Planet%'%'%'<%br>"}},
        {tags={"Mobile Defense",},text={"New Mission Types - Ice Planet Mobile Defense!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
},--misc end
["additions"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Added Rejoin Last Session as a new matchmaking option."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Added outline icons in Survival for incoming and activating Life Support Capsules."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*New loading screen! Now you can see your new Liset customization!"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Added resource drops to enemies in Archwing Missions!"}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Improved texture streaming settings."}},
        {tags={"Syndicates",},text={"*Altered the icons seen on the Syndicates screen to better support foreign language translations."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Improved tintmask visuals."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Improved the performance of in game spawn points."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Warframes will now play a standard animation when Sigils are being previewed."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Updated Augment Mod cards to specify what type of augment they are."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players can now rebind Move Up and Move Down on a gamepad or controller to any button they choose."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Certain hallways and catwalks have been given a facelift!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Performed multiple optimizations toward memory usage reduction."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased the sound gain on numerous Syndicate Powers."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased the volume on numerous Moa attack sound effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Improved the look and performance of many head tracking animations seen during the tutorial levels."}},
        {tags={"Shade",},text={"*Removed a number of low performance sprites effects to improve shader memory."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various other improvements to memory efficiency."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made several improvements to the consumable item wheel including making item selection easier to see."}},
        {tags={"Saryn",},text={"*Added ability timer for Saryn’s Molt."}},
        {tags={"Reflect",},text={"*Updated Inventory page to reflect changes made to the UI post Update 14."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Quest","Ordis",},text={"*Removed unnecessary descriptive wording from Ordis transmission in Limbo Theorem Quest."}},
        {tags={"Velocitus",},text={"*Updated visual on Velocitus hit effect, increased visual distance of shot."}},
        {tags={"Scoliac","Dex",},text={"*Removed the word ‘charge’ from the Scoliac’s Codex description as it’s no longer relevant."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various tweaks to lighting, bloom and particle effects."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Survival","Alert","Invasion",},text={"*Defense and Survival now have a finite length during Invasions / Outbreaks to match Alerts."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Survival start mission console Ciphers will now be easy, regardless of enemy levels."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Updated trading in Dojo to no longer incorrectly display Mod stacks. Will now properly display how many Mods are being offered in a trade."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added Ability Augment category to Mods and Upgrade screen."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Changed boss scaling to consider Mastery Rank 0 players. Should prevent boss encounters from becoming too difficult at lower ranks when higher rank players join missions."}},
        {tags={"Ember","Frost",},text={"*Frost’s Freeze ability now has an AoE on impact similar to Ember’s Fireball."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased Outbreak rewards on ‘Hard’ Planets: Doubled the offered rewards (i.e 2x Fieldron, etc)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players can no longer use or benefit from energy restore items while using an Ability that drains energy."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Those under the effect of Limbo%'s Banish can now un-banish themselves via acrobatics: Front roll or backflip will reverse the Banish."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made various optimizations to help reduce load times and video memory consumption as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/352478-big-update-download-yes-optimizations-incoming/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Updated in-store icon for the Dendra Armor better represent the purchasable item."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Improved loading and hitching issues when DirectX11 & Multi-threading were used"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Augment Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Valkyr","Prolonged Paralysis",},text={"*Updated Valkyr’s Prolonged Paralysis Mod to leave enemies stunned on the ground, open to execution attacks."}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Savage Silence",},text={"*Enemies affected by Banshee’s Savage Silence Mod are now open to finishes while under the effects of crowd control."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Zephyr",},text={"*Reduced pull distance and increased pull force on Zephyr’s Dive Bomb Augment Mod."}},
        {tags={"Nova","Neutron Star",},text={"*Slightly increased the range and damage of Nova’s Neutron Star Mod."}},
        {tags={"Hydroid","Volt","Curative Undertow",},text={"*Healing speed from Hydroid’s Curative Undertow Mod slowed. Also increased energy consumption from 2 to 5"}},
        {tags={"Rhino","Ironclad Charge",},text={"*Buffed effects from Rhino’s Ironclad Charge Mod."}},
        {tags={"Trinity","Vauban","Tesla Link",},text={"*Limited the number of links that Tesla can make when under the Tesla Link Augment. Now connects with up to 4 other Tesla orbs."}},
        {tags={"Nekros",},text={"*Removed the health and shield penalties from Nekros’ Soul Punch Augment Mod"}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Invasion",},text={"*Fixed an issue where players completing the final mission of an Invasion set would not receive battle pay if the Invasion completes while they are in the mission. There is now a one hour grace period for missions in progress."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with the World State bar overlapping the notifications window."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue where players could not rebind buttons on a gamepad or controller if the control scheme was set to Inverted play."}},
        {tags={"Ember",},text={"*Fixed Ember’s Fire Blast only damaging targets once, rather than causing damage over time."}},
        {tags={"Frost","Nova",},text={"*Fixed issue where Nova’s Null Star could cause game to freeze."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various Augment Mod bugs."}},
        {tags={"Prime",},text={"*Fixed pricing and scroll bar display issues on Prime Access and Prime Accessories Marketplace tab."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with mis-aligned objects in Ice Planet tunnel."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue in French, Italian and Russian UI that would cause text overlap on the Mission Result screen."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue where Matchmaking dropdown would get stuck on player’s screen after entering Navigation."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed gamepad / controller navigation error when viewing Training in Codex."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed UI incorrectly selecting equipment from the top left tile when navigating equipment."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed a number of text strings running off the screen when viewing certain Codex pages in specific languages."}},
        {tags={"Interception",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Extract button that appears in Extraction and Interception missions exceeding the size of the associated button when playing in certain languages."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players not being able to use the left thumb stick on a controller to accept an invite when looking at their player card or Matchmaking dropdown."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed squad invites sometimes auto-accepting."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various issues with the elevator on the Ice Planet tileset."}},
        {tags={"Lecta",},text={"*Fixed planet, node and matchmaking buttons not notifying players when selectable."}},
        {tags={"Saryn",},text={"*Fixed Saryn’s Molt not dying properly when the ability expires. Also removed Molt image properly when Molt dies."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed login rewards potentially causing a game freeze."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed textures on Corpus door frames that allowed players to see outside the level."}},
        {tags={"Rescue",},text={"*Fixed markers created by a player in Rescue saying ‘Enemy’ if attached to hostage NPC."}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Fixed bug that caused Mind Controlled enemies to only die when killed by clients, and not the host."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed loot crates and NPCs spawning inside of walls / the ground."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed spawn trigger that would cause players to respawn outside of a level."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed respawn trigger on Ice Planet tileset that would cause players to become stuck in a teleport loop between two objects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed occasional instances of overlay map not functioning when joining a mission in progress."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed weapons inaccurately showing increased accuracy PERZENTO when equipped with a Mod that gives negative accuracy."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest","Corpus Ship",},text={"*Fixed players being able to activate Archwing early during the third Archwing quest by jumping out of a window on the Corpus ship. Kinda made sense, but still not ok."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed tileset art overlapping on some levels."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Infested tileset features appearing in non-Infestation missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Marketplace not counting Noggle decorations that have been placed as Noggle decorations owned."}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"*Fixed improper indentation on Nova’s Abilities panel."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed lockers appearing both red and green for clients."}},
        {tags={"Vor",},text={"*Fixed Auto-install Mod option equipping two of the same type of Mod with different levels. Will now only equip one Mod per type, favoring higher ranked Mod."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Ciphers not solving Corpus minigames."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed resources not appearing in player’s inventory."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed error that would occur when scrolling through inventory while highlighting sorting tabs."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed reconnection errors that would occur when attempting to rejoin a session that you’ve been dropped from."}},
        {tags={"Rhino","Ironclad Charge",},text={"*Fixed enemies not taking damage from Rhino’s Charge when under the effect of Ironclad Charge. Also correct strength Mods double applying to explosion damage."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Mastery Sigil rank not displaying properly in profile diorama."}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Stalk",},text={"*Fixed Stalker music blending in with level music in some instances."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed ‘global’ chat channels not appearing when in the Liset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where opening a player profile when your profile was already open would show your stats instead of selected player’s stats."}},
        {tags={"Excavation",},text={"*Fixed clients in Excavation being unable to see the scanner (but can still see waypoints and effects)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue where signing in with one profile, logging out, and then signing in with a second profile will give the second profile the wrong keybindings."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed player not being able to leave the Market after entering the Market"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed floating chunks of ice as seen in: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/348236-floating-ice-incorrect-stratos-clipping-console-respawn-outside/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various localization issues for text descriptions."}},
        {tags={"Oberon","Smite Infusion",},text={"*Fixed typo on Oberon’s Smite Infusion Mod."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed error when selecting Show Profile immediately after loading into game."}},
        {tags={"Hydroid",},text={"*Fixed enemy bodies becoming invisible after dying inside of Hydroid’s Undertow."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed Ash’s Bladestorm disabling all stealth finishers after use."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Extractor Drones picking up the wrong planets resources. (For real this time!)"}},
        {tags={"Defense","Interception",},text={"*Fixed issues with Interception missions showing rewards as Waves in increments of 5 (like Defense) instead of Rounds in increments of 1."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.3.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 25th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/351670-hotfix-1533/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Raptors",},text={"*Raptor boss will now play a proper 3D transmission when taunting players, instead of its previous 2D image."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Improved load times for clients."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*<%s>Fixed Extractor Drones getting wrong system’s resources.<%/s> Not fixed yet!"}},
        {tags={"Corvas",},text={"*Fixed the Corvas charge shot not having any additional effect or damage."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Fixed error that would leave players running instead of flying in space during final Archwing quest."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed Archwing missions allowing players without an Archwing to start mission."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Interception",},text={"*Fixed an issue in Archwing Interception missions that could affect enemy spawning."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Interception",},text={"*Fixed Archwing Interception objective point generation that was causing map generation issues."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
