["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 21st, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/349994-hotfix-1532/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Removed unnecessary audio cues from Syndicate Death Squad sounds."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Fixed Warframe Launcher attempting to optimize cache requests when there isn’t enough disc space."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed other various Launcher operational issues, including high DPI monitors not being able to select any options “Play/agree”."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.3.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 20th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/349380-hotfix-1531/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Made slight lighting changes to Dojo so that objects feel more connected within the Dojo’s environment."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added localization for Gifting inbox message."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Enabled automatic optimization of the download cache as needed (this optimizes load-times and was previously only run manually through the launcher settings)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made several adjustments to Syndicate XP sounds."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added sound and particle FX to sending a Gift, same as when buying an item."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed several typos within Gifting system localization."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed possible incorrect report of update size on launcher in Windows XP."}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Fixed Nyx’s Absorb not playing the end explosion animation."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed Limbo’s Banish ability playing the incorrect sound."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed issues with Grineer Skiff and Drone sounds in Archwing missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the Gifting present rattling even after it’s been unwrapped."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.3"},
["date"]={"November 20th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/349166-update-1530/"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Launcher and Website 2.0 are here! Read all about it here in our %[%https://warframe.com/news/warframe-gets-new-look News post%]."}},
        {tags={"Trinity",},text={"*Trinity’s Immortal Skin has arrived and is now available in the Market!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Tenno to Tenno Gifting is here! You may now send gifts through the Marketplace! Read on to learn how:"}},
        {tags={},text={"#Choose an item you want to gift."}},
        {tags={},text={"#Select the new ‘Gift’ beside the Purchase button."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"#Choose a recipient from Friends/Clan/Recents lists (or enter an Alias in the box)."}},
        {tags={},text={"#You can enter a short message to accompany the gift or leave it blank."}},
        {tags={},text={"#After confirmation and successful delivery the appropriate Platinum will be deducted."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"If delivery fails you will not be charged! Keep in mind you can’t send a Gift to a recipient if it’s restricted for them to purchase themselves (XP Locked, Already Owned, Quest locked). <%br>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Happy Gifting, Tenno! <%br>"}},
        {tags={"Lotus",},text={"- The Lotus <%br>"}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Notification message when failing to meet a Conclave restriction will now be more informative."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Capture",},text={"*Capture targets are now immune to Nyx’s Mind Control."}},
        {tags={"Survival",},text={"*Life Support drop rate in Survival will now scale with node difficulty. %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/349126-survival-spawning/?p=3897024#entry3897024 forum thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Archwing weapons can no longer be purchased if Archwing Quest has not been completed"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Warframe Changes%'%'%' "}},
        {tags={"Loki",},text={"*Loki’s Decoy now has an ability timer."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Phage",},text={"*Fixed Phage sound firing indefinitely when player is killed / revived."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Mastery Sigils being unable to identify players Mastery Rank correctly."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed the name of a Clan now properly updating within an Alliance."}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Stalk",},text={"*Fixed Stalker’s music not looping."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Infested behavior to sprint to target rather than briskly walking."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Mastery rank of Sigils resetting on login."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue where game client was not updating properly."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed Eximus enemies that required too many scans in Codex (mostly Orokin). Reduced scan requirement to 3. Please note, should also fix Affinity Issues with Corrupted Eximus units. An attempt was made to fix this in 15.2.0, but we’ve got it for real this time!"}},
        {tags={"Elytron",},text={"*Fixed Elytron Warhead damage and radius not increasing when ranked up."}},
        {tags={"Opticor",},text={"*Fixed Spectres being unable to use the Opticor correctly."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed players being able to fly through Grineer ships in Archwing with no collision."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Fixed Archwing not equipping to players during third mission of the Archwing quest."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue where rare Loot Crates would cause mission to not save any items obtained once mission was completed."}},
        {tags={"Grustrag Three",},text={"*Fixed the Grustrag Three and Zanuka not spawning properly under particular circumstances."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Challenge tab locking up if player inspects tab but hasn’t completed any challenges."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.2.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 14th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/345935-hotfix-1521-1522/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Base standing gain now ten times faster than before (for real this time!)"}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Loki","Saryn",},text={"*Fixed instance when Molt’s decoy would not properly die at the end of the ability."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed unintentional cap on Syndicate bonus standing gain. (This fix will manifest after login resets at 7PM EST)."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.2.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 14th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/345935-hotfix-1521-1522/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Syndicate Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added standing multiplier to Sigils - the higher the tier of Sigil, the higher the multiplier."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added sigil multiplier to Syndicate mission bonus if sigil matches the Syndicate hosting the mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Syndicate Standing gain per day now capped at 2000 + (2000 x Mastery Rank Number)."}},
        {tags={},text={"**For example, I am a Mastery Rank 15 player."}},
        {tags={},text={"**My personal Syndicate Reputation Cap is 2000 + (2000 x 15)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Using BEDMAS: 2000 + 30000"}},
        {tags={},text={"**32000 is my Daily Syndicate Standing cap as a Mastery Rank 15 player."}},
        {tags={},text={"*<%s>(Coming in next build, missed build cutoff!)<%/s> Base Syndicate standing gain is now ten times faster than before."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Warframe Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Mag","Trinity",},text={"*Removed LOS checks for Radial Javelin, Energy Vampire, and Shield Polarize (reverted the 15.2.0 change)."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"%'%'%'Archwing Changes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Reduced AI ‘sprint’ acceleration and max ‘sprint’ speed to make enemies easier to target."}},
        {tags={"Velocitus",},text={"*Updated Velocitus firing sounds."}},
        {tags={"Velocitus","Focus",},text={"*Reduced lens flare effect on Velocitus."}},
        {tags={"Velocitus",},text={"*Bigger hit effect on the Velocitus’ projectile."}},
        {tags={"Velocitus",},text={"*Small buff to Velocitus’ damage, included ability to sweep a sphere giving some ‘slack’ on the shot."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Enemy spawns will be staggered so enemies are not as bunched together."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Small optimization made to AI pathing in Archwing."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Hydroid",},text={"*Adjusted holster position for staff weapons on Hydroid."}},
        {tags={"Capture","Interception",},text={"*Relocated capture and spawn points on Viver, Interception missions."}},
        {tags={"Fragor",},text={"*Improved the weapon trail on the Fragor/Brokk attack animations."}},
        {tags={"Hive","Eris",},text={"*Viver, Eris is now a Infested Hive mission to return Eris to a purely Infested region."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Zephyr","Boar",},text={"*Fixed Flappy Zephyr missing from leaderboards."}},
        {tags={"Rathbone",},text={"*Fixed Rathbone requiring two handles to craft, now reduced to one handle."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Sigils not showing their proper selected color."}},
        {tags={"Rathbone",},text={"*Fixed Rathbone having 9 Mod slots, reduced to 8."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Made improvements to Archwing enemy navigation, should no longer get stuck in asteroids when being fired at."}},
        {tags={"Gilded Truth",},text={"*Fixed Gilded Truth Mod giving higher fire rate than intended, now gives up to 80PERZENTO when fully maxed."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed issue that caused Zephyr suddenly stopping mid air due to physics bug."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Quest",},text={"*Fixed VIP not spawning in quest defense missions."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Haven",},text={"*Fixed World State Window not accessing a Syndicate Mission on a planet you haven’t unlocked."}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"*Fixed script error that would stop progress on Capture missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed portions of Ice Planet tileset near crashed ship where players could fall out of map."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Various localization fixes and updates."}},
        {tags={"Interception","Corpus Ship",},text={"*Fixed Interception point D on Corpus Ship, no longer floating off the ground."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Interception",},text={"*Fixed issue regarding host-migration, items will no longer be lost during host-migration mid Defense or Interception missions."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Joining an Archwing mission that’s already in progress will no longer mute Archwing sounds."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed inaccurate collision on crashed ship on Ice Planet tileset."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Fixed players not receiving Archwing during space extraction cinematic on Archwing quest mission."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.2"},
["date"]={"November 13th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/344505-update-1520/"},
["title"]={"Tenno Reinforcements: Rathbone & Velocitus"},
["image"]={"U15.2 Promo Image.jpg"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Mag","Archwing","Rathbone","Rush",},text={"*%'%'%'RATHBONE%'%'%': Archwing Melee: Using Archwing’s jets for thrust, this massive hammer crushes anything in its path."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Volt","Archwing","Velocitus",},text={"*%'%'%'VELOCITUS%'%'%': Archwing Primary: When fully charged, the magnetized barrel of the Velocitus accelerates a metal slug to tremendous speeds, piercing hulls and obliterating armor."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*New Player Quest: Introduction to Infested: Once Awake"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Intercepted Grineer communications reveal that Grineer have been experimenting with some sort of bio weapon. You must investigate."}},
        {tags={"Vor","Quest","Dex",},text={"**This new quest will be immediately available to any player who has completed Vor’s Prize, and can be accepted directly from the Codex. It will also be made available to players who skipped Vor’s Prize when the quest system was first implemented, meaning that only players who are yet to complete Vor’s Prize and are brand new to Warframe will need to wait to play!"}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*%'%'%'Warframe Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"**Enemies targeted by Nyx’s Mind Control no longer take friendly damage until the duration of the ability ends, at which point all accumulated damage to the target is applied at once."}},
        {tags={"Trinity","Vauban","Tesla Link",},text={"**Vauban’s Tesla beam from the Tesla Link Mod has received an improved visual effect to reduce visual clutter when cast multiple times and"}},
        {tags={"Trinity","Vauban","Tesla Link",},text={"**Vauban’s Tesla beam from the Tesla Link Mod should now properly deal Slash damage."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Mag",},text={"**Excalibur’s Radial Javelin and Mag’s Shield Polarize will now check for line of sight when targeting enemies."}},
        {tags={"Trinity",},text={"**Trinity’s Energy Vampire now only benefits teammates who can see or can be seen by the targeted victim (victims remember who they have seen for a short duration)."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Regen",},text={"**Banish / Rift Walk will no longer stack energy regeneration when used on targets in a Cataclysm bubble."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Interception",},text={"*%'%'%'Interception Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Points can no longer sit neutral, they must be owned by either AI or players"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Score now scaled directly by the number of points you have, instead of being reduced if the AI owns more."}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"**Abandoned points (eg. was capturing but left point before completing the capture) now start to drain faster (5 seconds after leaving from 10 seconds) and drain back to the original state far quicker (at a rate of 25PERZENTO per second)"}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"**Updated all point prefabs so the points will start captured by the AI and must be taken by the player, score does not start accruing until a player captures a point"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"*%'%'%'General Changes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"**Optimized Dojo memory use to improve performance and load times."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Optimized game code to improve loading times and performance."}},
        {tags={},text={"**HUD will now display the correct button mapping when using a controller with a remapped button layout."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"**Enemy spawn points are now spread into clusters in Dark Sector Conflicts."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"**Removed brown fog from Archwing missions."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"**Made small adjustments to flaming debris in Archwing Trench Run missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Previewing the front sigil will now display the default color for the sigil you are viewing without changing the color of your equipped back sigil."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Huras","Hunt",},text={"**Huras Kubrow Hunt Mod will now prompt Kubrow to pick an enemy near other enemies (since the charge is AoE)."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Huras",},text={"***Added knockdown to Huras Kubrow charge that should make enemies hit suffer an entertaining knockback."}},
        {tags={"Gilded Truth",},text={"**Gilded Truth Mod will now provide a multiplier of 1.2 instead of a multiplier of 0.2."}},
        {tags={"Vor",},text={"**Moved Vor’s transmissions during Vor’s Prize to sit on the right hand side of the screen to reduce obstruction."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Adjusted timers on special effects particles to prevent lingering FX (ie. smoke from destroyed crates)."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Removed Neural Sensor Requirements from Large Restore Crafting Recipes."}},
        {tags={"Void","Dex",},text={"**Eximus units in the Void now require 3 scans for a Codex entry, as opposed to the original 20."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"**Changed all entries of ‘Fire’, ‘Poison’ or ‘Freeze’ damage elements to ‘Heat’, ‘Toxin’ and ‘Cold’ respectively to remove inconsistency."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*%'%'%'Archwing Fixes:%'%'%' "}},
        {tags={"Elytron",},text={"**Fixed description for Elytron’s Core Vent ability incorrectly stated that it hurts enemies."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Extend",},text={"**Fixed an issue with Archwing melee weapons not extending when viewed in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"**Fixed color changes on Archwing weapons appearing to revert when modified in Arsenal, changes now correctly saved."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"**Fixed Archwing parts loading into non-archwing missions."}},
        {tags={"Odonata",},text={"**Fixed Odonata’s Disarray not affecting Dreg Spheres."}},
        {tags={"Elytron",},text={"**Fixed Elytron’s Core Vent not affecting Dreg Spheres."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Odonata",},text={"**Fixed the activation of Odonata Archwing abilities not properly issuing 25/50/75/100 Affinity respective of ability used (1/2/3/4)."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*%'%'%'General Fixes:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Infested Ship",},text={"**Fixed an issue with a checkered wall blocking progression on certain areas of the Infested Ship tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed a UI Bug that made Affinity Orbs appear to give 100 Affinity in the ‘pickup ticker’ when a Affinity Booster is active, when 200 Affinity was in fact being rewarded."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed pause menu becoming inaccessible when viewing mission progress."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed a crash that would occur for players with pending recipes available on login."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"**Fixed an error where Kubrow would still be heard when using Arsenal or when muted."}},
        {tags={"Fusion Core",},text={"**Fixed issue where using the search bar for Mods would always display Fusion Cores in the results."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"**Fixed a game freeze that could occur when selecting an event from the World State Window."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed scrollbar inaccuracies in Events tab of World State Window."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed inbox messages breaking after transmissions have played."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed some profiles appearing incorrectly as Grand Master."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed Trench Run minimap issues to correctly display objective marker and load correct room."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Huras","Hunt",},text={"**Fixed issue with Huras Kubrow Hunt Mod where charge would end after 5 frames of no movement."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Huras","Hunt",},text={"**Fixed issue with Huras Kubrow Hunt Mod where all Kubrow in mission would share the same cooldown recharge time."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed issue that caused Sigils to never display warframe energy color."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed issue that caused planets to not zoom in when on Solar Map, also reduced lingering on the full map once a planet/node has been selected."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"**Fixed dual wielded secondary weapons appearing in players hands in Archwing Arsenal."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed menu tabs not displaying correctly when player switches quickly from one tab to the next."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed login screen not displaying last used Warframe, should now be corrected after one login."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Alert","Ceres",},text={"**Fixed defense objective on Ceres alerts spawning off the lift, breaking enemy pathing."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Shipyard",},text={"**Fixed loading issue with Grineer shipyard zones."}},
        {tags={"Europa","Conclave",},text={"**Fixed Europa incorrectly displaying 500-1200 conclave requirement, now properly reads 500-1000."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Ship",},text={"**Fixed pathing error on Infested Corpus Ship tileset that would occur when entering certain hallways."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"**Fixed various gameplay freezes and crashes."}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Stalk",},text={"**Fixed continued issues with Stalker not spawning properly."}},
},--fixes end
["localizations"]={
        {tags={"Limbo","Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Localized the Limbo Theorem and Archwing quest summaries for Russian and Japanese."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Localized final Archwing mission transmissions to French."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Resized Syndicate names to not be so long in Russian."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Various localization fixes and updates."}},
},--localizations end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.1.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 8th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/343682-hotfix-1513/"},
["misc"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Highlighted Improvements:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Continued memory optimizations to support Windows XP systems."}},
},--misc end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Improved performance and stability of long missions by setting a cap on active Mods in a session. 50 mods can now exist simultaneously before new mods will replace the old."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Minor Improvements to Syandana physics in space."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Performance improvements to mod-pickup effects."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a Client-side hitch when Miniboss type enemies spawn."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue with floating props on the Gas City tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Resource drop chance boosters having no effect in game. We are currently generating a script to refund all purchases, stand by, this may take a while."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed weapons being enabled in the Liset after releasing the Grustrag Bolt (for real this time)."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail",},text={"*Fixed missing localization for Solar Rail customizations."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Sentinel","Dex",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Kubrow and Sentinel Codex entries freezing the game."}},
        {tags={"Ballistica",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Ballistica where it would only burst fire on the first attempt. After that the %'tap fire%' would only shoot once."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Ballistica",},text={"*Fixed an issue where Ballistica’s magazines floated after reloading."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed size scaling issues on Melee Attachments."}},
        {tags={"Gram",},text={"*Fixed minor grammar and typo errors on Training entries."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with missing Orokin Stairs as reporte %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/337776-the-ancient-mystery-of-the-missing-orokin-stairs/ here%]"}},
        {tags={"Corpus Outpost",},text={"*Fixed issues with brightness on the Corpus Outpost levels as seen %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/342680-too-bright-on-corpus-outpost-screenshots-included/ here%]"}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Dojo Transporters being broken if created without a name."}},
        {tags={"Sargas Ruk",},text={"*Fixed numerous instances of Sargas Ruk being spelled ‘Sargus’."}},
        {tags={"Excavation",},text={"*Fixed issues with Excavation Scanners sometimes not appearing in missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issues with the correct appearance of Harvester/Grustrag 3 Deathmarks on Profile Screens."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue with energy colours not working on Archwings as seen %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/343478-it-appears-that-energy-color-does-not-work-for-archwing/ here%]"}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.1.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 8th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/341594-hotfix-1512/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Improved Dojo load times and Dojo stability for 32-bit systems."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Made improvements to long host migration times caused by recent optimizations."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Warframes can now be rotated in the Sigil preview diorama."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Tweaked the volumes of Ancient Screams."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Improved the Corpus Drone engine audio and increased the audible distance of related sound effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased the volume of Grineer hacking minigame related sound effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Removed the doppler effect from Orokin alarm sound effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased the volume of numerous Grineer combat barks."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Increased the movement speed of co-op hack doors to match the movement speed of other doors."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Rewrote and re-recorded one of the Archwing Quest opening transmissions."}},
        {tags={"Pyrana",},text={"*Reduced the amount of reverb heard on the Pyrana sound effects."}},
        {tags={"Pyrana",},text={"*Increased the volume of the Pyrana reload sound effect."}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"*Capture Targets’s Shields Drone will not provide instant shield recharges."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Defense","Interception",},text={"*Fixed potential issues with Interception and Defense reward screens not showing mods or player names past certain waves."}},
        {tags={"Odonata",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Odonata’s Energy Shell impeding Melee attack homing on enemies."}},
        {tags={"Europa",},text={"*Fixed issues with floating rooms and items on Europa."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Fixed an incorrect objective marker on the minimap during the Archwing Quest."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed Mag%'s Bullet Attractor killing player sentinels as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/254993-bullet-attractor-kills-sentinels-last-tested-nov-3/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Defense",},text={"*Fixed a floating Cryopod in Corpus Defense maps."}},
        {tags={"Excavation",},text={"*Fixed an issue with players not always receiving Cryotic after completing an Excavation mission."}},
        {tags={"Syndicates",},text={"*Fixed a loss of functionality that could occur when attempting to access the Syndicates menu via the Pause Menu."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a number of broken visuals associated with breaking windows during Trench Run mission."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Corrected an outdated Basic Training Codex line."}},
        {tags={"Valkyr","Archwing",},text={"*Fixed a scaling issue seen with Valkyr’s ambient visual effects during Archwing missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with Infested Healing Ancients spamming their charge sound effects."}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Stalk",},text={"*Fixed another instance of lockdown’s not ending correctly after the Stalker leaves."}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Defense","Interception","Stalk",},text={"*Fixed an instance of the Stalker getting stuck in waiting to spawn mode on Interception and Defense maps."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a number of AI navigation issues seen on certain Ice Planet tiles."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Smoke Shadow",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Ash’s Smoke Shadow Augment lasting forever if cast by a client who subsequently quits the mission."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Defense","Smoke Shadow",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Ash’s Smoke Shadow affecting Defense targets."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed being able to use weapons while on the Liset, specifically after removing the Grustrag Bolt."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue with getting inside asteroids as seen %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/340650-archwing-able-to-get-inside-asteroids/ here%]"}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"*Fixed an issue with enemies not always spawning at the start of Capture missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with the player getting stuck in missions if the End of Mission screen appeared while viewing mission progress via the pause menu."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.1.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"November 6th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/340842-hotfix-1511/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Trinity",},text={"*%[%[Trinity|Trinity’s%]%] %[%[Energy Vampire%]%] can no longer be cast on inactive %[%[Corpus Turret%]%]s."}},
        {tags={"Eris",},text={"*Changed and fixed several spawning, navigation, and objective details on %[%[Eris%]%] - Viver."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Revive button resulting in an instant mission fail when playing alone."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed %[%[Archwing%]%] Trench Run levels having invisible walls."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed issue with AI flying into geometry in Archwing missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed %[%[Melee 2.0#Stealth Attack|stealth finishers%]%] being far less effective than they should be."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed Christmas coming early for %[%[Dojo%]%] Decorations."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with %[%[Melee 2.0#Channeling|melee channeling%]%] effects not properly matching the selected energy color."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed several %[%[Sigils|Sigil%]%] icons appearing as ammo boxes."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed a Crash related to accessing a Clan Dojo."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed many crashes on Windows XP systems."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Various Crash fixes."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{

["date"]={"November 5th, 2014"},

["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed %[%[Sugatra%]%]s not being visible when equipping them."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Carrier","Excavation","Quest",},text={"*Fixed %[%[Power Carrier%]%] enemies not spawning in %[%[The Limbo Theorem|Limbo Quest%]%] %[%[Excavation%]%] mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed new %'Prefer Overlay Map%' setting not appearing in Options > HUD menu."}},

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.1"},
["date"]={"November 5th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/340143-update-151015101/"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Added an option to preview Syndicate Sigils on your Warframe."}},
        {tags={"Titania",},text={"*<%s>Founders Sigils are here and will be given to eligible accounts via script, we will update this line when the script is complete. Almost done, stand by to receive!<%/s> Founders Sigils are now distributed!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added new Mastery Tests for Rank 15>16, 16>17 and 17>18!"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Interception",},text={"*Added many Archwing mods into the Archwing Interception Mission Reward pool."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Changed the way Melee Channeling colours are generated to restore brightness to those with brighter customized selections %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/336159-warframe-melee-channeling-effect-back-to-dull-colors/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Optimized code to address out of memory issues on 32 bit systems."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Optimized visual effects to save memory."}},
        {tags={"Bo Prime","Prime","Arbiters of Hexis","Bite","Sacrifice",},text={"*Altered the %[%[Arbiters of Hexis%]%] sacrifice to be the %[%[Bo Prime%]%] Handle."}},
        {tags={"Nova",},text={"*Altered %[%[Nova|Nova%'s%]%] %[%[Worm Hole%]%] ability sound effects to now only be audible when in the proper proximity."}},
        {tags={"Syndicates",},text={"*Improved controller compatibility with the %[%[Syndicates%]%] UI."}},
        {tags={"New Loka",},text={"*Increased the resolution of the %[%[New Loka%]%] %[%[Sigils|Sigil%]%] ."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Removed redundant textures, tuned sound and texture defaults for better performance and faster load times."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Liset","Haven",},text={"*Removed the Update 14 Option on Login to choose between story/tutorial or instant Liset mode. If you haven’t logged in since before Update 14, you’ll be required to complete the tutorial."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added the option to have the minimap expanded or collapsed upon entering a mission. This is option can be selected in the Options Menu."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased the health of Syndicate %[%[Eximus%]%] teams."}},
        {tags={"Arbiters of Hexis","Bite","Sacrifice",},text={"*Changed the Sacrifice item for the fourth title in the Arbiters of Hexis Syndicate."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Changed status chance on Dual Stat Mods to be displayed as a base status chance for clarity."}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"*Capture targets less susceptible to knockdown and stagger locking."}},
        {tags={"Exterminate",},text={"*Changed the cinematic intro to the Exterminate Mastery Rank test to be cleaner."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Changed brightness in the Visual effect of %[%[Limbo|Limbo’s%]%] %[%[Rift Plane|Rift%]%]."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Regular %[%[Attack Drone%]%]s and corrupted drones now play proper explosion sound, not space explosions."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*Slightly tweaked Foundry usability: when choosing to build an item in the foundry, the foundry will try to instantly return to the item being built, instead of scrolling down the list."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Changed Calculation method of 2 Syndicate Augment mods:"}},
        {tags={"Sobek","Shattering Justice",},text={"*%[%[Shattering Justice%]%] (%[%[Sobek%]%] Mod) changed to max +20 Additive to base Status Chance."}},
        {tags={"Supra","Entropy Burst",},text={"*%[%[Entropy Burst%]%] (%[%[Supra%]%] Mod) changed to max +20 Additive to base Status Chance."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"Heavy Melee Weapon Changes:"}},
        {tags={"Gram",},text={"*%[%[Gram%]%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Base damage increased to 100 (up from 45)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Status chance increased to 15PERZENTO (up from 10PERZENTO)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Critical Chance increased to 15PERZENTO (up from 7.5PERZENTO)"}},
        {tags={"Galatine",},text={"*%[%[Galatine%]%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Base damage increased to 125 (up from 45)"}},
        {tags={"Scindo Prime","Prime",},text={"*%[%[Scindo Prime%]%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Base damage increased to 130 (up from 85)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Critical Chance increased to 20PERZENTO (up from 5PERZENTO)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Critical Multiplier increased to x2 (up from x1.5)"}},
        {tags={"Jat Kittag",},text={"*%[%[Jat Kittag%]%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Base damage increased to 130 (up from 80)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Increased ragdoll effect on enemies."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Critical Chance increased to 15PERZENTO (up from 5PERZENTO)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Critical Multiplier increased to x2 (up from x1.5)"}},
        {tags={"Magistar",},text={"*%[%[Magistar%]%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Base damage increased to 80 (up from 50)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Increased ragdoll effect on enemies."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Critical Chance increased to 20PERZENTO (up from 12PERZENTO)"}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"**Attack speed slightly decreased."}},
        {tags={"Fragor",},text={"*%[%[Fragor%]%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Based damage increased to 115 (up from 55)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Significantly increased ragdoll effect on enemies."}},
        {tags={"Scindo",},text={"*%[%[Scindo%]%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Base damage increased to 100 (up from 55)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Critical chance increased to 15PERZENTO (up from 10PERZENTO)"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Increased ragdoll effect on enemies."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"Archwing Mod changes:"}},
        {tags={"Energy Inversion",},text={"*%[%[Energy Inversion%]%]."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Changed from Max +20PERZENTO Shield Capacity to Max +80PERZENTO Shield Capacity."}},
        {tags={"Energy Amplifier",},text={"*%[%[Energy Amplifier%]%]."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Changed from Max +6PERZENTO Power Range to Max +60PERZENTO Power Range."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Stalk",},text={"%[%[Stalker%]%]/%[%[Death Mark|Miniboss%]%] Changes:"}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Stalk",},text={"*Stalker will now activate lockdown when he Spawns."}},
        {tags={},text={"*All minibosses will now be targeted less frequently by other enemies in a given mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Better spawning logic and placement for all miniboss type enemies."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Perhaps certain minibosses now have a chance to drop a Sigil…."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Windows XP systems not being able to play Warframe."}},
        {tags={"Vor","Void",},text={"*Fixed Captain Vor not firing his Void Key Laser Beam in the Tutorial."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with Syndicate messages not appearing properly in different languages."}},
        {tags={"Invasion",},text={"*Fixed Syndicate Assassins joining as allies if you%'re supporting their faction on an Invasion node."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Foundry",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Archwing components not appearing in the Archwing tab of the Foundry."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Ragdoll death effects causing performance drops."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Liset",},text={"*Fixed the Liset being semi-invisible during the first Archwing Mission end cinematic."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with incorrect stats being displayed on the Profile page."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a loss of functionality that could be encountered while opening the View Mission Progress window during the tutorial."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with performing a directional air melee attack right before landing, would cause the player%'s weapon to remain holstered while firing."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with the End of Mission results screen title becoming incorrectly swapped if the player clicks on the Syndicate Title."}},
        {tags={"Sentinel",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Sentinel Stats not appearing correctly in the end of mission stats if the Sentinel is dead."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed an issue with ‘Power in Use’ occurring on Limbo’s Banish when used on certain enemies (Grineer Rollers)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various map holes across multiple tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Sabotage",},text={"*Fixed a random black barrier blocking the objective room in Archwing Sabotage missions."}},
        {tags={"Fusion Core",},text={"*Fixed only seeing Fusion Cores when prompting the Mod Inventory screen."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed PBR error that caused black textures to appear on Liset and star map."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed Grineer Archwing enemies not playing movement sounds for clients."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with expanded minimaps being reverted to a small state after hacking minigames."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with Aura Polarity slots being swappable with Mod slots."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Event Nodes not automatically being chosen after selecting it in the World State Panel."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Burst weapons fire rate being frame-rate dependent (Semi-automatic weapon fix to come!)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed in-game Market packages not being purchasable."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed Grineer Archwing enemies not playing movements sounds for clients."}},
        {tags={"Quest","Dex",},text={"*Fixed Codex Quest info appearing in a wrong location after attempting to scroll within the text box."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the in-game Market not showing the price of items if you%'ve already purchased them."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed receiving an error when attempting to navigate through the Syndicate banners with a controller."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed being able to navigate away from a selected Syndicate banner when you%'re viewing their detailed info panel."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Ship",},text={"*Fixed an ice chunk texture colliding with the crashed Corpus ship."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed being able to select an already selected Syndicate banner."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*Fixed the Dark Sector Status Menu displaying 12 Mod slots."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*Fixed the Dark Sector Status Menu only displaying progress towards Mods placed in the Aura/Stance/bottom right slot."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*Fixed absent End of Mission results screen for Dark Sector missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed overlapping text in the mission info panel."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed activating a hack panel while sliding, causing you to keep sliding past the screen."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed absent Archwing movement/flight sounds upon joining a mission."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed the Archwing button in Arsenal overlapping Chat Box when displayed in Spanish."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issues with minimap waypoint generation in trench runs."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Mirage’s Hall of Mirrors clones not animating if you are using a different frames Animation Stance."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed the %'Search%' text in the search box not being removed when attempting to type."}},
        {tags={"Vauban","Opticor",},text={"*Fixed issues with Crewman in Bastille not being affected by headshots with the Opticor as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/335385-opticor-bastille-vs-corpus-cant-headshot/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Syndicat Sigils appearing as tradeable in the Dojo."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where purchasing things in the Arsenal and loading into a mission will not save any of the changes made after the purchase."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with Arcane Helmet descriptions not showing in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={"Defense",},text={"*Fixed a UI issue on the reward screen in Defense missions which would not show any rewards to players if a Host Migration occurred."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where Rank-Up test messages would continuously appear even after declining."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed broken UI and overlapping text when pausing during the End of Mission screen."}},
        {tags={"Opticor",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Opticor not having a listed Trigger type."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed multiple crashes and gameplay freezes."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.0.9"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"October 30th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/336430-hotfix-1509/"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Added new death effects for Corpus Crewman and Moas."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added a transparency slider for Sigils!"}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={"Reflect",},text={"*Tweaked snow and ice textures to be less reflective"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Invasion","Saturn",},text={"*Archwing nodes are now excluded from Invasions. There was (and right now still is) an Invasion on Pandora, Saturn which is preventing Archwing play."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Optimized code to better handle Syndicate Standing tracking."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Players can no longer taxi squad members to Syndicate missions above their current rank. Also corrected issue where Syndicate Mission XP was given for the incorrect node in a region."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with Bonus Syndicate mission reward standing not being displayed on the mission results screen."}},
        {tags={"Blind Rage","Fortitude","Rage","Transient Fortitude",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Corrupted Mod Transient Fortitude not being equippable simultaneously with Blind Rage."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed scale issues on Orokin Explosive Barrels and Orokin Caches."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issues with some PBR materials having incorrect opacity/alpha write values which resulted in character transmissions not looking correct."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed enemies not appearing for Clients in Archwing missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where closing a private conversation would result in a loss of functionality."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Grineer Skiff enemies firing at different rates for host and client."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Fixed Quests being re-startable (which is not fully supported right now) by simply pressing enter on the Quest Diorama screen, even though the %'Begin%' button is not showing."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed missions not starting when being selected via the Worldstate panel."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Exterminate","Quest",},text={"*Fixed issue with the Exterminate kill counter continuing to display in Archwing quest after being told to abort mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Syndicate Standing bar calculations to correctly display current Standing. Also, Standing bar will now display a message when a new title is required to progress."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed various ‘out of bounds’ bugs in tilesets that could cause players to get stuck or fall out of level."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with unnecessary explosions in Trench Run extraction area."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue where Corpus hack puzzle would display blank hexes."}},
        {tags={"Reach",},text={"*Fixed issues with extraction messages staying after reaching extraction."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue with too many News items disturbing layout of News Panel."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed waypoint marker issues in certain Archwing Trenchrun Maps."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Ciphers not being usable for Grineer hack panels."}},
        {tags={"Frost","Defense",},text={"*Fixed a game freeze that would occur in defense missions when one player leaves and the other continues playing."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed several crashes and game hangs."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.0.8"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"October 29th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/335717-hotfix-1508/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*On-going texture streaming improvements."}},
        {tags={},text={"*On-going Syndicate Offering page visual tweaks and improvements."}},
        {tags={"Syndicates","The Perrin Sequence","Sacrifice",},text={"*Altered numerous Perrin Sequence Sacrifice Credit costs to be in line with the rest of the Syndicates:"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Level 1 was 300,000 but should have been 30,000"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Level 2 was 100,000 but should have been 50,000"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Level 4 was 100,000 but should have been 250,000"}},
        {tags={"Sacrifice",},text={"**Please note: if you paid a sacrifice fee before this change went live and would like to receive a refund in your overpaid sacrifice, %[%http://support.warframe.com warframe support%] will be happy to help you!"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Removed a number of explosion sound effects from decorative explosions in Archwing missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Removed a number of redundant trade message popups."}},
        {tags={"Opticor",},text={"*Increased the intensity of the Opticor explosion visual effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Tweaked a number of Drahk Master animations."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Increased the number of Excavators found on a number of Ice Planet tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Opticor",},text={"*Tweaked the partially charged Opticor shot sound effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Adjusted the content within hidden loot crates to no longer include Platinum only cosmetics."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage","Derelict",},text={"*Corrupted Vaults now appear in Derelict Sabotage missions."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*%'%'%'PSA%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Crashing on Windows XP? Please delete your Warframe EE.cfg file found here: <%blockquote>PERZENTOUSERPROFILEPERZENTOLocal SettingsApplication DataWarframe<%/blockquote>"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Let us know if this helps. Please note deleting this file will delete keybindings, but it may help prevent crashes. We are currently working on a fix if you do not wish to delete your EE.cfg. "}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with elevator on Ice Planet tilesets being too fast which would cause players to clip into nearby geometry."}},
        {tags={"Vauban",},text={"*Fixed an issue with not being able to properly shoot Ancient Infested enemies trapped in Bastille containment fields."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed a texture flickering issue seen on Limbo%'s Cataclysm ability."}},
        {tags={"Defense","Exterminate",},text={"*Fixed an issue with mind-controlling a Drahk Master causing Exterminate and Defense game modes to break."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed an instance of Bladestorm not working correctly and becoming stuck in use."}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Quest","Stalk",},text={"*Fixed the Stalker and other minibosses appearing on quest missions which could break the mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with G3 not spawning correctly due to missing spawn points."}},
        {tags={"Lotus",},text={"*Corrected a couple of incorrect references in numerous Lotus transmissions."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed an issue with certain Limbo crafting components missing proper descriptors."}},
        {tags={"Halikar",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Halikar jump strike sound effects getting stuck on a continuous loop."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with elevator panels appearing as pure black when at the bottom of the elevator shaft."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Archwing mods becoming unequipped after being fused."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a number of art related issues seen on Ice Planet tilesets."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a number of map holes seen on Ice Planet tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed a number of invisible walls/blocking volumes on Archwing tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Interception",},text={"*Fixed an AFK related Interception exploit."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue with particles not properly disappearing after auto-installing Archwing mods in the Arsenal screen."}},
        {tags={"Rescue",},text={"*Fixed a potential progression stopper related to getting stuck inside certain art assets in Infested Rescue tilesets."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with mods being displayed at the end of match screen despite the mission having been failed."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue with objective markers not properly pointing towards the objective in certain Archwing tilesets."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with random floating bodies being seen in certain Ice Planet tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed a crash related to attempting to equip Archwing mods."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with only mods being assigned to polarity slots being saved after using the auto-installation feature."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with players potentially getting stuck under stairs on certain Orokin tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Bronco Prime","Prime",},text={"*Fixed a missing drop icon for the Bronco Prime."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the player Camera becoming incorrectly clamped at a bad angle after being revived in Archwing missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Drahk Master enemy type occasionally breaking and not properly attacking the player."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where deleting saved loadouts would cause a loss of functionality."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Fixed %'You must have an Archwing%' message appearing when trying to launch the final mission of the Archwing Quest immediately after claiming the Archwing from the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Syndicate Transmissions cutting out mid-way through."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.0.7"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"October 28th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/334873-hotfix-1507/"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest","Dex",},text={"*Added a final summary line to the Codex when viewing the completed Archwing Quest."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Furious Javelin",},text={"*Added a visual effect to Furious Javelin which will grow in intensity the more times it is cast."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added in a new Power Menu input for binding when using a gamepad. The old power cycling/activating old remains for players wishing to use this configuration but the Power Menu option is now on by default."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*General Changes:"}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Volt",},text={"**Tweaked the Banish cast animation and increased casting speeds."}},
        {tags={},text={"**On going Syndicate Offering UI improvements."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Flow",},text={"**Glass windows in Archwing missions can now be flown through without taking damage."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"**Toned down the Archwing hit reaction animations."}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Stalk",},text={"**Tweaked a number of Stalker sound effects."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Game Optimization Changes:"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Note: The following game/memory performance changes should help alleviate a number of win32 related crashes while also improve the overall load times on all systems."}},
        {tags={"Vectis",},text={"**Improved the Vectis Scope visuals."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Improved the performance of multiple transmissions."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Improved loading screen performance."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"**Improved the performance of multiple UI elements seen on the Liset."}},
        {tags={"Stalker","Stalk",},text={"**Improved the performance of the Stalker%'s initial loading into a level."}},
        {tags={"Rhino",},text={"**Improved the collision of the Hecate Syandana on the Rhino warframe."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Improved the performance of numerous in game sound effects.Adjusted the position of the Sun in the Ice Planet tileset skybox."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with the game pad bindings for Crouch was appearing incorrectly."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a number of display issues seen on the End of Match results screen."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Archwing abilities using incorrect stats."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Void",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Void laser traps still harming Limbo while in Rift Walk mode."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed an issue with gravity building up during repeated casts of Zephyr%'s Tail Wind."}},
        {tags={"Phobos",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Nav Segments not properly dropping from the Phobos Boss."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with incorrect Master Ranks being displayed on the Profile "}},
        {tags={"Frost","Coolant Leak",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Coolant Leak removing Snow Globe slow on enemies."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed some geometry clipping issues seen on certain Ice Planet tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Opticor",},text={"*Fixed the charge damage multiplier not working correctly for the Opticor."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a number of broken art and lighting assets seen in numerous Ice Planet tiles."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with Clients not hearing Grineer Skiff sound effects correctly."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Phorid",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Phorid bolts damaging Limbo whilst Rift Walking."}},
        {tags={"Elytron",},text={"*Fixed an issue with erroneous Elytron Wing descriptions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with certain Ice Planet tilesets missing proper minimaps and thus breaking waypoints."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed an instance of Bladestorm attacks getting stuck as continually in use."}},
        {tags={"Oberon",},text={"*Fixed an issue with broken Beastmaster item pickups."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue certain art assets clipping into certain Archwing tilesets causing potential progression stoppers."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Bloomer explosion visual effects not appearing correctly for clients."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed the HUD marker for Demolitions Archwing abilities registering and appearing incorrectly."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed an issue with clicking on the top menu while inside Clan Stats causing the menu system to break."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where selecting a color picker would cause the currently equipped color to be switched to an incorrect color selection."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with erroneous Infested art assets appearing in certain Ice Planet tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Oberon",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Beastmaster boomerang switching hands when equipping and throwing."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.0.6"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"October 27th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/333871-hotfix-1506/"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Added a message to be displaced on the end-of-mission “Syndicate” screen if a user is not wearing a Sigil."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Further optimized memory footprint and load-times."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Adjusted the maximum brightness of the Archwing jet flares."}},
        {tags={"Ember","Frost","Oberon","Saryn",},text={"*Players affected by the Fireball/Freeze/Venom/Smite Augments will now see a visual effect."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Changed font choices throughout various UI elements."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Lowered the frequency at which the Drahk Master steals weapons."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Optimized both Grineer and Corpus Cipher minigames."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Changed the Worldstate panel demanding you to do Alerts in regions you didn’t have unlocked via ‘red notification’."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Tweaked enemy explosion sounds."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*General Fixes:"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed an issue with the UI Rewards screen displaying levelled up mods as rewards."}},
        {tags={"Nikana",},text={"**Fixed an issue with Nikana weapons using the wrong animation for jump slams."}},
        {tags={"Rescue","Alert",},text={"**Fixed an issue with Nightmare Rescue Alerts giving out additional mods."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed an issue with Syndicate Standing/Reputation varying if you had fewer weapons in your loadout at end of mission."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed an issue with all ability icons not being the same size."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"**Fixed issues with things spawning in the environment in Archwing missions."}},
        {tags={"Europa",},text={"**Fixed issues with the skybox in on the Europa Ice Planet tileset."}},
        {tags={"Interception",},text={"**Fixed AI not spawning when idling at Interception points."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed issues with unsightly leg animations while using directional air attacks."}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"**Fixed Quest blueprints being purchasable multiple times."}},
        {tags={"Glaive",},text={"**Fixed Grineer Drahk Masters stolen weapon having an incorrect drop type if he is killed with the Glaive with the stolen weapon is in the air."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"**Fixed an issue with names in Clan profile dioramas not being in the same order as the Warframes."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"**Fixed issues with Mirage’s Eclipse ability not correctly working with the Edo Chest armor."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"**Fixed issues with missing Archwing sound effects when joining a mission in progress."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"**Fixed several game freezes and crashes."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*%'%'%'Limbo%'%'%' Changes/Fixes:"}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"**Sped up casting animation for Rift Surge."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Quest",},text={"**Fixed issues with the Limbo Theorem quest inbox not automatically appearing."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Stalker","Stalk",},text={"**Stalker ability cancel ability can close rifts. Prevents people from just using Banish or Tear in Space to avoid being hurt by the Stalker."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Void",},text={"**Fixed issues with Void laser and Ice traps affecting Limbo while Rift Walk is active."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"**Fixed issues with Limbo dropping items like the datamass while activating Rift Walk potentially losing them forever."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Controller & Binding Changes/Fixes:"}},
        {tags={"Junction",},text={"**Fixed a controller issue where certain face buttons weren%'t working as expected in conjunction with the Power Menu modifier, particularly when those face buttons were bound to Sprint or Sprint/Run or Place Marker."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"**Fixed an issue where players could not rebind the controls for Move Up / Down (mostly used in Archwing missions). This is in response to a lot of players not liking the default (Space/Ctrl) or getting confused by not being able to use CROUCH consistently (some were saying that toggle crouch worked, while hold crouch didn%'t - in actual fact neither were true, but the default for toggle crouch is the same as the default for Down (Ctrl))."}},
        {tags={"Boar",},text={"**Removing the ‘Power Menu’ binding for Keyboard and Mouse, as this context action is redundant for a Keyboard and Mouse layout."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.0.5"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"October 27th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/333619-hotfix-1505/"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Sabotage","Earth","Antitoxin",},text={"*Explorers rejoice! Hidden Caches are back on Sabotage: Earth missions! There will be 3 caches in each level, with rewards from the Sabotage tables. In case you missed the Cicero Crisis Event, you can still scan and make antitoxins to receive rewards from this mission type! "}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Foundry",},text={"*Added an Archwing category to the Foundry."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Optimized code to handle further server load."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Tweaked the landing sound effects heard during the Ice Planet spawn intro cinematic."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Tweaked the ambient sound effects heard during the exit cinematic on Ice Planet tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Reduced the intensity of random space explosions visuals seen during Archwing mission."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Tweaked the arrow position for Mirage%'s Agile stance."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Improved AI navigation for certain Archwing tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Rage",},text={"*Limited the number of augment damage stacks that can be applied to Mirage’s Hall of Mirror clones to 10."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Adjusted a number of Syndicate related strings so as to prevent them from getting cut off at certain resolutions."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*All active and uncompleted Alerts will now appear in the World State window. Locked alerts will be indicated as such."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Force fields have been added to certain Archwing Trenchrun tilesets to prevent players from unintentionally exiting the game space."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Removed a number of erroneous tiles from the Archwing Trenchrun tileset."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Tweaked the direction melee attack animations for Axe weapons."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"%'%'%'Limbo Changes and Fixes%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Increased the intensity of the Rift Surge buff visual effects."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Stalker","Stalk",},text={"*Rift Walk can now be disabled by the Stalker."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Banish can now be recast on targets to bring them back and forth from the Rift."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Void",},text={"*Fixed Void laser traps still harming Limbo in Rift Walk mode."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the visual effects for Limbo%'s channeling not appearing correctly."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed Corpus Mine Osprey mines (as well as other replicated projectiles) affecting across Limbo’s Rift on Clients."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Please note that more tweaks to Limbo will come in the next Hotfix."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed Archwing missions being unplayable on 32-bit machines."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed ability casting not working in Archwing mode if you are using a controller."}},
        {tags={"Ember","Frost","Vauban",},text={"*Fixed ability screen not showing stats for Ember Fireball, Frost Freeze, or Vauban%'s abilities."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed Zephyr%'s gravity reduction being removed after using Dive Bomb or melee attacks."}},
        {tags={"Halikar",},text={"*Fixed issues with colour customization not working on the Halikar."}},
        {tags={"Oberon",},text={"*Fixed an issue with some of Smite%'s visual effects not properly being culled after a certain amount of time."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Kubrow%'s spawned by Drahk Masters not properly counting towards Extermination goals."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Settlement",},text={"*Fixed some clipping issues seen on certain Grineer Settlement art assets."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a number of map holes seen on Ice Planet tilesets."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with the incorrect ground hit animation being played for Dual Dagger and Dual Sword ground hit attacks."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a crash related to purchases Syndicate favours."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Fixed an issue with art assets seen during the Archwing quest unintentionally knocking players out of the level."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Prime",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Targis Prime visual effects appearing oversized during Archwing missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with usernames comprised of all number appearing incorrect in the Chat window."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a number of map holes in Outpost tilesets."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue with players being able to get stuck inside certain art assets during Archwing missions."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.0.4"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"October 25th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/331599-hotfix-1504/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Controller bindings to allow ability cast binds/pre U15 behaviour. Still defaults to U15 %'power menu%' however."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where players using a Controller and who have chosen to make Sprint toggleable found that after performing any action that forced their Warframe to walk (such as aiming), they had to toggle Sprint twice to see any change. "}},
        {tags={"Opticor",},text={"*Fixed Opticor%'s Color customization corrupting memory and causing crashes (and other similar cases)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issues on new Profile UI: equipment tab was not showing up some times, weapon usage was often incorrect"}},
        {tags={"Europa",},text={"*Fixed motion blur in several elevators (particularly obvious on Europa)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed equipment tab not showing up sometimes. "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed most used weapon always showing a 0PERZENTO usage. "}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed the completion of Archwing missions not %'unlocking%' the node. This was preventing Extractors from being deployed. "}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.0.3"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"October 24th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/330379-hotfix-1503/"},
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Invasion",},text={"*Fixed an issue where no Clients would receive mission rewards for any missions, and games in Solo mode would not count toward Invasions."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Exterminate","Interception","Free Space",},text={"*Fixed Free Space Archwing modes (Grineer Exterminate and Interception) having forced extractions in the middle of gameplay."}},
        {tags={"Hek",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Hek Shotgun Syndicate mod being 500PERZENTO multishot instead of 50PERZENTO."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed several gameplay crashes, particularly for 32-bit Windows."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.0.2"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"October 24th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/329893-hotfix-1502/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Tuned texture detail settings for 32-bit systems to improve stability."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Interception",},text={"*Tweaked Archwing Interception missions:"}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"**AI capture time for a given point increased from 10 to 20 seconds."}},
        {tags={"Capture",},text={"**Reduced the number of AI squads that can capture a point simultaneously for all player counts."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Increased enemy spawn delay time."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed several crashes and gameplay hangs."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a common join-in-progress crash."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue where you could use Warframe abilities with Archwing."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.0.1"},
["type"]={"fix"},
["date"]={"October 24th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/329586-hotfix-1501/"},
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Optimized server queries to deal with popularity."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Camera zoom will now only occur when player is viewing sigils for optimal viewing pleasure."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Changed the description of the Limbo Theorem quest to better indicate that Archwings are required to complete."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed several common and rampant gameplay crashes."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage",},text={"*Fixed a progression stopper in Orokin Sabotage missions due to a missing Miniboss"}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Limbo Profile Avatar pack’s not pointing to the correct icons."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Limbo’s animation sets both being labelled “AGILE”."}},
        {tags={"Atterax","Extend",},text={"*Fixed issue with the Atterax whip not extending."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Point Blank",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Corpus Archwing enemies not attacking when you’re in point blank range."}},
        {tags={"Corpus Ice Planet",},text={"*Fixed infested decorations appearing for no reason on Corpus Ice Planet room."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Fixed several broken Archwing quest states that would prevent players from advancing."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where player would respawn outside the level after revive in trench-run if you died close to the ceiling."}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed rounding errors causing some weapons to show at rank 29 in the codex."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with Mastery Rank showing as ‘0’ in player Profiles."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed materials on Grineer caches"}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Codex showing unreleased weapons."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed Limbo’s Banish becoming stuck as ‘Power in use” as per: %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/328908-power-in-use-stuck-while-using-limbo-limbo-hat-animation/ this thread%]"}},
        {tags={"Opticor",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Opticor’s sound being heard by entire squad as if they were using it."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage","Void",},text={"*Fixed the wrong mission reward being given for the main objective for Orokin Void Sabotage missions."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed issued with the scale and position of Syandanas on Limbo."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue with early extractions from Archwing missions not showing proper mission result loadout information."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where viewing other player’s profile would hang game."}},
        {tags={"Invasion",},text={"*Fixed the World State panel becoming unresponsive after selecting an invasion for a locked planet"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue with level doors not being properly replicated to Client/Host in Archwing Trench Run missions."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Quest","Ordis",},text={"*Fixed subtitle on Ordis inbox message for Limbo Quest."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Fixed an issue with players who don’t have Archwings being able to join the last part of the Archwing quest."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed Cipher minigames not working with a controller."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Volt%'s Overload not showing up on the Abilities screen."}},
        {tags={"Sacrifice",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Syndicate Sacrifice being deducted more than one time in the same Rank."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed for Grineer bobblehead having missing head during placement mode."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage",},text={"*Fixed a progression stopper in Orokin Sabotage missions due to a missing Miniboss."}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Nova","Valkyr",},text={"*Fixed a bug that was incorrectly scaling Syandanas down on Banshee, Nova and Valkyr by 20PERZENTO. This should address some physics issues with Syandanas on these Warframes too."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with casting Warframe abilities on a controller not working properly."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issue with %'Corvette%' Corpus Enemy damage output being significantly larger than the rest of the enemies."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed a case where the player last targeted by a beam would get an initial hit when the beam fires at the next target (if they don%'t move)."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*Fixed issues with Limbo%'s ability sounds."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed Zephyr’s passive low-gravity not working. Not yet fixed."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed issue with the Damage Output of Zephyr’s Divebomb"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed Radial Javelin not attributing kills to the caster as well as resulting in friendly fire."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Corpus Trench Run Sabotage maps having missing connectors."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Fixed an issue where accessing and leaving the ‘mods’ terminal from the Archwing Subsection causes functionality loss."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end
{
["update number"]={"15.0"},
["date"]={"October 24th, 2014"},
["forumurl"]={"https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/328727-warframe-archwing/"},
["title"]={"Archwing"},
["image"]={"Archwing_Header.jpg"},
["additions"]={
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*Your journey with Archwing includes many options that all begin with the Archwing Quest."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*All players Mastery Rank 2 or greater are eligible to begin the Archwing quest."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Quest",},text={"*New Quest: Archwing! Complete the quest to begin your Archwing journey!"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Odonata","Protect",},text={"**New Archwing - Odonata. An Archwing that includes 4 unique abilities that equally protect and devastate your enemies!"}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Elytron",},text={"**New Archwing - Elytron. An Archwing that includes 4 unique abilities that offer incredible firepower to destroy your enemies! "}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Veritux",},text={"**New Archwing Weapon - Veritux! Weightless space turns this impossibly massive sword into an agile instrument of destruction."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Imperator",},text={"**New Archwing Weapon - Imperator! A long range rifle capable of firing in space, the Imperator%'s versatility makes it an ideal weapon for space combat."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Onorix","Defense","Protect",},text={"**New Archwing Weapon - Onorix! This laser bladed battle-axe easily hacks through a ship%'s defenses and the forces protecting it."}},
        {tags={"Archwing","Corvas",},text={"**New Archwing Weapon - Corvas! This rocket launcher fires modified torpedoes, meant for eliminating entire ships in the deep of space."}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*New Archwing Mods!"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Four new Archwing Gamemodes!"}},
        {tags={"Exterminate","Interception",},text={"*Freespace Modes (Exterminate, Interception) Grineer"}},
        {tags={"Exterminate","Sabotage",},text={"*Trench Run Modes (Exterminate, Sabotage) Corpus"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"**note: When modding for an Archwing mission, the only mods that transfer over from your Warframe Mod loadout are mods that are: Health, Shield, Armour, and Energy related."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"%'%'%'New Grineer Archwing Enemies%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={},text={"*OGMA (Grineer Combat Pod): Armed with a Demolition Cannon and Mining Drill."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*TEMPORAL DREG (Grineer Stasis Drone): Emits a tractor beam, slowing its target down."}},
