```lua
        {tags={},text={"*LANCER DREG (Grineer Laser Drone): Fires highly accurate laser beams."}},
        {tags={},text={"*SHIELD DARGYN (Grineer Shield Skiff): A Dargyn equipped with Shield defences."}},
        {tags={},text={"*HELLION DARGYN (Grineer Missile Skiff): Missiles seek out targets and inflict high damage."}},
        {tags={},text={"*SHIELD-HELLION DARGYN (Grineer Missile & Shield Skiff): A Hellion Dargyn equipped with Shield defences."}},
        {tags={"Dera",},text={"*CANNON BATTERY (Grineer Space Turret): Inflicts moderate damage at a fast rate."}},
        {tags={},text={"*MISSILE BATTERY (Grineer Space Missile Turret): Launches homing missiles."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"%'%'%'New Corpus Archwing Enemies%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"*CORVETTE (Corpus Flak Ship): Voltage grenades detonate to create an electric field."}},
        {tags={},text={"*FRIGATE (Corpus Laser Ship): Armed with a highly accurate laser cannon."}},
        {tags={"Carrier",},text={"*CARRIER (Corpus Swarm Ship): Launches several locust drones."}},
        {tags={},text={"*LOCUST DRONE (Corpus Swarm Drone): Follows a target while attacking them with lasers."}},
        {tags={},text={"*RANGER (Corpus Jet Pack Spaceman): Crewman equipped with a jetpack."}},
        {tags={"Penta",},text={"*PENTA RANGER (Corpus Jet Pack Spaceman with Penta): Crewman equipped with a jetpack."}},
        {tags={"Quanta",},text={"*QUANTA RANGER (Corpus Jet Pack Spaceman with Quanta): Crewman equipped with a jetpack."}},
        {tags={},text={"*GOX (Corpus Pod): Armed with a Mining Laser and Explosive Launcher."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*New Customization - Archwing & Weapon Colour Customization"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Void",},text={"*%'%'%'Limbo: The Void Sorcerer!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"**BANISH: Displaces a target into the rift plane, inflicting additional damage if the target is hostile."}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"**RIFT WALK: Pass over into the rift plane."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Void",},text={"**RIFT SURGE: Surge the void energy through the rift plane, increasing the damage inflicted on enemies that have been banished there."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Void",},text={"**CATACLYSM: A violent blast of void energy tears open a pocket of rift plane which can sustain itself for a short period before collapsing in another lethal blast."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Quest",},text={"*New Quest added to obtain Limbo!"}},
        {tags={"Limbo",},text={"*ARISTEAS Limbo Alt Helmet can be found in the Market today!"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Syndicates",},text={"%'%'%'Syndicates have emerged!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Begin your affiliation with a Syndicate at Mastery Rank 3. "}},
        {tags={"Liset","Syndicates",},text={"**Learn all about the emerging Syndicates by accessing the “Syndicates” panel in your Liset. This panel is to the left of Navigation!"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Each Syndicate is headed by a new character with unique models, dialog, and they want YOU!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Syndicates",},text={"*There are 6 Syndicates for you to explore and align with"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Increase your rank with a Syndicate by earning positive Standing! Standing is earned by wearing a Sigil while completing missions (found in the new “Regalia” section of the Arsenal)."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Each Syndicate comes with its own unique rewards as your Standing increases."}},
        {tags={"Volt",},text={"**Note: The Weapon-Specific Mods earned through Syndicate Standing have experimental bonus effects that we will be developing further based on feedback. The experimental bonus effect is: While having a Syndicate Weapon Mod equipped, its XP gained will fill up a Syndicate specific meter. When this meter is full it discharges granting some AOE and temporary bonuses!"}},
        {tags={"Syndicates",},text={"**Note: while working for one syndicate your standing with other syndicates may be reduced"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Syndicate Info/FAQ:"}},
        {tags={},text={"**You can only earn Standing toward One Syndicate at a time by wearing a Syndicate Sigil while completing missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Your Sigil does not affect your team mates Standing, it’s per-player only"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Frost","Europa",},text={"%'%'%'Europa Freezes Over!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Europa","Corpus Ice Planet",},text={"*The Corpus Ice planets have been added permanently to missions in Europa!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Sabotage",},text={"%'%'%'Orokin Sabotage Returns!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Sabotage","Void",},text={"*New Game Mode - Orokin Sabotage! The portal missions have returned and can be accessed through 4 tiers of Void Key."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage","Void",},text={"*The 4 tiers of Orokin Sabotage Void Key include 4 hybrid maps:"}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"**Tier 1 = Forest x Orokin Void"}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"**Tier 2 = Ice Planet x Orokin Void"}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"**Tier 3 = Desert x Orokin Void"}},
        {tags={"Void","Corpus Outpost",},text={"**Tier 4 = Corpus Outpost x Orokin Void"}},
        {tags={"Jackal","Lynx","Void","Corpus Outpost",},text={"**With the Orokin Void x Corpus Outpost & Ice Planet set, face off against a new Corpus Miniboss: The LYNX! This mini-Jackal spawns the defensive Shield Osprey and new Turrets!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Void","Prime",},text={"*Void Rewards have been added to these missions at mission complete. The reward pool includes items from the existing Void that were only represented once in the reward table. See the full drop table %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/172911-prime-gear-drop-locations/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Feel like exploring?"}},
        {tags={"Sabotage",},text={"**The 3 Hidden Loot Caches within Orokin Sabotage missions are back, and finding them will be rewarding! If you find 2 caches, the second cache gives you a shot at Rare Resources. If you find all 3, the last (third) cache gives you an additional shot at the end-of-mission rewards!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Opticor","Halikar",},text={"%'%'%'New Weapons: Opticor & Halikar!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Halikar",},text={"*HALIKAR – This jet powered Grineer throwing mace always comes back to its master."}},
        {tags={"Opticor",},text={"*OPTICOR – Once charged, this Corpus laser cannon dispatches a devastating blast of light energy."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Worldstate Panel Preview!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Alert","Invasion","Quest",},text={"*A more relevant and dynamic Worldstate panel is coming! Find out what Events, Quests, Alerts, Invasions and Syndicate missions are occurring at a glance!"}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"**Note: Dark Sectors in conflict will be added to the panel soon"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'The Enemy Expands!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*New Enemy - Grineer Drahk and Drahk Master! The Grineer Drahk Master summons Drahk, Armored Kubrows, to fight!"}},
        {tags={"Sprag",},text={"*Sprag and Ven’kra have returned!"}},
        {tags={"Jackal","Lynx","Void","Corpus Outpost",},text={"*Corpus Lynx! A new Corpus Miniboss, this mini-Jackal spawns the defensive Shield Osprey and new Turrets! Find him in the Orokin Void x Corpus Outpost & Ice Planet keys!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Customizations: Sigils, Idles, and more!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*New Customization - Sigils!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Found in the “Regalia” section of the Arsenal, equip and customize Sigils to show your loyalties and accomplishments!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Please note: All Founders will be receiving Sigils corresponding to their rank at a later Update."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Customization - Warframe Idles Feature!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*You can now equip the 40+ Agile and Noble stances across all Warframes! Each Stance is unlocked with Platinum and will work across all Warframes once purchased."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"%'%'%'New Player Ship Customization - Liset Decorations (x10)!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Clan","Liset",},text={"*Bobbleheads are here! You can buy these and place them in your ship through the pause menu -> Equipment -> Decorate Liset. Placement and removal works similar to Dojo decoration placement."}},
        {tags={"Soma",},text={"*New Customization - Soma Skin"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Directional Air Melee!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Allows you to jump, pick an angle and then hit the melee, which will then send you with momentum in the given direction"}},
        {tags={},text={"**This will enable you to hit enemies in the air"}},
        {tags={},text={"**Note: this feature is planned on being revised depending on feedback"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"%'%'%'New Mods - Archwing Mods (x25)%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Archwing",},text={"*Found by killing all the new Archwing enemies!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Mods - Weapon/Warframe mods (x16)%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Standard Mods:"}},
        {tags={"Extend","Reach","Sinister Reach",},text={"**Sinister Reach: Rifle: Extend range on beam weapons."}},
        {tags={"Extend","Ruinous Extension",},text={"**Ruinous Extension: Pistol: Extend range on beam weapons."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Terminal Velocity",},text={"**Terminal Velocity: Rifle: Weapon Projectile Speed."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Fatal Acceleration",},text={"**Fatal Acceleration: Shotgun: Weapon Projectile Speed."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Lethal Momentum",},text={"**Lethal Momentum: Pistol: Weapon Projectile Speed."}},
        {tags={"Misery","Continuous Misery",},text={"**Continuous Misery: Rifle: Weapon Proc Time."}},
        {tags={"Torment","Lingering Torment",},text={"**Lingering Torment: Shotgun: Weapon Proc Time."}},
        {tags={"Perpetual Agony",},text={"**Perpetual Agony: Pistol: Weapon Proc Time."}},
        {tags={"Lasting Sting",},text={"**Lasting Sting: Melee: Weapon Proc Time."}},
        {tags={"Rapid Resilience",},text={"**Rapid Resilience: Warframe: Reduces Proc Time applied to player."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Corrupted Mods:"}},
        {tags={"Anemic Agility",},text={"**Anemic Agility: Pistol: Increased fire rate, but decreased damage."}},
        {tags={"Vile Acceleration",},text={"**Vile Acceleration: Rifle: Increased fire rate, but decreased damage."}},
        {tags={"Frail Momentum",},text={"**Frail Momentum: Shotgun: Increased fire rate, but decreased damage."}},
        {tags={"Critical Deceleration",},text={"**Critical Deceleration: Shotgun: Increased crit chance, but decreased fire rate."}},
        {tags={"Creeping Bullseye",},text={"**Creeping Bullseye: Pistol: Increased crit chance, but decreased fire rate."}},
        {tags={"Fortitude","Transient Fortitude",},text={"**Transient Fortitude: Warframe: Increased power strength, but decreased duration."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Mods - Ability Augment Mods (x20)!%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Ability Augment Mods tweak and enhance Warframe Abilities in interesting ways. They are equipped in Mod Slots via the Warframe Upgrade Screen."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Syndicates",},text={"*These mods can be found within the Reward tiers of Syndicates. Higher Standing will allow Tenno to access the corresponding Augments per Syndicate."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Smoke Shadow",},text={"**Ash: Smoke Screen: Smoke Shadow: Smoke Screen now affects all allies for x seconds."}},
        {tags={"Banshee","Savage Silence",},text={"**Banshee: Silence: Savage Silence: While active, finisher damage is increased by PERZENTO."}},
        {tags={"Ember","Fireball Frenzy",},text={"**Ember: Fireball: Fireball Frenzy: Casting Fireball on allies will add fire damage to their attacks for x seconds."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Furious Javelin",},text={"**Excalibur: Radial Javelin: Furious Javelin: Each enemy hit will increase Excalibur’s melee damage by x for y seconds."}},
        {tags={"Frost","Freeze Force",},text={"**Frost: Freeze: Freeze Force: Casting Freeze on allies will add cold damage to their attacks for x seconds."}},
        {tags={"Hydroid","Curative Undertow","Reach",},text={"**Hydroid: Undertow: Curative Undertow: Allies with missing health can enter Undertow and receive a heal for PERZENTO of health, each ally in the pool will increase energy drain. **Once an ally reaches max health he will be expelled from the puddle."}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Haven",},text={"**Limbo: Banish: Haven: Allies banished to the rift will have a PERZENTO of their max health restored."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Loki","Safeguard","Safeguard Switch",},text={"**Loki: Switch Teleport: Safeguard Switch: After teleporting with an ally Loki will be invulnerable for x seconds."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Greedy Pull",},text={"**Mag: Pull: Greedy Pull: Pulls pickups towards Mag."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Hall of Malevolence","Rage",},text={"**Mirage: Hall of Mirrors: Hall of Malevolence: The damage of your doppelgängers is increased by PERZENTO, every time you kill an enemy."}},
        {tags={"Nekros","Vor","Soul Survivor",},text={"**Nekros: Soul Punch: Soul Survivor: Using Soul Punch on a downed ally will instantly revive the ally with PERZENTO of health. Nekros%'s health will drop to PERZENTO and all his shields and energy will be consumed."}},
        {tags={"Nova","Neutron Star",},text={"**Nova: Null Star: Neutron Star: Recasting Null Star will make all remaining Null Star Particles explode for x damage with a guaranteed blast proc."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Pacifying Bolts",},text={"**Nyx: Psychic Bolts: Pacifying Bolts: Affected enemies will become disarmed in confusion for x seconds."}},
        {tags={"Oberon","Smite Infusion",},text={"**Oberon: Smite: Smite Infusion: Casting Smite on allies will add x Radiation damage to their attacks for y seconds."}},
        {tags={"Rhino","Ironclad Charge",},text={"**Rhino: Charge: Ironclad Charge: Increases Rhino%'s armor by PERZENTO for each enemy hit for x seconds."}},
        {tags={"Saryn","Venom Dose",},text={"**Saryn: Venom: Venom Dose: Casting Venom on allies will add Toxin damage to their attacks for x seconds."}},
        {tags={"Trinity","Pool of Life",},text={"**Trinity: Well of Life: Pool of Life: On death, marked enemies will drop health orbs with a chance of dropping an energy orb as well."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Valkyr","Prolonged Paralysis",},text={"**Valkyr: Paralysis: Prolonged Paralysis: Affected enemies are pulled towards Valkyr and the stun will be PERZENTO longer."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Shock Trooper",},text={"**Volt: Shock: Shock Trooper: Casting Shock on allies will add x Electricity damage to their attacks for y seconds."}},
        {tags={"Vauban","Zephyr","Divebomb Vortex",},text={"**Zephyr: Dive Bomb: Divebomb Vortex: Enemies within x meters of the point of impact are dragged into it."}},
        {tags={"Trinity","Vauban","Tesla Link",},text={"**Vauban: Tesla: Tesla Link: Every pair of Teslas will connect via a beam that will cause x slash damage when touched."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'New Mods - Syndicate Weapon Mods (x18)%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={"Acrid","Toxic Sequence",},text={"*Acrid: Toxic Sequence: adds 50PERZENTO status duration. "}},
        {tags={"Bolto","Entropy Spike",},text={"*Bolto: Entropy Spike: adds 0.1PERZENTO projectile explosion chance"}},
        {tags={"Burston Prime","Prime","Gilded Truth",},text={"*Burston Prime: Gilded Truth: adds 20PERZENTO fire rate"}},
        {tags={"Dark Dagger","Gleaming Blight",},text={"*Dark Dagger: Gleaming Blight: adds 25PERZENTO status chance"}},
        {tags={"Dual Cleavers","Justice Blades",},text={"*Dual Cleavers: Justice Blades: adds 25PERZENTO melee damage"}},
        {tags={"Mag","Embolist",},text={"*Embolist: adds 50PERZENTO magazine capacity"}},
        {tags={"Furis","Winds of Purity",},text={"*Furis: Winds of Purity: adds 0.1 life steal"}},
        {tags={"Grinlok","Deadly Sequence",},text={"*Grinlok: Deadly Sequence: adds 50PERZENTO crit chance"}},
        {tags={"Hek","Scattered Justice",},text={"*Hek: Scattered Justice: adds 50PERZENTO multishot"}},
        {tags={"Jaw Sword",},text={"*Jaw Sword: adds 25PERZENTO melee damage."}},
        {tags={"Volt","Kestrel","Entropy Flight",},text={"*Kestrel: Entropy Flight: adds 35PERZENTO flight speed"}},
        {tags={"Mire","Toxic Blight",},text={"*Mire: Toxic Blight: adds 25PERZENTO extra poison damage"}},
        {tags={"Skana","Bright Purity",},text={"*Skana: Bright Purity: adds 25PERZENTO damage"}},
        {tags={"Sobek","Shattering Justice",},text={"*Sobek: Shattering Justice: adds 5PERZENTO status chance"}},
        {tags={"Spectra","Sequence Burn",},text={"*Spectra: Sequence Burn: adds 5m range"}},
        {tags={"Supra","Entropy Burst",},text={"*Supra: Entropy Burst: Adds 5PERZENTO status chance"}},
        {tags={"Mag","Viper","Stinging Truth",},text={"*Viper: Stinging Truth: Adds 10 magazine capacity"}},
        {tags={"Vulkar","Lasting Purity",},text={"*Vulkar: Lasting Purity: adds 15PERZENTO dead aim"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Additions to Mod inventory:%'%'%'"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added a Search Bar to the Mod Screen/Inventory! You can now type in what you are looking for to find it instantly!"}},
        {tags={"Lex","Alert",},text={"*Added in a new Grineer hacking minigame! All Grineer tilesets will now feature this new reflex-based game when a ship enters lockdown or an ‘alerted’ state!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added a reskinned Corpus hacking minigame!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added new icons to correspond with pickups! You will now see icons representing all pick-ups at the bottom of the screen!"}},
        {tags={"Limbo","Kubrow","Recovery","Rush","Recover","Rush",},text={"*Added the ability to rush Kubrow stasis recovery from the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added new Profile screens! As well as displaying player information, this new screen shows a 360° view of your equipped frame and personalized equipment information! P.S *this includes Deathmark visibility!"}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Added Kubrow information to Profile stats!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added more information to the End-of-mission screen! Learn about your Syndicate Standing and more!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added Mission Progress shortcut option to the keybindings menu."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added bullet ‘whizz-by’ sound support, meaning that we now are able to support sounds for projectiles/bullets passing by the player."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Added Clan Profiles and Dioramas! Clans with many players will have a diorama populated by 4 random members!"}},
        {tags={"Quest",},text={"*Added better UI support for Quests including ‘end of Quest’ screens and more!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added new grip and brand new movement set for Axe/Hammer/Mace weapons."}},
},--additions end
["changes"]={
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Enemy Level/Nav Segment Changes%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Starting enemy level ranges on planets have been adjusted accordingly:"}},
        {tags={"Mercury","Earth",},text={"**%[1-5%] Mercury & Earth"}},
        {tags={"Venus","Mars",},text={"**%[5-10%] Venus & Mars"}},
        {tags={"Jupiter","Saturn",},text={"**%[10-15%] Jupiter & Saturn"}},
        {tags={"Phobos","Sedna",},text={"**%[15-20%] Sedna & Phobos"}},
        {tags={"Europa","Uranus",},text={"**%[20-25%] Uranus & Europa"}},
        {tags={"Neptune","Eris",},text={"**%[25-30%] Eris & Neptune"}},
        {tags={"Ceres","Pluto",},text={"**%[30-35%] Pluto & Ceres"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*Nav Segment locations have been altered. Killing the bosses of the following planets will issue you the listed Nav Segment:"}},
        {tags={"Mercury","Venus","Mars",},text={"**Mercury Boss – Mars and Venus"}},
        {tags={"Venus","Jupiter",},text={"**Venus Boss – Jupiter"}},
        {tags={"Mars","Saturn",},text={"**Mars Boss – Saturn"}},
        {tags={"Jupiter","Sedna",},text={"**Jupiter Boss – Sedna"}},
        {tags={"Phobos","Saturn",},text={"**Saturn Boss – Phobos"}},
        {tags={"Phobos","Europa",},text={"**Phobos Boss – Europa"}},
        {tags={"Uranus","Sedna",},text={"**Sedna Boss – Uranus"}},
        {tags={"Europa","Neptune",},text={"**Europa Boss - Neptune"}},
        {tags={"Uranus","Eris",},text={"**Uranus Boss – Eris"}},
        {tags={"Ceres","Neptune","Pluto",},text={"**Neptune Boss – Ceres & Pluto"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"%'%'%'Alert System Changes%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={"Alert","Phobos","Corpus Outpost","Corpus Ship","Grineer Galleon",},text={"*Tilesets used for alerts now align with those appearing in the underlying planet region (i.e. no more Corpus Outpost alerts in Phobos). Shipyards and Infested Corpus tilesets now available, Grineer Galleon & Corpus ships are free to appear in any area (because space ships)."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Enemy factions for alerts now align with their associated planet, infestation however can still appear anywhere."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Update enemy levels for Alerts to align with current star chart topology."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow","Fusion Core",},text={"*Added some new items to the mix: Oxium, Argon, R5 Fusion Core Pack, Kubrow Egg."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Slightly increased frequency of Resource Alerts."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Alerts no longer occur on Boss Nodes."}},
        {tags={"Alert","Mercury",},text={"*Added the new U14 Mercury nodes to node list of eligible Alert Locations."}},
        {tags={"Alert",},text={"*Cleared out some nonsensical alert descriptions."}},
        {tags={"Hive","Eris",},text={"*40PERZENTO chance of Hive missions on Eris, home of the Infested!"}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Colour Blind Locker changes%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={"Focus",},text={"*All in-game lockers have a new representation of being locked or unlocked. This new representation focuses on symbols and shapes to be accessible to our colour blind players."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'Ability Mod Changes%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={},text={"%[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/325551-warframe-ability-mods-coming-changes/ full explanation thread%]"}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"*The former 4 Ability Slots have undergone the following changes:"}},
        {tags={"Forma",},text={"**All Warframes have had 2 Ability Slots removed. Any Forma spent on these slots will be refunded."}},
        {tags={"Forma",},text={"**All Warframes have had 2 previous Ability Slots converted to be blank polarities (unless otherwise Polarized)."}},
        {tags={},text={"**All Warframes purchased or crafted moving forward will have their abilities automatically viewable in the Arsenal."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Abilities are all unlocked as a Warframe is levelled up."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Abilities are all ranked up as a Warframe is levelled up."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={},text={"*On Login, the following will occur for all accounts:"}},
        {tags={},text={"**All highest Rank Ability Mods will be preserved and duplicated to fit the new Ability system. This means you won’t need to rank up any abilities if you already had a Maxed (or partially fused) Ability Mod."}},
        {tags={"Forma",},text={"**Two slots will be removed (prefers ability polarity) and Forma used on Ability slots will be refunded."}},
        {tags={"Fusion Core",},text={"**All Ability Mods converted to Fusion Cores of the appropriate level."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"%'%'%'Excalibur Changes%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={"Ash","Excalibur","Loki",},text={"*Super Jump: Super Jump will now add invisibility to Excalibur while in air. Additionally, Super Jump will cause a small radial stun to all enemies around Excalibur when performed, similar to the way Ash’s Smoke Screen works."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur","Rage",},text={"*Radial Blind: No longer able to affect enemies in rooms separated by walls. To encourage and facilitate different gameplay styles (like stealth), enemies aware of Excalibur within a few seconds, or enemies that Excalibur can see, will also be affected."}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Radial Javelin: All enemies within radius of this ability will now be hit with a javelin. Instead of having this ability radiate out from Excalibur, javelins will now spawn directly around opponents. Javelins will optimize to find a point on each enemy that is safe to be hit, bypassing enemies that may be holding shields or otherwise in cover. There is also no longer a target maximum for this ultimate, meaning anyone in the radius will be an eligible target. Additionally, if the javelin does not kill the enemy, each target that survives will be put into stun for a brief period of time."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"%'%'%'Dark Sector UI Update%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*Players starting a Dark Sector Conflict will now have to wait 20 seconds before the match officially begins. New players can join both sides of the conflict during this time."}},
        {tags={},text={"*A menu displaying the level of your Warframe and weapons will be viewable, showing at what ranks you’ll unlock each new Mod and the name of the next unlockable Mod. This menu can be brought up at any time during the battle, and is assigned to the Q key by default."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*Players will now have access to all of their chosen Warframe’s Abilities during a Dark Sector Conflict."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*All Warframes begin Dark Sector Conflicts with their first Ability available, and additional Powers will unlock at Rank 2, 4 and 8."}},
        {tags={"Reach",},text={"*Warframe Powers unlock in order (1, 2, 3, 4) and can be used as soon as players reach the appropriate Rank."}},
        {tags={},text={" "}},
        {tags={"Defense","Interception","Survival",},text={"*Defense and Interception Mission rewards now stack! This removes the %'inventory checkpoints%' every 5 waves in Defense -- it%'s all or nothing now. The Reward Rotation for *Defense and Interception has also been changed (to be like Survival) as Stacking Rewards is now in place. The Rewards now rotate through the reward tiers."}},
        {tags={"Survival","Void",},text={"*Significant improvements to enemy spawning in non-Void Survival missions. Removed a bunch of rules that made spawns too spread out and frustrating."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Reskinned the toggle buttons in the Options Menu!"}},
        {tags={"Laser Rifle",},text={"*Changed the MOA Laser rifle sound!"}},
        {tags={"Dex",},text={"*The Codex has been redesigned! See the new look today!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added a new algorithm that can improve enemy weapon accuracy. (So, bad guys won%'t shoot like Storm Troopers)."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Ciphers now scale in difficulty with enemy level."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Dynamic music mixing adjustments for smoother transitions."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Improved physics on Zephyr%'s helmet in an attempt to lower extreme object movement."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Many weapons updated to use zone maps which results in better metals."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Clan",},text={"*Dojo Teleporter decorations now have a 40 character name limit."}},
        {tags={"Duel","Clan",},text={"*Changed the ‘under the hood’ way Dojo Dueling works to fix several issues with this feature."}},
        {tags={"Quanta",},text={"*Reduced the explosion effects of Quanta%'s cubes in an attempt to improve performance."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Disruptor",},text={"*Changed Ancient Disruptors from having a forced magnetic proc on hit to only be a 10PERZENTO chance."}},
        {tags={"Rescue","Uranus","Sedna",},text={"*Swapped the Rescue level difficulties on Veles, Sedna and Uranus, Caliban to better match enemy levels."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Polished and tweaked Spectator view for Conclaves/PVP."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Significantly polished the Tutorial and New Player experience."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Improved the downward sweep attacks of staff weapons; they should now be easier to hit with."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Rearranged order of different settings in the Options tab."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*Changed Dark Sector Conflict battle pay logic to solve the problem of Attackers leaving and causing Defenders to not earn battle play."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"%'%'%'General Arc Trap changes%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Reduced Arc Trap radius from 11 meters to 8 meters."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Created new Arc Trap waypoint type. Better Wording: Arc Traps now spawn at strategically placed points as per a new %'under the hood%' waypoint spawn system."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Spawn between 2-8 Arc Traps at the designated level areas, number of which is scaled by difficulty."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Arc Traps now explode after 3 seconds of zapping."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Minimum enemy level required for Arc Trap to spawn is 10."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Arc Traps will no longer spawn when Ice Layer is active."}},
        {tags={"Sabotage","Earth",},text={"*Tweaked the difficulty of Sabotage missions on Earth. Included in this tweak is added Arc Traps and injector health adjustments."}},
        {tags={"Rescue",},text={"*Added Arc Traps to Grineer Rescue room on some cell doors."}},
        {tags={"Excavation","Survival",},text={"*Altered the spawning logic of Excavation to better match Survival scaling."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={"Defense","Derelict",},text={"%'%'%'Tweaked Orokin Derelict Defense%'%'%':"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Added more areas for the AI to navigate the terrain via jumping and flight."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Did a pass on what is considered ‘out of bounds’, should prevent some degree of spawn-camping at the top of the map."}},
},--changes end
["fixes"]={
        {tags={"Defense","Derelict",},text={"*Fixed a hole near the side stair route in Orokin Derelict Defense."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players getting a %'Failed to join session%' message after a mission ends."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed players not being able to progress past finding a squad when selecting a node on the Star Map."}},
        {tags={"Solar Rail Conflict","Solar Rail",},text={"*Fixed several broken Achievements: Dark Sectors, Mercenary, Shield Saver, and Martial Prowess challenges and any other challenge configured to not show incremental progress never could be unlocked."}},
        {tags={"Mag","Vauban",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Vauban%'s Bounce resulting in spamming %'0%' Magnetic Damage to enemies. Bounce will now properly deal damage to enemies."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed in-game challenge pop-ups not appearing when the ESC menu was open."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed various gameplay crashes and freezes."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed a crash that would occur when leaving a Conclave mission while the timer for next match is counting down."}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed an issue where sending 1 character messages in chat could freeze UI."}},
        {tags={"Eris",},text={"*Fixed Aura names in the squad panel displaying as asterisks when viewed in a language other than English."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed an issue where Kubrows could spawn into a mission dead."}},
        {tags={"Ember",},text={"*Fixed Ember%'s Accelerant not applying extra fire damage for clients when they kill an affected enemy with fire. %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/312482-ember-accelerant-only-benefiting-host/ forum post%] This fix also improves poor connections not showing accurate results on Accelerant affected enemies for clients."}},
        {tags={"Nyx","Buzlok",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Buzlok Rifle shots not adding damage to an ally using Nyx%'s Absorb."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where Mastery Rank tests would occasionally fail to load for some users."}},
        {tags={"Grineer Shipyard",},text={"*Fixed an issue with an Oil texture on the Grineer Shipyards tileset."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed issue with looping Liset ship seen %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/311059-corpus-outpost-extraction-bug-liset-lands-and-lands-again-and-again forum post%]"}},
        {tags={"Frost",},text={"*Fixed Frost’s Snow Globe inner snowstorm effects not scaling properly with Range mods, resulting in it not matching the outer globe scale."}},
        {tags={"Vauban","Buzlok",},text={"*Fixed the Buzlok%'s homing projectiles having strange bounce angles when using punch-through mods."}},
        {tags={"Oberon",},text={"*Fixed Smite%'s targeting on shorter enemies (drones, etc)."}},
        {tags={"Extend",},text={"*Fixed an issue where whip type weapons would not extend on slide spin attacks."}},
        {tags={"Valkyr",},text={"*Fixed an issue where not equipping a melee weapon while using Valkyr would break Hysteria."}},
        {tags={"Nyx",},text={"*Fixed Absorb (and other bullet attractors) attracting fire in cases they definitely should not be, e.g. when you are outside them, aiming away."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed the Dojo Trophy Room banners not showing the Clan Emblem if a given Clan has a custom emblem."}},
        {tags={"Hijack","Nightmare Mode",},text={"*Fixed an issue where Hijack missions in Nightmare Mode with the ‘no shield’ permutation would be unbeatable."}},
        {tags={"Ash","Nova",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Slipstream Nova profile icon pack stating that the purchase will receive Locust Ash profile icons."}},
        {tags={"Zephyr",},text={"*Fixed weird ragdoll animation on enemies being killed by Zephyr%'s Tornado that could cause missions dependent on killing enemies to not complete or move on."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Foundry","Rage",},text={"*Fixed issues with the way the Foundry displays certain builds-in-progress, as seen with Mirage’s Harlequin helmet %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/321773-harlequin-mirage-helmet-in-foundry forum post%]"}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Glaive","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Mirage’s Hall of Mirror clones not throwing Glaives if you%'re the Client."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Glaive","Rage",},text={"*Fixed Mirage’s Hall of Mirror clones turning and throwing the Glaive in the wrong direction when you have it equipped as the host."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an instance where clicking and dragging off an ability icon would cause the icon to be stuck on the screen."}},
        {tags={"Glaive",},text={"*Fixed an issue with Glaives/boomerangs staying scaled down/weapon trails off if you attack during the previous attack scaling down."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed UI unresponsiveness when attempting to use directional keys to navigate back to the main menu from the Squad or Invite panel."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed weapon and Warframe upgrade screens only displaying up-to 13 polarization stars."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed player’s UI breaking after attempting to join a match in progress in Conclaves."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed several issues with Ash’s Bladestorm losing functionality after activating the ability while holding a datamass, powercell, or other secondary items."}},
        {tags={"Ash",},text={"*Fixed Companions being completely invisible when under the effect of Ash%'s Smokescreen."}},
        {tags={"Liset",},text={"*Fixed issues where players can get stuck in certain areas of the Liset if slide jumping aggressively."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*Fixed active Foundry animations playing after crafting items are completed."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*Fixed an issue where claiming or crafting an item in Foundry results in the list being reset back to top, scrolling is now preserved."}},
        {tags={"Mirage","Quest","Rage",},text={"*Fixed an issue where players may not receive the first Inbox message for the Mirage Quest."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Aegis sword not showing weapon trails during certain events."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed issues with Player List showing enemy players in PVP even though health and shields aren’t updated."}},
        {tags={"Clan",},text={"*Fixed an issue with trying to set a Clan/Alliance MOTD as empty resulting in an error message."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*Fixed instance where a grey box would appear when selecting a Dragon Key to be crafted in the Foundry."}},
        {tags={"Foundry",},text={"*Fixed Warframe position overlapping Foundry screen when trying to view and craft items."}},
        {tags={"Oberon",},text={"*Fixed Oberon’s Hallowed Ground removing proc immunity when exiting one strip, but still on another (overlapping strips) as seen %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/318871-hallowed-ground-bug/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where previous Mission Complete UI from last completed mission will always appear when aborting other missions."}},
        {tags={"Kubrow",},text={"*Fixed an issue where pet Kubrows would remain on damage-dealing surfaces like electric water. Kubrows now jump off these surfaces!"}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with overlapping UI elements when selecting ‘Abort’ in spectator mode."}},
        {tags={"Conclave",},text={"*Fixed an issue where players joining a Conclave session just as others were leaving would result in a broken Player selection screen."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with a texture hole as seen %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/318931-texture-hole-on-asteroid-mining-base-tileset/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={"Excalibur",},text={"*Fixed issues with physics and effects of Excalibur’s Pendragon Helmet."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where players would have no indication of XP progress on failed or aborted missions."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where mods that improve reload time were showing as red numbers in UI where they should have been green - lower reload time is good!"}},
        {tags={"Void",},text={"*Fixed a number of Void water volumes that were off and misaligned as prompted by %[%https://forums.warframe.com/index.php?/topic/315860-void-water-out-of-place/ forum post%]"}},
        {tags={"Loki",},text={"*Fixed an issue with the Grineer Commander not always using his Switch Teleport ability."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue where there would sometimes be no ‘Exit’ button in the mission results screen."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with Excavators not appearing when a player enters the objective area."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Fixed an issue with only the most recent Excavator showing on the UI, preventing players from knowing the status of multiple Excavators."}},
        {tags={"Helios","Dex",},text={"*Fixed a number of problems with the Codex Scanner (including Helios’s user of the Scanner):"}},
        {tags={"Helios",},text={"**Fixed handling of certain compound objects like Death Orbs (you could not scan them and neither could Helios)."}},
        {tags={},text={"**Fixed over-scanning from within a mission (it used to only realize you were done an entry at the end of the mission)."}},
        {tags={"Helios",},text={"**Fixed Helios refusing to scan decorations while there were scannable objects out of sight."}},
        {tags={""},text={"<%br/>"}},
        {tags={},text={"*These fixes have also brought about the following optimizations:"}},
        {tags={"Helios",},text={"**Changed Helios to reduce time between scans (often over 3 seconds was wasted)."}},
        {tags={"Helios",},text={"**Simplified Helios%' scan logic to avoid a bunch of heavy-lifting that is already done by native code."}},
        {tags={},text={"*Further code optimizations."}},
},--fixes end

},--update end

}
return PatchData
```
